FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\analytics\recommendations.py
================================================================================

"""Recommendation generator synthesizing patterns, resource hogs, and inventory findings."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import json
import logging
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set
import pandas as pd

from desktop_intelligence.analytics.patterns import LaunchPattern, detect_launch_patterns
from desktop_intelligence.analytics.resources import ResourceHogFinding, analyze_resource_hogs
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import Clock, SystemClock
from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig
from desktop_intelligence.models import (
    InventoryItem,
    RecommendationRecord,
    format_utc_iso,
    parse_utc_iso,
    new_id,
)
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CsvStore

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def generate_recommendations(
    config: AppConfig,
    store: CsvStore,
    sessions_df: pd.DataFrame,
    samples_df: pd.DataFrame,
    inventory_items: Optional[List[InventoryItem]] = None,
    clock: Optional[Clock] = None,
) -> List[RecommendationRecord]:
    """Generate evidence-backed recommendations with duplicate prevention and cooldown."""
    clk = clock or SystemClock()
    now_ts = format_utc_iso(clk.now_utc())
    cooldown_cutoff = clk.now_utc() - timedelta(days=7)

    # Read existing recommendations to prevent duplicate pending recommendations and respect cooldown
    existing_rows = store.read_rows("recommendations", store.paths.recommendations_csv)
    existing_signatures: Set[str] = set()
    for r in existing_rows:
        status = r.get("status", "")
        sig = f"{r.get('category')}::{r.get('subject')}"
        if status in {"pending", "approved"}:
            existing_signatures.add(sig)
        elif status in {"declined", "dismissed"}:
            try:
                c_dt = parse_utc_iso(r.get("created_ts_utc", ""))
                if c_dt > cooldown_cutoff:
                    existing_signatures.add(sig)
            except Exception:
                existing_signatures.add(sig)

    new_recommendations: List[RecommendationRecord] = []

    # 1. Habit Launch Recommendations
    patterns = detect_launch_patterns(sessions_df, config.patterns)
    for p in patterns:
        subject = f"Launch {p.app_name} on {p.day_of_week} at {p.time_bucket}"
        sig = f"habit_launch::{subject}"
        if sig in existing_signatures:
            continue

        rec = RecommendationRecord(
            recommendation_id=new_id("rec"),
            created_ts_utc=now_ts,
            category="habit_launch",
            subject=subject,
            evidence_json=json.dumps(p.evidence, sort_keys=True),
            confidence=round(p.frequency, 2),
            status="pending",
        )
        new_recommendations.append(rec)
        existing_signatures.add(sig)

    # 2. Resource Hog Recommendations
    hogs = analyze_resource_hogs(samples_df, config.resources)
    for h in hogs:
        if not h.is_hog:
            continue

        subject = f"High memory consumption in {h.process_name}"
        sig = f"resource_hog::{subject}"
        if sig in existing_signatures:
            continue

        evidence = {
            "process_name": h.process_name,
            "max_memory_percent": h.max_memory_percent,
            "avg_memory_percent": h.avg_memory_percent,
            "max_rss_bytes": h.max_rss_bytes,
            "sustained_samples": h.sustained_samples,
            "reason": h.reason,
        }

        confidence = round(min(1.0, max(0.5, h.max_memory_percent / 100.0)), 2)
        rec = RecommendationRecord(
            recommendation_id=new_id("rec"),
            created_ts_utc=now_ts,
            category="resource_hog",
            subject=subject,
            evidence_json=json.dumps(evidence, sort_keys=True),
            confidence=confidence,
            status="pending",
        )
        new_recommendations.append(rec)
        existing_signatures.add(sig)

    # 3. Unused Application Recommendations
    # Only propose if observation span >= minimum_observation_days
    if inventory_items and not sessions_df.empty:
        dates = pd.to_datetime(sessions_df["local_date"], errors="coerce").dropna()
        if not dates.empty:
            total_days = (dates.max() - dates.min()).days + 1
            if total_days >= config.patterns.minimum_observation_days:
                unused_items = [it for it in inventory_items if it.observed_launch_count == 0]
                if len(unused_items) > 3:
                    subject = f"Review {len(unused_items)} unused applications"
                    sig = f"unused_application::{subject}"
                    if sig not in existing_signatures:
                        evidence = {
                            "unused_count": len(unused_items),
                            "sample_apps": [it.display_name for it in unused_items[:10]],
                            "observation_days": total_days,
                            "label": f"{len(unused_items)} installed applications have not been launched during monitoring.",
                        }
                        rec = RecommendationRecord(
                            recommendation_id=new_id("rec"),
                            created_ts_utc=now_ts,
                            category="unused_application",
                            subject=subject,
                            evidence_json=json.dumps(evidence, sort_keys=True),
                            confidence=0.75,
                            status="pending",
                        )
                        new_recommendations.append(rec)
                        existing_signatures.add(sig)
                elif len(unused_items) > 0:
                    for item in unused_items:
                        subject = f"Application not observed in use: {item.display_name}"
                        sig = f"unused_application::{subject}"
                        if sig in existing_signatures:
                            continue

                        evidence = {
                            "display_name": item.display_name,
                            "exe_path": item.exe_path,
                            "inventory_source": item.inventory_source,
                            "first_seen_ts_utc": item.first_seen_ts_utc,
                            "observation_days": total_days,
                            "label": "Not observed since monitoring began",
                        }

                        rec = RecommendationRecord(
                            recommendation_id=new_id("rec"),
                            created_ts_utc=now_ts,
                            category="unused_application",
                            subject=subject,
                            evidence_json=json.dumps(evidence, sort_keys=True),
                            confidence=0.75,
                            status="pending",
                        )
                        new_recommendations.append(rec)
                        existing_signatures.add(sig)

    # Persist new recommendations to CSV
    for rec in new_recommendations:
        store.append_recommendation(rec)

    return new_recommendations



================================================================================
FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\analytics\resources.py
================================================================================

"""Resource consumption analysis and outlier/hog detection."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from desktop_intelligence.config import ResourcesConfig


@dataclass
class ResourceHogFinding:
    process_name: str
    sustained_samples: int
    max_memory_percent: float
    avg_memory_percent: float
    max_rss_bytes: int
    is_hog: bool
    reason: str


def analyze_resource_hogs(
    samples_df: pd.DataFrame,
    config: Optional[ResourcesConfig] = None,
) -> List[ResourceHogFinding]:
    """Identify applications with sustained high memory or system pressure."""
    if samples_df is None or samples_df.empty:
        return []

    cfg = config or ResourcesConfig()
    threshold = cfg.high_memory_percent
    min_count = cfg.sustained_observation_count

    findings: List[ResourceHogFinding] = []

    for app_name, group in samples_df.groupby("process_name"):
        mem_series = group["process_memory_percent"].dropna()
        if mem_series.empty:
            continue

        mem_arr = mem_series.to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
        over_threshold_mask = mem_arr >= threshold
        over_count = int(np.sum(over_threshold_mask))

        max_mem = float(np.max(mem_arr))
        avg_mem = float(np.mean(mem_arr))

        rss_series = group["rss_bytes"].dropna()
        max_rss = int(rss_series.max()) if not rss_series.empty else 0

        is_hog = over_count >= min_count
        if is_hog:
            reason = (
                f"Process exceeded memory threshold of {threshold:.1f}% across {over_count} "
                f"distinct observations (max: {max_mem:.1f}%)."
            )
        else:
            reason = f"Normal consumption within threshold (max: {max_mem:.1f}%)."

        findings.append(
            ResourceHogFinding(
                process_name=app_name,
                sustained_samples=over_count,
                max_memory_percent=round(max_mem, 1),
                avg_memory_percent=round(avg_mem, 1),
                max_rss_bytes=max_rss,
                is_hog=is_hog,
                reason=reason,
            )
        )

    # Sort so hogs appear first, then by max memory percent descending
    findings.sort(key=lambda x: (not x.is_hog, -x.max_memory_percent))
    return findings



================================================================================
FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\analytics\usage.py
================================================================================

"""Usage statistics, grouping, aggregations, and NumPy baseline calculations."""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

DAYS_OF_WEEK_ORDER = ["Monday", "Tuesday", "Wednesday", "Thursday", "Friday", "Saturday", "Sunday"]


def calculate_usage_summary(sessions_df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Aggregate total usage per application across all observed sessions."""
    if sessions_df is None or sessions_df.empty:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=[
            "process_name", "total_hours", "active_hours", "idle_hours",
            "session_count", "avg_session_min", "median_session_min", "pct_active_time"
        ])

    grouped = sessions_df.groupby("process_name")

    agg_df = grouped.agg(
        total_seconds=("duration_seconds", "sum"),
        active_seconds=("active_seconds", "sum"),
        idle_seconds=("idle_seconds", "sum"),
        session_count=("session_id", "count"),
        avg_session_sec=("duration_seconds", "mean"),
        median_session_sec=("duration_seconds", "median"),
    ).reset_index()

    total_active_all = agg_df["active_seconds"].sum()

    # Vectorized conversion using NumPy
    agg_df["total_hours"] = np.round(agg_df["total_seconds"] / 3600.0, 2)
    agg_df["active_hours"] = np.round(agg_df["active_seconds"] / 3600.0, 2)
    agg_df["idle_hours"] = np.round(agg_df["idle_seconds"] / 3600.0, 2)
    agg_df["avg_session_min"] = np.round(agg_df["avg_session_sec"] / 60.0, 1)
    agg_df["median_session_min"] = np.round(agg_df["median_session_sec"] / 60.0, 1)

    if total_active_all > 0:
        agg_df["pct_active_time"] = np.round((agg_df["active_seconds"] / total_active_all) * 100.0, 1)
    else:
        agg_df["pct_active_time"] = 0.0

    cols = [
        "process_name", "total_hours", "active_hours", "idle_hours",
        "session_count", "avg_session_min", "median_session_min", "pct_active_time"
    ]
    return agg_df[cols].sort_values(by="active_hours", ascending=False).reset_index(drop=True)


def calculate_daily_summary(sessions_df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Calculate daily total active and idle hours."""
    if sessions_df is None or sessions_df.empty:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=["local_date", "active_hours", "idle_hours", "total_hours", "session_count"])

    grouped = sessions_df.groupby("local_date")
    daily = grouped.agg(
        active_sec=("active_seconds", "sum"),
        idle_sec=("idle_seconds", "sum"),
        total_sec=("duration_seconds", "sum"),
        session_count=("session_id", "count"),
    ).reset_index()

    daily["active_hours"] = np.round(daily["active_sec"] / 3600.0, 2)
    daily["idle_hours"] = np.round(daily["idle_sec"] / 3600.0, 2)
    daily["total_hours"] = np.round(daily["total_sec"] / 3600.0, 2)

    return daily[["local_date", "active_hours", "idle_hours", "total_hours", "session_count"]].sort_values("local_date").reset_index(drop=True)


def calculate_day_of_week_distribution(sessions_df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Aggregate active hours per day of the week in standard order."""
    if sessions_df is None or sessions_df.empty:
        return pd.DataFrame({"day_of_week": DAYS_OF_WEEK_ORDER, "active_hours": [0.0] * 7})

    dow_grouped = sessions_df.groupby("day_of_week")["active_seconds"].sum()
    hours_map = (dow_grouped / 3600.0).to_dict()

    data = [{"day_of_week": d, "active_hours": round(hours_map.get(d, 0.0), 2)} for d in DAYS_OF_WEEK_ORDER]
    return pd.DataFrame(data)


def calculate_hourly_heatmap_matrix(sessions_df: pd.DataFrame) -> np.ndarray:
    """Create a 7x24 NumPy matrix of total active minutes (7 days of week x 24 hours of day).

    Accurately distributes multi-hour session active durations across their respective hourly buckets.
    """
    matrix = np.zeros((7, 24), dtype=np.float64)
    if sessions_df is None or sessions_df.empty:
        return matrix

    dow_index = {d: i for i, d in enumerate(DAYS_OF_WEEK_ORDER)}

    start_col = "start_dt" if "start_dt" in sessions_df.columns else ("start_ts_utc" if "start_ts_utc" in sessions_df.columns else None)
    end_col = "end_dt" if "end_dt" in sessions_df.columns else ("end_ts_utc" if "end_ts_utc" in sessions_df.columns else None)

    for _, row in sessions_df.iterrows():
        dow = str(row.get("day_of_week", "")).strip()
        active_sec = float(row.get("active_seconds", 0.0))
        if active_sec <= 0:
            continue

        if dow not in dow_index:
            continue

        r_idx = dow_index[dow]

        # Slicing across hours if start/end timestamps are valid
        sliced = False
        if start_col and end_col and pd.notna(row.get(start_col)) and pd.notna(row.get(end_col)):
            try:
                s_val = row[start_col]
                e_val = row[end_col]
                s_dt = s_val if isinstance(s_val, pd.Timestamp) else pd.to_datetime(s_val)
                e_dt = e_val if isinstance(e_val, pd.Timestamp) else pd.to_datetime(e_val)

                total_span = (e_dt - s_dt).total_seconds()
                if total_span > 0:
                    curr = s_dt
                    while curr < e_dt:
                        next_hr = curr.floor("h") + pd.Timedelta(hours=1)
                        hr_end = min(e_dt, next_hr)
                        hr_span = (hr_end - curr).total_seconds()
                        hr_active = active_sec * (hr_span / total_span)
                        c_idx = curr.hour
                        if 0 <= c_idx < 24:
                            matrix[r_idx, c_idx] += hr_active / 60.0
                        curr = hr_end
                    sliced = True
            except Exception:
                sliced = False

        if not sliced:
            time_b = str(row.get("time_bucket", "")).strip()
            hour_str = time_b.split(":")[0] if ":" in time_b else "0"
            try:
                c_idx = int(hour_str)
                if 0 <= c_idx < 24:
                    matrix[r_idx, c_idx] += active_sec / 60.0
            except ValueError:
                pass

    return np.round(matrix, 1)


def calculate_app_baselines(sessions_df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Calculate personal statistical baselines using robust NumPy statistics.

    Uses NumPy median, mean, standard deviation, and 75th/95th percentiles.
    """
    if sessions_df is None or sessions_df.empty:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=[
            "process_name", "mean_daily_min", "median_daily_min", "std_daily_min",
            "p75_daily_min", "p95_daily_min", "days_observed"
        ])

    # First aggregate active duration per app per day
    daily_app = sessions_df.groupby(["process_name", "local_date"])["active_seconds"].sum().reset_index()
    daily_app["daily_minutes"] = daily_app["active_seconds"] / 60.0

    records = []
    for app_name, group in daily_app.groupby("process_name"):
        arr = group["daily_minutes"].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
        if len(arr) == 0:
            continue

        mean_val = float(np.mean(arr))
        median_val = float(np.median(arr))
        std_val = float(np.std(arr)) if len(arr) > 1 else 0.0
        p75_val = float(np.percentile(arr, 75))
        p95_val = float(np.percentile(arr, 95))

        records.append({
            "process_name": app_name,
            "mean_daily_min": round(mean_val, 1),
            "median_daily_min": round(median_val, 1),
            "std_daily_min": round(std_val, 1),
            "p75_daily_min": round(p75_val, 1),
            "p95_daily_min": round(p95_val, 1),
            "days_observed": int(len(arr)),
        })

    result_df = pd.DataFrame(records)
    if not result_df.empty:
        result_df = result_df.sort_values(by="median_daily_min", ascending=False).reset_index(drop=True)
    return result_df

FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\automation\launcher.py
================================================================================

"""Safe application launcher with path validation and duplicate instance prevention."""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
import logging
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
from typing import Callable, List, Optional, Set, Tuple
import psutil

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class BaseLauncher(ABC):
    """Abstract launcher interface."""

    @abstractmethod
    def is_app_running(self, process_name: str, exe_path: str = "") -> bool:
        """Check if an instance of the application is already active."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def launch(self, exe_path: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """Launch an application safely. Returns (success, message)."""
        pass


class SafeProcessLauncher(BaseLauncher):
    """Safe Windows process launcher avoiding shell execution and verifying allowlists."""

    def __init__(self, allowlist_provider: Optional[Callable[[], Set[str]]] = None):
        self.allowlist_provider = allowlist_provider

    def is_app_running(self, process_name: str, exe_path: str = "") -> bool:
        norm_name = process_name.lower().strip()
        norm_exe = os.path.normpath(exe_path).lower().strip() if exe_path else ""

        try:
            for p in psutil.process_iter(["name", "exe"]):
                try:
                    p_name = (p.info["name"] or "").lower()
                    if norm_name and p_name == norm_name:
                        return True
                    if norm_exe and p.info.get("exe"):
                        if os.path.normpath(p.info["exe"]).lower() == norm_exe:
                            return True
                except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
                    continue
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("Error checking running processes: %s", exc)

        return False

    def launch(self, exe_path: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        if not exe_path or not isinstance(exe_path, str):
            return False, "Executable path is empty"

        target = Path(exe_path).resolve()
        if not target.exists():
            return False, f"Executable not found on disk: {target}"
        if not target.is_file():
            return False, f"Target is not a file: {target}"

        # Allowlist check if configured
        if self.allowlist_provider:
            allowlist = self.allowlist_provider()
            target_str = str(target).lower()
            target_name = target.name.lower()
            if allowlist and (target_str not in allowlist and target_name not in allowlist):
                return False, f"Executable '{target.name}' is not in the approved application allowlist"

        # Safe launch without shell string interpolation
        try:
            subprocess.Popen(
                [str(target)],
                shell=False,
                close_fds=(os.name != "nt"),
            )
            return True, f"Launched successfully: {target.name}"
        except Exception as exc:
            return False, f"Failed to launch process: {exc}"


class FakeLauncher(BaseLauncher):
    """Deterministic fake launcher for unit tests."""

    def __init__(self, running_apps: Optional[List[str]] = None):
        self.running_apps: List[str] = [a.lower() for a in (running_apps or [])]
        self.launched_history: List[str] = []
        self.fail_all: bool = False
        self.failure_reason: str = "Simulated launch failure"

    def is_app_running(self, process_name: str, exe_path: str = "") -> bool:
        p_name = process_name.lower()
        if p_name in self.running_apps:
            return True
        if exe_path and Path(exe_path).name.lower() in self.running_apps:
            return True
        return False

    def launch(self, exe_path: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        if self.fail_all:
            return False, self.failure_reason

        app_name = Path(exe_path).name if exe_path else "unknown.exe"
        self.launched_history.append(str(exe_path))
        self.running_apps.append(app_name.lower())
        return True, f"Fake launched: {app_name}"



================================================================================
FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\automation\rules.py
================================================================================

"""Automation rule definitions and synchronization from approved recommendations."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
import json
import logging
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

from desktop_intelligence.models import InventoryItem, RecommendationRecord

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass
class AutomationRule:
    rule_id: str
    app_name: str
    exe_path: str
    day_of_week: str
    time_bucket: str
    frequency_percent: float
    approved: bool
    enabled: bool = True
    last_triggered_date: str = ""


def sync_rules_from_recommendations(
    recommendations: List[RecommendationRecord],
    inventory_items: Optional[List[InventoryItem]] = None,
    exe_path_map: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
) -> List[AutomationRule]:
    """Convert approved habit_launch recommendations into actionable automation rules."""
    path_lookup: Dict[str, str] = {}

    if inventory_items:
        for item in inventory_items:
            if item.exe_path:
                path_lookup[item.display_name.lower()] = item.exe_path
                exe_name = Path(item.exe_path).name.lower()
                path_lookup[exe_name] = item.exe_path

    if exe_path_map:
        for k, v in exe_path_map.items():
            path_lookup[k.lower()] = v

    rules: List[AutomationRule] = []

    for rec in recommendations:
        if rec.category != "habit_launch":
            continue

        try:
            ev = json.loads(rec.evidence_json)
        except Exception:
            continue

        app_name = ev.get("app_name", "").strip()
        day_of_week = ev.get("day_of_week", "").strip()
        time_bucket = ev.get("time_bucket", "").strip()
        freq_pct = float(ev.get("frequency_percent", rec.confidence * 100.0))

        # Resolve executable path
        resolved_exe = path_lookup.get(app_name.lower(), "")
        if not resolved_exe:
            # Check if app_name itself is an executable path
            if os.path.isabs(app_name) and os.path.exists(app_name):
                resolved_exe = app_name

        rule = AutomationRule(
            rule_id=rec.recommendation_id,
            app_name=app_name,
            exe_path=resolved_exe,
            day_of_week=day_of_week,
            time_bucket=time_bucket,
            frequency_percent=freq_pct,
            approved=(rec.status == "approved"),
            enabled=True,
        )
        rules.append(rule)

    return rules



================================================================================
FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\automation\scheduler.py
================================================================================

"""Automation scheduler managing rule triggering, consent behavior, and audit logging."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import logging
from typing import Callable, Dict, List, Optional
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from desktop_intelligence.automation.launcher import BaseLauncher, SafeProcessLauncher
from desktop_intelligence.automation.rules import AutomationRule
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import Clock, SystemClock
from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig
from desktop_intelligence.models import AutomationLogRecord, format_utc_iso, new_id
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CsvStore

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

ConsentCallback = Callable[[AutomationRule, int], str]  # returns "approve", "reject", or "timeout"


class AutomationScheduler:
    """Evaluates approved automation rules and executes scheduled launches."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: AppConfig,
        store: CsvStore,
        launcher: Optional[BaseLauncher] = None,
        clock: Optional[Clock] = None,
        consent_provider: Optional[ConsentCallback] = None,
    ):
        self.config = config
        self.store = store
        self.launcher = launcher or SafeProcessLauncher()
        self.clock = clock or SystemClock()
        self.consent_provider = consent_provider
        self.rules: List[AutomationRule] = []

    def set_rules(self, rules: List[AutomationRule]) -> None:
        self.rules = rules
        self._hydrate_last_triggered_dates()

    def _hydrate_last_triggered_dates(self) -> None:
        """Populate last_triggered_date for each rule from historical automation logs."""
        try:
            logs = self.store.read_rows("automation_log", self.store.paths.automation_log_csv)
            if not logs:
                return

            tz_str = self.config.general.timezone
            if tz_str.lower() == "utc":
                local_tz = timezone.utc
            elif tz_str.lower() == "local":
                local_tz = datetime.now().astimezone().tzinfo
            else:
                try:
                    local_tz = ZoneInfo(tz_str)
                except Exception:
                    local_tz = datetime.now().astimezone().tzinfo

            latest_dates: Dict[str, str] = {}
            for r in logs:
                rule_id = r.get("rule_id", "")
                ts_str = r.get("ts_utc", "")
                result = r.get("result", "")
                if rule_id and ts_str and result in {"success", "skipped_duplicate"}:
                    try:
                        from desktop_intelligence.models import parse_utc_iso
                        dt = parse_utc_iso(ts_str).astimezone(local_tz)
                        date_str = dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
                        if rule_id not in latest_dates or date_str > latest_dates[rule_id]:
                            latest_dates[rule_id] = date_str
                    except Exception:
                        pass

            for rule in self.rules:
                if rule.rule_id in latest_dates and not rule.last_triggered_date:
                    rule.last_triggered_date = latest_dates[rule.rule_id]
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("Failed hydrating last_triggered_dates: %s", exc)

    def evaluate_and_trigger(self) -> List[AutomationLogRecord]:
        """Evaluate all rules against current time and trigger eligible actions."""
        executed_logs: List[AutomationLogRecord] = []

        now_utc_dt = self.clock.now_utc()
        now_ts_str = format_utc_iso(now_utc_dt)

        # Determine current local date, day of week, and time bucket
        tz_str = self.config.general.timezone
        if tz_str.lower() == "utc":
            local_tz = timezone.utc
        elif tz_str.lower() == "local":
            local_tz = datetime.now().astimezone().tzinfo
        else:
            try:
                local_tz = ZoneInfo(tz_str)
            except Exception:
                local_tz = datetime.now().astimezone().tzinfo

        local_dt = now_utc_dt.astimezone(local_tz)
        local_date_str = local_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        current_dow = local_dt.strftime("%A")
        bucket_mins = self.config.patterns.time_bucket_minutes
        b_minute = (local_dt.minute // bucket_mins) * bucket_mins
        current_time_bucket = f"{local_dt.hour:02d}:{b_minute:02d}"

        for rule in self.rules:
            # 1. Master automation enabled check
            if not self.config.automation.enabled:
                continue

            # 2. Rule approval and enabled check
            if not rule.approved or not rule.enabled:
                continue

            # 3. Schedule match
            if rule.day_of_week != current_dow or rule.time_bucket != current_time_bucket:
                continue

            # 4. Once-per-day trigger check (prevent rapid duplicate triggers in same bucket)
            if rule.last_triggered_date == local_date_str:
                continue

            # 5. Check if executable path is resolved
            if not rule.exe_path:
                log = AutomationLogRecord(
                    log_id=new_id("log"),
                    ts_utc=now_ts_str,
                    rule_id=rule.rule_id,
                    action="launch",
                    result="error",
                    reason=f"Executable path unresolved for {rule.app_name}",
                )
                self.store.append_automation_log(log)
                executed_logs.append(log)
                continue

            # 6. Duplicate running instance check
            if self.config.automation.prevent_duplicate_instances:
                if self.launcher.is_app_running(rule.app_name, rule.exe_path):
                    log = AutomationLogRecord(
                        log_id=new_id("log"),
                        ts_utc=now_ts_str,
                        rule_id=rule.rule_id,
                        action="launch",
                        result="skipped_duplicate",
                        reason=f"Application {rule.app_name} is already running",
                    )
                    self.store.append_automation_log(log)
                    executed_logs.append(log)
                    rule.last_triggered_date = local_date_str
                    continue

            # 7. Consent check if prompt is configured
            if self.consent_provider:
                timeout_sec = self.config.automation.prompt_timeout_seconds
                response = self.consent_provider(rule, timeout_sec)
                if response == "reject":
                    log = AutomationLogRecord(
                        log_id=new_id("log"),
                        ts_utc=now_ts_str,
                        rule_id=rule.rule_id,
                        action="prompt",
                        result="rejected",
                        reason="User rejected launch prompt",
                    )
                    self.store.append_automation_log(log)
                    executed_logs.append(log)
                    rule.last_triggered_date = local_date_str
                    continue
                elif response == "timeout":
                    no_resp = self.config.automation.no_response_action.lower()
                    if no_resp == "skip":
                        log = AutomationLogRecord(
                            log_id=new_id("log"),
                            ts_utc=now_ts_str,
                            rule_id=rule.rule_id,
                            action="prompt",
                            result="timeout_skip",
                            reason=f"No response within {timeout_sec}s timeout; skipping launch",
                        )
                        self.store.append_automation_log(log)
                        executed_logs.append(log)
                        rule.last_triggered_date = local_date_str
                        continue

            # 8. Execute launch
            success, msg = self.launcher.launch(rule.exe_path)
            res_str = "success" if success else "error"
            log = AutomationLogRecord(
                log_id=new_id("log"),
                ts_utc=now_ts_str,
                rule_id=rule.rule_id,
                action="launch",
                result=res_str,
                reason=msg,
            )
            self.store.append_automation_log(log)
            executed_logs.append(log)
            rule.last_triggered_date = local_date_str

        return executed_logs

"""Automation package for Desktop Intelligence."""

from desktop_intelligence.automation.rules import AutomationRule, sync_rules_from_recommendations
from desktop_intelligence.automation.launcher import BaseLauncher, SafeProcessLauncher, FakeLauncher
from desktop_intelligence.automation.scheduler import AutomationScheduler

__all__ = [

    "sync_rules_from_recommendations",

================================================================================
FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\collectors\activity_manager.py

"""Activity manager coordinating event intake, queue processing, and session state."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import json
import logging
import os
from pathlib import Path
import queue
import threading
import time
from typing import List, Optional
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig
from desktop_intelligence.models import (
    ActivityEvent,
    ActivitySession,
    format_utc_iso,
    new_id,
    parse_utc_iso,
)
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import BaseEventSource, Clock, SystemClock, RawEvent
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CsvStore

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Known system/shell processes that should close user sessions rather than accumulate usage
SHELL_PROCESSES = {
    "lockapp.exe",
    "logonui.exe",
    "shellexperiencehost.exe",
    "startmenuexperiencehost.exe",
    "searchapp.exe",
    "unknown",
}


@dataclass
class ActiveSessionState:
    process_name: str
    pid: int
    start_utc: datetime
    last_active_utc: datetime
    accumulated_idle_seconds: float = 0.0
    is_idle: bool = False


class ActivityManager:
    """Manages application event ingestion and foreground session tracking."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: AppConfig,
        store: CsvStore,
        clock: Optional[Clock] = None,
        event_sources: Optional[List[BaseEventSource]] = None,
    ):
        self.config = config
        self.store = store
        self.clock = clock or SystemClock()
        self.event_sources: List[BaseEventSource] = event_sources or []

        self.event_queue: queue.Queue[Optional[RawEvent]] = queue.Queue(maxsize=10000)
        self._running = False
        self._worker_thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None
        self._heartbeat_thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None

        self.active_session: Optional[ActiveSessionState] = None
        self._state_lock = threading.RLock()

    def _get_open_session_path(self) -> Path:
        return self.store.paths.data_dir / "open_session.json"

    def _save_open_session_state(self, session: ActiveSessionState) -> None:
        try:
            target = self._get_open_session_path()
            data = {
                "process_name": session.process_name,
                "pid": session.pid,
                "start_utc": format_utc_iso(session.start_utc),
                "last_active_utc": format_utc_iso(session.last_active_utc),
                "accumulated_idle_seconds": round(float(session.accumulated_idle_seconds), 3),
                "is_idle": session.is_idle,
            }
            tmp = target.with_suffix(".tmp")
            tmp.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
            os.replace(tmp, target)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("Could not persist open session state: %s", exc)

    def _clear_open_session_state(self) -> None:
        try:
            target = self._get_open_session_path()
            if target.exists():
                target.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def recover_orphaned_sessions(self) -> None:
        """Recover any unclosed session from an abnormal previous shutdown."""
        open_session_file = self._get_open_session_path()
        if not open_session_file.exists():
            return

        try:
            content = open_session_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
            if not content:
                open_session_file.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                return

            data = json.loads(content)
            p_name = (data.get("process_name") or "").strip()
            pid = int(data.get("pid", 0))
            start_utc_str = data.get("start_utc")
            last_active_str = data.get("last_active_utc")
            idle_sec = float(data.get("accumulated_idle_seconds", 0.0))

            if p_name and start_utc_str and last_active_str:
                start_utc = parse_utc_iso(start_utc_str)
                last_active_utc = parse_utc_iso(last_active_str)
                duration = (last_active_utc - start_utc).total_seconds()
                if duration > 0.05:
                    self._record_session_slices(
                        process_name=p_name,
                        pid=pid,
                        start_utc=start_utc,
                        end_utc=last_active_utc,
                        total_idle_seconds=min(idle_sec, duration),
                    )
                    logger.info(
                        "Recovered orphaned session for '%s' (duration %.1fs)",
                        p_name, duration,
                    )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Failed to recover orphaned session from %s: %s", open_session_file, exc)
        finally:
            try:
                open_session_file.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            except Exception:
                pass

    def start(self) -> None:
        """Start event sources, worker queue, and idle heartbeat."""
        if self._running:
            return
        self._running = True

        self.recover_orphaned_sessions()

        self._worker_thread = threading.Thread(
            target=self._process_event_queue, daemon=True, name="ActivityQueueWorker"
        )
        self._worker_thread.start()

        self._heartbeat_thread = threading.Thread(
            target=self._heartbeat_loop, daemon=True, name="ActivityHeartbeatWorker"
        )
        self._heartbeat_thread.start()

        for source in self.event_sources:
            try:
                source.start()
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.error("Failed to start event source %s: %s", source, exc)

    def stop(self) -> None:
        """Stop event sources and cleanly close open sessions."""
        if not self._running:
            return
        self._running = False

        for source in self.event_sources:
            try:
                source.stop()
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.error("Error stopping source %s: %s", source, exc)

        # Signal queue worker to finish
        self.event_queue.put(None)
        if self._worker_thread and self._worker_thread.is_alive():
            self._worker_thread.join(timeout=3.0)

        # Close any active session
        with self._state_lock:
            if self.active_session:
                self._close_session(self.clock.now_utc())

    def on_event(self, event: RawEvent) -> None:
        """Thread-safe callback for event sources."""
        if not self._running:
            return
        try:
            self.event_queue.put_nowait(event)
        except queue.Full:
            logger.warning("Event queue full; dropping event: %s", event)

    def _process_event_queue(self) -> None:
        while True:
            try:
                item = self.event_queue.get(timeout=0.5)
            except queue.Empty:
                if not self._running:
                    break
                continue

            if item is None:
                # Sentinel shutdown
                break

            try:
                self._handle_raw_event(item)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.exception("Error handling event: %s", exc)
            finally:
                self.event_queue.task_done()

    def _handle_raw_event(self, event: RawEvent) -> None:
        with self._state_lock:
            p_name = (event.process_name or "").strip()
            norm_name = p_name.lower()
            # 1. Record raw event in activity_events.csv
            evt_model = ActivityEvent(
                event_id=new_id("evt"),
                event_ts_utc=format_utc_iso(event.timestamp_utc),
                event_type=event.event_type,
                pid=event.pid,
                process_name=p_name,
                exe_path=event.exe_path,
                event_source=event.event_source,
            )
            self.store.append_activity_event(evt_model)

            # 2. Trigger resource snapshots if configured
            if event.event_type == "process_start" and self.config.collection.resource_snapshot_on_process_start:
                from desktop_intelligence.collectors.resource_snapshot import capture_resource_sample
                sample = capture_resource_sample(event.pid, p_name, "process_start", self.clock)
                if sample:
                    self.store.append_resource_sample(sample)

            elif event.event_type == "foreground_change" and self.config.collection.resource_snapshot_on_foreground_change:
                from desktop_intelligence.collectors.resource_snapshot import capture_resource_sample
                sample = capture_resource_sample(event.pid, p_name, "foreground_change", self.clock)
                if sample:
                    self.store.append_resource_sample(sample)

            # 3. Foreground transitions manage sessions
            if event.event_type == "foreground_change":
                self._handle_foreground_transition(event)

    def _handle_foreground_transition(self, event: RawEvent) -> None:
        now_ts = event.timestamp_utc
        p_name = (event.process_name or "").strip()
        norm_name = p_name.lower()

        # Idempotence: if same process and pid as current active session, just update last active
        if self.active_session:
            if (
                self.active_session.pid == event.pid
                and self.active_session.process_name.lower() == norm_name
            ):
                self.active_session.last_active_utc = now_ts
                self.active_session.is_idle = False
                self._save_open_session_state(self.active_session)
                return

            # Switching to a different application: close the old session
            self._close_session(now_ts)

        # If the newly focused window belongs to desktop/shell or is unknown, do not start session
        if norm_name in SHELL_PROCESSES or event.pid <= 0 or not p_name:
            self.active_session = None
            return

        # Start new foreground session
        self.active_session = ActiveSessionState(
            process_name=p_name,
            pid=event.pid,
            start_utc=now_ts,
            last_active_utc=now_ts,
            accumulated_idle_seconds=0.0,
            is_idle=False,
        )
        self._save_open_session_state(self.active_session)

    def _close_session(self, end_utc: datetime) -> None:
        if not self.active_session:
            return

        session = self.active_session
        self.active_session = None
        self._clear_open_session_state()

        if end_utc < session.start_utc:
            end_utc = session.start_utc

        total_duration = (end_utc - session.start_utc).total_seconds()
        if total_duration < 0:
            total_duration = 0.0

        # Don't persist micro-sessions under 0.05 seconds if duration is essentially zero
        # but keep normal sessions
        if total_duration <= 0.001:
            return

        idle_sec = min(session.accumulated_idle_seconds, total_duration)

        # Handle midnight crossing: split into calendar days if needed
        self._record_session_slices(
            process_name=session.process_name,
            pid=session.pid,
            start_utc=session.start_utc,
            end_utc=end_utc,
            total_idle_seconds=idle_sec,
        )

    def _record_session_slices(
        self,
        process_name: str,
        pid: int,
        start_utc: datetime,
        end_utc: datetime,
        total_idle_seconds: float,
    ) -> None:
        """Slice session across midnight boundaries in local timezone."""
        tz_str = self.config.general.timezone
        if tz_str.lower() == "utc":
            local_tz = timezone.utc
        elif tz_str.lower() == "local":
            local_tz = datetime.now().astimezone().tzinfo
        else:
            try:
                local_tz = ZoneInfo(tz_str)
            except Exception:
                local_tz = datetime.now().astimezone().tzinfo

        start_local = start_utc.astimezone(local_tz)
        end_local = end_utc.astimezone(local_tz)

        bucket_mins = self.config.patterns.time_bucket_minutes

        current_slice_start = start_local
        total_span = (end_local - start_local).total_seconds()

        while current_slice_start.date() < end_local.date():
            # Slice up to midnight of current day
            next_midnight = datetime(
                current_slice_start.year,
                current_slice_start.month,
                current_slice_start.day,
                tzinfo=local_tz,
            ) + timedelta(days=1)

            slice_duration = (next_midnight - current_slice_start).total_seconds()
            slice_ratio = (slice_duration / total_span) if total_span > 0 else 0.0
            slice_idle = total_idle_seconds * slice_ratio

            self._write_session_row(
                process_name=process_name,
                pid=pid,
                start_utc=current_slice_start.astimezone(timezone.utc),
                end_utc=next_midnight.astimezone(timezone.utc),
                duration=slice_duration,
                idle_sec=slice_idle,
                slice_local=current_slice_start,
                bucket_mins=bucket_mins,
            )
            current_slice_start = next_midnight

        # Final (or only) slice
        final_duration = (end_local - current_slice_start).total_seconds()
        if final_duration > 0.001:
            slice_ratio = (final_duration / total_span) if total_span > 0 else 1.0
            slice_idle = total_idle_seconds * slice_ratio
            self._write_session_row(
                process_name=process_name,
                pid=pid,
                start_utc=current_slice_start.astimezone(timezone.utc),
                end_utc=end_local.astimezone(timezone.utc),
                duration=final_duration,
                idle_sec=slice_idle,
                slice_local=current_slice_start,
                bucket_mins=bucket_mins,
            )

    def _write_session_row(
        self,
        process_name: str,
        pid: int,
        start_utc: datetime,
        end_utc: datetime,
        duration: float,
        idle_sec: float,
        slice_local: datetime,
        bucket_mins: int,
    ) -> None:
        local_date = slice_local.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        day_of_week = slice_local.strftime("%A")
        bucket_m = (slice_local.minute // bucket_mins) * bucket_mins
        time_bucket = f"{slice_local.hour:02d}:{bucket_m:02d}"

        ses_model = ActivitySession(
            session_id=new_id("ses"),
            process_name=process_name,
            pid=pid,
            start_ts_utc=format_utc_iso(start_utc),
            end_ts_utc=format_utc_iso(end_utc),
            duration_seconds=round(duration, 3),
            idle_seconds=round(idle_sec, 3),
            local_date=local_date,
            day_of_week=day_of_week,
            time_bucket=time_bucket,
        )
        self.store.append_activity_session(ses_model)

    def _heartbeat_loop(self) -> None:
        """Low-frequency check for idle status."""
        interval = max(1, self.config.collection.idle_check_interval_seconds)
        timeout = self.config.collection.idle_timeout_seconds

        while self._running:
            time.sleep(interval)
            if not self._running:
                break

            idle_duration = self.clock.get_idle_seconds()
            with self._state_lock:
                if self.active_session:
                    if idle_duration >= timeout:
                        if not self.active_session.is_idle:
                            self.active_session.is_idle = True
                        self.active_session.accumulated_idle_seconds += float(interval)
                    else:
                        if self.active_session.is_idle:
                            self.active_session.is_idle = False
                        self.active_session.last_active_utc = self.clock.now_utc()
                    self._save_open_session_state(self.active_session)



================================================================================
FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\collectors\application_inventory.py
================================================================================

"""Application inventory discovery from Windows Registry and Start Menu shortcuts."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import logging
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Set

from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import Clock, SystemClock
from desktop_intelligence.models import InventoryItem, format_utc_iso

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def normalize_app_key(display_name: str, exe_path: str = "") -> str:
    """Generate a stable deduplication key for an application entry."""
    if exe_path:
        norm_path = os.path.normpath(exe_path).lower().strip()
        if norm_path:
            return norm_path
    return display_name.lower().strip()


def resolve_shortcut_target(lnk_path: str) -> str:
    """Resolve a .lnk Windows shortcut to its target executable path."""
    if sys.platform != "win32":
        return str(lnk_path)
    try:
        import pythoncom
        import win32com.client
        pythoncom.CoInitialize()
        try:
            shell = win32com.client.Dispatch("WScript.Shell")
            shortcut = shell.CreateShortcut(str(lnk_path))
            target = getattr(shortcut, "TargetPath", "")
            if target and target.strip():
                return target.strip()
        finally:
            pythoncom.CoUninitialize()
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.debug("Failed to resolve shortcut %s: %s", lnk_path, exc)
    return str(lnk_path)


class ApplicationInventoryScanner:
    """Discovers installed software and correlates with recorded observation history."""

    def __init__(self, clock: Optional[Clock] = None):
        self.clock = clock or SystemClock()

    def scan_windows_registry(self) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
        """Query Windows uninstall registry hives for installed applications."""
        if sys.platform != "win32":
            return []

        import winreg

        results: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
        hives = [
            (winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall"),
            (winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r"Software\Wow6432Node\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall"),
            (winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall"),
        ]

        for hive, subkey in hives:
            try:
                with winreg.OpenKey(hive, subkey) as key:
                    count, _, _ = winreg.QueryInfoKey(key)
                    for i in range(count):
                        try:
                            sub_name = winreg.EnumKey(key, i)
                            with winreg.OpenKey(key, sub_name) as app_key:
                                try:
                                    name, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(app_key, "DisplayName")
                                    name = str(name).strip()
                                    if not name:
                                        continue
                                except (FileNotFoundError, OSError):
                                    continue

                                # Filter out system components and updates if flag is explicitly set
                                try:
                                    system_component, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(app_key, "SystemComponent")
                                    if system_component == 1:
                                        continue
                                except (FileNotFoundError, OSError):
                                    pass

                                try:
                                    loc, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(app_key, "InstallLocation")
                                except (FileNotFoundError, OSError):
                                    loc = ""

                                try:
                                    icon, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(app_key, "DisplayIcon")
                                    exe_path = icon.split(",")[0].strip('"') if icon else ""
                                except (FileNotFoundError, OSError):
                                    exe_path = ""

                                results.append({
                                    "display_name": name,
                                    "exe_path": exe_path,
                                    "source": "registry",
                                })
                        except Exception:
                            continue
            except FileNotFoundError:
                continue
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.debug("Error querying registry key %s: %s", subkey, exc)

        return results

    def scan_start_menu_shortcuts(self) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
        """Scan Start Menu directories for .lnk shortcuts."""
        results: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
        search_dirs: List[Path] = []

        appdata = os.environ.get("APPDATA")
        if appdata:
            search_dirs.append(Path(appdata) / r"Microsoft\Windows\Start Menu\Programs")

        progdata = os.environ.get("PROGRAMDATA")
        if progdata:
            search_dirs.append(Path(progdata) / r"Microsoft\Windows\Start Menu\Programs")

        for sdir in search_dirs:
            if not sdir.exists():
                continue
            try:
                for lnk in sdir.rglob("*.lnk"):
                    # Ignore uninstaller shortcuts
                    stem = lnk.stem
                    if "uninstall" in stem.lower():
                        continue
                    resolved_exe = resolve_shortcut_target(str(lnk))
                    results.append({
                        "display_name": stem,
                        "exe_path": resolved_exe,
                        "source": "start_menu",
                    })
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.debug("Error scanning start menu directory %s: %s", sdir, exc)

        return results

    def build_inventory(
        self,
        raw_items: Optional[List[Dict[str, str]]] = None,
        observed_launch_counts: Optional[Dict[str, int]] = None,
        last_launch_timestamps: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
        existing_first_seen: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
    ) -> List[InventoryItem]:
        """Aggregate discovery from all sources and correlate with observation statistics."""
        if raw_items is None:
            raw_items = self.scan_windows_registry() + self.scan_start_menu_shortcuts()

        now_ts = format_utc_iso(self.clock.now_utc())
        launch_counts = observed_launch_counts or {}
        last_launches = last_launch_timestamps or {}
        first_seen_map = existing_first_seen or {}

        # Deduplicate items
        merged: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}

        for item in raw_items:
            d_name = item.get("display_name", "").strip()
            if not d_name:
                continue

            exe = item.get("exe_path", "").strip()
            source = item.get("source", "manual")

            key = normalize_app_key(d_name, exe)

            if key not in merged:
                merged[key] = {
                    "display_name": d_name,
                    "exe_path": exe,
                    "sources": {source},
                }
            else:
                merged[key]["sources"].add(source)
                if not merged[key]["exe_path"] and exe:
                    merged[key]["exe_path"] = exe

        inventory: List[InventoryItem] = []
        for key, entry in merged.items():
            d_name = entry["display_name"]
            exe = entry["exe_path"]
            source_str = ";".join(sorted(entry["sources"]))

            # Look up launch metrics using various plausible keys
            exe_name = Path(exe).name.lower() if exe else ""
            counts = (
                launch_counts.get(d_name.lower())
                or launch_counts.get(exe.lower())
                or launch_counts.get(exe_name)
                or 0
            )

            last_ts = (
                last_launches.get(d_name.lower())
                or last_launches.get(exe.lower())
                or last_launches.get(exe_name)
                or ""
            )

            first_seen = first_seen_map.get(key) or now_ts

            inventory.append(
                InventoryItem(
                    observed_ts_utc=now_ts,
                    display_name=d_name,
                    exe_path=exe,
                    inventory_source=source_str,
                    first_seen_ts_utc=first_seen,
                    last_observed_launch_ts_utc=last_ts,
                    observed_launch_count=counts,
                )
            )

        # Sort alphabetically by display name
        inventory.sort(key=lambda x: x.display_name.lower())
        return inventory



================================================================================
FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\collectors\base.py
================================================================================

"""Base abstractions for event sources and clocks."""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
import ctypes
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import os
import sys
import time
from typing import Callable, Optional
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RawEvent:
    """Raw application event captured by an event source."""
    event_type: str  # "process_start", "foreground_change", "system_error", etc.
    pid: int
    process_name: str
    exe_path: str
    timestamp_utc: datetime
    event_source: str  # "wmi", "winevent", "fake", etc.
    metadata: Optional[dict] = None


class Clock(ABC):
    """Abstract clock interface for deterministic time and idle query."""

    @abstractmethod
    def now_utc(self) -> datetime:
        """Return current timezone-aware UTC datetime."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def now_local(self, tz_name: str = "local") -> datetime:
        """Return current datetime in specified or local timezone."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def get_idle_seconds(self) -> float:
        """Return user idle duration in seconds."""
        pass


class SystemClock(Clock):
    """Real system clock implementation."""

    def now_utc(self) -> datetime:
        return datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    def now_local(self, tz_name: str = "local") -> datetime:
        if tz_name.lower() == "utc":
            return datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if tz_name.lower() == "local":
            return datetime.now().astimezone()
        try:
            return datetime.now(ZoneInfo(tz_name))
        except Exception:
            return datetime.now().astimezone()

    def get_idle_seconds(self) -> float:
        """Query Windows system idle time via GetLastInputInfo."""
        if sys.platform != "win32":
            return 0.0

        class LASTINPUTINFO(ctypes.Structure):
            _fields_ = [("cbSize", ctypes.c_uint), ("dwTime", ctypes.c_uint)]

        lii = LASTINPUTINFO()
        lii.cbSize = ctypes.sizeof(LASTINPUTINFO)
        if ctypes.windll.user32.GetLastInputInfo(ctypes.byref(lii)):
            tick = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetTickCount()
            return idle_seconds(tick, lii.dwTime)
        return 0.0


def idle_seconds(tick: int, last_input: int) -> float:
    """Calculate idle seconds handling 32-bit GetTickCount wrap-around correctly."""
    diff_ms = (int(tick) - int(last_input)) & 0xFFFFFFFF
    return max(0.0, float(diff_ms) / 1000.0)


calculate_idle_seconds = idle_seconds


class FakeClock(Clock):
    """Deterministic fake clock for unit tests."""

    def __init__(self, start_utc: datetime, idle_seconds: float = 0.0):
        if start_utc.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError("FakeClock requires timezone-aware datetime")
        self._current_utc = start_utc.astimezone(timezone.utc)
        self._idle_seconds = idle_seconds

    def set_time(self, new_utc: datetime) -> None:
        if new_utc.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError("FakeClock requires timezone-aware datetime")
        self._current_utc = new_utc.astimezone(timezone.utc)

    def advance(self, seconds: float) -> None:
        self._current_utc += timedelta(seconds=seconds)

    def set_idle_seconds(self, seconds: float) -> None:
        self._idle_seconds = max(0.0, seconds)

    def now_utc(self) -> datetime:
        return self._current_utc

    def now_local(self, tz_name: str = "local") -> datetime:
        if tz_name.lower() == "utc":
            return self._current_utc
        if tz_name.lower() == "local":
            return self._current_utc.astimezone()
        try:
            return self._current_utc.astimezone(ZoneInfo(tz_name))
        except Exception:
            return self._current_utc.astimezone()

    def get_idle_seconds(self) -> float:
        return self._idle_seconds


@dataclass
class CollectorHealth:
    """Structured health state for an event collector."""
    name: str
    state: str  # "active" | "degraded" | "failed" | "disabled" | "stopped"
    message: str
    since_utc: Optional[str] = None
    last_event_ts_utc: Optional[str] = None
    event_count: int = 0


class BaseEventSource(ABC):
    """Abstract interface for application event sources."""

    def __init__(self, callback: Callable[[RawEvent], None], name: str = "collector"):
        self.callback = callback
        self.name = name
        self._running = False
        self.health_state: str = "stopped"  # "active" | "degraded" | "failed" | "disabled" | "stopped"
        self.status_message: str = "Stopped"
        self._start_time_utc: Optional[str] = None
        self._last_event_ts_utc: Optional[str] = None
        self._event_count: int = 0

    @abstractmethod
    def start(self) -> None:
        """Start listening for events."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def stop(self) -> None:
        """Stop listening for events."""
        pass

    @property
    def is_running(self) -> bool:
        return self._running

    def health(self) -> CollectorHealth:
        """Return structured health information."""
        return CollectorHealth(
            name=self.name,
            state=self.health_state,
            message=self.status_message,
            since_utc=self._start_time_utc,
            last_event_ts_utc=self._last_event_ts_utc,
            event_count=self._event_count,
        )
