"""Fake event source for deterministic testing."""

from typing import Callable, List
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import BaseEventSource, RawEvent


class FakeEventSource(BaseEventSource):
    """Event source that dispatches pre-scripted or manually injected events."""

    def __init__(self, callback: Callable[[RawEvent], None]):
        super().__init__(callback)
        self.emitted_events: List[RawEvent] = []

    def start(self) -> None:
        self._running = True

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self._running = False

    def emit(self, event: RawEvent) -> None:
        if self._running:
            self.emitted_events.append(event)
            self.callback(event)



================================================================================
FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\collectors\foreground_events.py
================================================================================

"""Windows foreground window event hook collector."""

import ctypes
from ctypes import wintypes
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import logging
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import threading
from typing import Callable, Optional
import psutil

from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import BaseEventSource, Clock, SystemClock, RawEvent

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND = 0x0003
WINEVENT_OUTOFCONTEXT = 0x0000
WINEVENT_SKIPOWNPROCESS = 0x0002
WM_QUIT = 0x0012

# Win32 callback type
WINEVENTPROC = ctypes.WINFUNCTYPE(
    None,
    wintypes.HANDLE,
    wintypes.DWORD,
    wintypes.HWND,
    wintypes.LONG,
    wintypes.LONG,
    wintypes.DWORD,
    wintypes.DWORD,
)


def get_process_info_from_pid(pid: int) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Retrieve process name and executable path given a PID."""
    if pid <= 0:
        return ("unknown", "")
    try:
        proc = psutil.Process(pid)
        name = proc.name()
        try:
            exe = proc.exe()
        except (psutil.AccessDenied, psutil.ZombieProcess):
            exe = ""
        return (name, exe)
    except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied, Exception):
        pass

    # Fallback to kernel32 QueryFullProcessImageNameW
    if sys.platform == "win32":
        PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION = 0x1000
        h_process = ctypes.windll.kernel32.OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, False, pid)
        if h_process:
            try:
                buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(1024)
                size = wintypes.DWORD(1024)
                if ctypes.windll.kernel32.QueryFullProcessImageNameW(h_process, 0, buf, ctypes.byref(size)):
                    exe_path = buf.value
                    proc_name = Path(exe_path).name
                    return (proc_name, exe_path)
            finally:
                ctypes.windll.kernel32.CloseHandle(h_process)

    return ("unknown", "")


def resolve_uwp_window(hwnd: int) -> tuple[int, str, str]:
    """If hwnd belongs to ApplicationFrameHost, inspect child windows to find the real UWP process."""
    if sys.platform != "win32" or not hwnd:
        return (0, "unknown", "")

    pid = wintypes.DWORD()
    ctypes.windll.user32.GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, ctypes.byref(pid))
    frame_pid = pid.value
    proc_name, exe_path = get_process_info_from_pid(frame_pid)

    if proc_name.lower() == "applicationframehost.exe":
        real_info: list[tuple[int, str, str]] = []

        WNDENUMPROC = ctypes.WINFUNCTYPE(wintypes.BOOL, wintypes.HWND, wintypes.LPARAM)

        def enum_child_proc(child_hwnd, lparam):
            child_pid = wintypes.DWORD()
            ctypes.windll.user32.GetWindowThreadProcessId(child_hwnd, ctypes.byref(child_pid))
            c_pid = child_pid.value
            if c_pid > 0 and c_pid != frame_pid:
                c_name, c_exe = get_process_info_from_pid(c_pid)
                if c_name.lower() != "applicationframehost.exe":
                    real_info.append((c_pid, c_name, c_exe))
                    return False  # stop enumeration
            return True

        try:
            ctypes.windll.user32.EnumChildWindows(hwnd, WNDENUMPROC(enum_child_proc), 0)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("Failed child window enumeration on hwnd %s: %s", hwnd, exc)

        if real_info:
            return real_info[0]

    return (frame_pid, proc_name, exe_path)


class WindowsForegroundHook(BaseEventSource):
    """Listens for Windows foreground window change events using SetWinEventHook."""

    def __init__(self, callback: Callable[[RawEvent], None], clock: Optional[Clock] = None):
        super().__init__(callback, name="Foreground Hook")
        self.clock = clock or SystemClock()
        self._thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None
        self._thread_id: Optional[int] = None
        self._hook = None
        self._proc_delegate = None
        self.health_state = "stopped"
        self.status_message = "Stopped"

    def start(self) -> None:
        if self._running or sys.platform != "win32":
            if sys.platform != "win32":
                self.health_state = "failed"
                self.status_message = "Unsupported on non-Windows OS"
            return

        self._running = True
        self._start_time_utc = self.clock.now_utc().strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%fZ")
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run_hook_loop, daemon=True, name="ForegroundHookThread")
        self._thread.start()

    def stop(self) -> None:
        if not self._running:
            return
        self._running = False

        if sys.platform == "win32" and self._thread_id:
            ctypes.windll.user32.PostThreadMessageW(self._thread_id, WM_QUIT, 0, 0)
        if self._thread and self._thread.is_alive():
            self._thread.join(timeout=2.0)
        self.health_state = "stopped"
        self.status_message = "Stopped"

    def _run_hook_loop(self) -> None:
        self._thread_id = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetCurrentThreadId()

        def win_event_callback(hWinEventHook, event, hwnd, idObject, idChild, dwEventThread, dwmsEventTime):
            if not self._running:
                return

            if event == EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND and hwnd:
                target_pid, proc_name, exe_path = resolve_uwp_window(hwnd)
                # Skip own process to prevent dashboard observing itself
                if target_pid > 0 and target_pid != os.getpid():
                    now = self.clock.now_utc()
                    self._last_event_ts_utc = now.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%fZ")
                    self._event_count += 1
                    raw_event = RawEvent(
                        event_type="foreground_change",
                        pid=target_pid,
                        process_name=proc_name,
                        exe_path=exe_path,
                        timestamp_utc=now,
                        event_source="winevent",
                    )
                    try:
                        self.callback(raw_event)
                    except Exception as exc:
                        logger.exception("Error in foreground hook callback: %s", exc)

        self._proc_delegate = WINEVENTPROC(win_event_callback)
        self._hook = ctypes.windll.user32.SetWinEventHook(
            EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND,
            EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND,
            0,
            self._proc_delegate,
            0,
            0,
            WINEVENT_OUTOFCONTEXT | WINEVENT_SKIPOWNPROCESS,
        )

        if not self._hook:
            self.health_state = "failed"
            self.status_message = "Failed: SetWinEventHook rejected"
            logger.error("Failed to install SetWinEventHook for foreground window")
            self._running = False
            return

        self.health_state = "active"
        self.status_message = "Active (Windows WinEvent Hook)"

        try:
            msg = wintypes.MSG()
            while self._running:
                b_ret = ctypes.windll.user32.GetMessageW(ctypes.byref(msg), 0, 0, 0)
                if b_ret <= 0:
                    break
                ctypes.windll.user32.TranslateMessage(ctypes.byref(msg))
                ctypes.windll.user32.DispatchMessageW(ctypes.byref(msg))
        finally:
            if self._hook:
                ctypes.windll.user32.UnhookWinEvent(self._hook)
                self._hook = None
            if not self._running:
                self.health_state = "stopped"
                self.status_message = "Stopped"



================================================================================
FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\collectors\resource_snapshot.py
================================================================================

"""Resource snapshot collector capturing per-process and system memory/CPU metrics."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import logging
from typing import Optional
import psutil

from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import Clock, SystemClock
from desktop_intelligence.models import ResourceSample, format_utc_iso, new_id

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def capture_resource_sample(
    pid: int,
    process_name: str,
    trigger_event: str,
    clock: Optional[Clock] = None,
) -> Optional[ResourceSample]:
    """Capture a lightweight resource snapshot for a given process and system state.

    Catches NoSuchProcess and AccessDenied gracefully, returning a partial sample or None.
    Does not claim an instantaneous per-process CPU percentage; records cumulative CPU time.
    """
    if pid <= 0:
        return None

    clk = clock or SystemClock()
    sample_ts = format_utc_iso(clk.now_utc())

    rss_bytes: Optional[int] = None
    proc_mem_pct: Optional[float] = None
    cpu_time_sec: Optional[float] = None
    sys_mem_pct: Optional[float] = None

    # System memory pressure
    try:
        sys_mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
        sys_mem_pct = round(float(sys_mem.percent), 2)
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.debug("Failed to read system virtual memory: %s", exc)

    # Process metrics
    try:
        proc = psutil.Process(pid)
        mem_info = proc.memory_info()
        rss_bytes = int(mem_info.rss)

        try:
            proc_mem_pct = round(float(proc.memory_percent()), 2)
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            cpu_times = proc.cpu_times()
            cpu_time_sec = round(float(cpu_times.user + cpu_times.system), 3)
        except Exception:
            pass

    except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied, psutil.ZombieProcess):
        # Process exited or inaccessible; retain system metrics if available
        pass
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.debug("Unexpected error reading process metrics for PID %d: %s", pid, exc)

    return ResourceSample(
        sample_id=new_id("res"),
        sample_ts_utc=sample_ts,
        trigger_event=trigger_event,
        pid=pid,
        process_name=process_name or "unknown",
        rss_bytes=rss_bytes,
        process_memory_percent=proc_mem_pct,
        cpu_time_seconds=cpu_time_sec,
        system_memory_percent=sys_mem_pct,
    )


def format_bytes_to_human(byte_count: Optional[int]) -> str:
    """Format bytes into readable unit (B, KB, MB, GB)."""
    if byte_count is None:
        return "N/A"
    if byte_count < 1024:
        return f"{byte_count} B"
    elif byte_count < 1024 * 1024:
        return f"{byte_count / 1024:.1f} KB"
    elif byte_count < 1024 * 1024 * 1024:
        return f"{byte_count / (1024 * 1024):.1f} MB"
    else:
        return f"{byte_count / (1024 * 1024 * 1024):.2f} GB"



================================================================================
FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\collectors\system_errors.py
================================================================================

"""Windows application error and reliability event collector."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json
import logging
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import Clock, SystemClock
from desktop_intelligence.models import format_utc_iso

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Standard Windows Event Log type constants
EVENTLOG_SUCCESS = 0x0000
EVENTLOG_ERROR_TYPE = 0x0001
EVENTLOG_WARNING_TYPE = 0x0002
EVENTLOG_INFORMATION_TYPE = 0x0004
EVENTLOG_AUDIT_SUCCESS = 0x0008
EVENTLOG_AUDIT_FAILURE = 0x0010

SUPPORTED_ERROR_PROVIDERS = {
    "Application Error",
    "Application Hang",
    "Windows Error Reporting",
}


@dataclass
class SystemErrorEvent:
    event_ts_utc: str
    app_name: str
    error_code: str
    faulting_module: str
    description: str
    source: str = "Windows_Application_Error"
    severity: str = "Error"
    provider: str = "Application Error"
    event_id: int = 1000


def is_supported_reliability_event(source_name: str, event_id: int, event_type: int) -> bool:
    """Determine whether an event record represents a genuine application failure or error report.

    Explicitly excludes informational service events (e.g., service starts, background heartbeats)
    that happen to share Event ID 1000 or other IDs under non-crash providers.
    """
    if not source_name:
        return False

    clean_source = source_name.strip()

    # Informational events are only eligible if from Windows Error Reporting
    if event_type == EVENTLOG_INFORMATION_TYPE and clean_source != "Windows Error Reporting":
        return False

    # Check known genuine error providers
    if clean_source in SUPPORTED_ERROR_PROVIDERS:
        if clean_source == "Application Error" and event_id in {1000, 1001}:
            return True
        if clean_source == "Application Hang" and event_id in {1002}:
            return True
        if clean_source == "Windows Error Reporting" and event_id in {1001}:
            return True

    # Generic Application log errors with error severity
    if event_type == EVENTLOG_ERROR_TYPE and "Error" in clean_source:
        return True

    return False


def parse_application_error_message(msg: str, provider: str = "") -> Dict[str, str]:
    """Parse Windows Event ID 1000/1001/1002 message text into structured fields.

    Handles English and localized variants, application crashes, application hangs,
    and Windows Error Reporting / LiveKernel diagnostic payloads safely.
    Preserves honest metadata without fabricating missing fields.
    """
    fields = {
        "app_name": "unknown",
        "error_code": "",
        "faulting_module": "",
        "description": msg.strip()[:240] if msg else "",
    }
    if not msg:
        return fields

    # 1. Faulting application name (Application Error ID 1000)
    app_match = re.search(r"Faulting application name:\s*([^,\r\n]+)", msg, re.IGNORECASE)
    if app_match:
        fields["app_name"] = app_match.group(1).strip()
    else:
        # Check Application Hang (ID 1002) pattern:
        # "The program notepad.exe version 10.0.19041.1 stopped interacting with Windows..."
        hang_match = re.search(r"The program\s+([^,\s]+)\s+version\s+[^,\r\n]+\s+stopped interacting", msg, re.IGNORECASE)
        if hang_match:
            fields["app_name"] = hang_match.group(1).strip()

    # 2. Faulting module name
    mod_match = re.search(r"Faulting module name:\s*([^,\r\n]+)", msg, re.IGNORECASE)
    if mod_match:
        fields["faulting_module"] = mod_match.group(1).strip()

    # 3. Exception code
    code_match = re.search(r"Exception code:\s*([^,\r\n]+)", msg, re.IGNORECASE)
    if code_match:
        fields["error_code"] = code_match.group(1).strip()

    # 4. Windows Error Reporting / Kernel diagnostic telemetry (Event ID 1001)
    if "LiveKernelEvent" in msg:
        fields["app_name"] = "System / OS Report"
        # Extract bugcheck / error code (e.g. 1b8, 1a8, 1b0)
        lk_code = re.search(r"LiveKernelEvent\s+(?:Not available|[^\s]+(?:\s+[^\s]+)?)\s+\d+\s+([0-9a-fA-F]+)", msg, re.IGNORECASE)
        if not lk_code:
            lk_code = re.search(r"Kernel_([0-9a-fA-F]+)_", msg, re.IGNORECASE)
        if lk_code:
            code_hex = lk_code.group(1)
            fields["error_code"] = f"0x{code_hex.upper()}" if not code_hex.lower().startswith("0x") else code_hex
        # Detect watchdog or driver component from report path if present
        mod_match = re.search(r"LiveKernelReports\\([A-Za-z0-9_]+)\\", msg)
        if mod_match:
            fields["faulting_module"] = mod_match.group(1)

    elif "BlueScreen" in msg:
        fields["app_name"] = "System / OS Report"
        bs_code = re.search(r"BlueScreen\s+(?:Not available|[^\s]+(?:\s+[^\s]+)?)\s+\d+\s+([0-9a-fA-F]+)", msg, re.IGNORECASE)
        if not bs_code:
            bs_code = re.search(r"BlueScreen\s+.*?\s+([0-9a-fA-F]{1,8})\b", msg, re.IGNORECASE)
        if bs_code:
            code_hex = bs_code.group(1)
            fields["error_code"] = f"0x{code_hex.upper()}" if not code_hex.lower().startswith("0x") else code_hex

    elif "APPCRASH" in msg or "BEX" in msg or "MoAppCrash" in msg:
        # Standard WER crash bucket
        p1 = re.search(r"(?:P1|Problem signature:\s*P1):\s*([^\r\n\s]+)", msg, re.IGNORECASE)
        if p1 and p1.group(1).lower() not in {"not available", "unknown"}:
            fields["app_name"] = p1.group(1).strip()
        p4 = re.search(r"(?:P4|Problem signature:\s*P4):\s*([^\r\n\s]+)", msg, re.IGNORECASE)
        if p4 and not fields["faulting_module"] and p4.group(1).lower() not in {"not available", "unknown"}:
            fields["faulting_module"] = p4.group(1).strip()
        p7 = re.search(r"(?:P7|Problem signature:\s*P7):\s*([^\r\n\s]+)", msg, re.IGNORECASE)
        if p7 and not fields["error_code"] and p7.group(1).lower() not in {"not available", "unknown"}:
            fields["error_code"] = p7.group(1).strip()

    # If provider is explicitly Windows Error Reporting and application could not be identified
    if provider == "Windows Error Reporting" or ("Windows Error Reporting" in msg and fields["app_name"] == "unknown"):
        if fields["app_name"] == "unknown":
            fields["app_name"] = "Windows Error Reporting"

    return fields


_ERRORS_CACHE: Optional[Tuple[List[SystemErrorEvent], str]] = None
_ERRORS_CACHE_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_ERRORS_CACHE_TIME = 0.0


def clear_errors_cache() -> None:
    """Clear in-memory cached reliability errors."""
    global _ERRORS_CACHE, _ERRORS_CACHE_TIME
    with _ERRORS_CACHE_LOCK:
        _ERRORS_CACHE = None
        _ERRORS_CACHE_TIME = 0.0


def query_recent_application_errors(
    max_records: int = 50,
    clock: Optional[Clock] = None,
    force_refresh: bool = False,
    cache_ttl_seconds: float = 60.0,
) -> Tuple[List[SystemErrorEvent], str]:
    """Query recent Windows Application Error records (Event ID 1000/1002/1001).

    Uses a thread-safe in-memory cache (default 60s) to keep GUI operations non-blocking.
    Filters strictly for genuine application errors and hangs, cleanly excluding
    informational service status events (e.g. Codex sandbox service heartbeats).
    Returns a tuple of (events_list, diagnostic_status).
    """
    global _ERRORS_CACHE, _ERRORS_CACHE_TIME

    if not force_refresh:
        with _ERRORS_CACHE_LOCK:
            if _ERRORS_CACHE is not None and (time.time() - _ERRORS_CACHE_TIME) < cache_ttl_seconds:
                return _ERRORS_CACHE

    events, status = _fetch_recent_application_errors(max_records=max_records, clock=clock)

    with _ERRORS_CACHE_LOCK:
        _ERRORS_CACHE = (events, status)
        _ERRORS_CACHE_TIME = time.time()

    return events, status


def _fetch_recent_application_errors(
    max_records: int = 50,
    clock: Optional[Clock] = None,
) -> Tuple[List[SystemErrorEvent], str]:
    clk = clock or SystemClock()
    now_ts = format_utc_iso(clk.now_utc())

    if sys.platform != "win32":
        return [], "System event monitoring is only supported on Windows."

    # Try pywin32 win32evtlog if present
    try:
        import win32evtlog  # type: ignore
        server = "localhost"
        logtype = "Application"
        flags = win32evtlog.EVENTLOG_BACKWARDS_READ | win32evtlog.EVENTLOG_SEQUENTIAL_READ
        hlog = win32evtlog.OpenEventLog(server, logtype)
        events: List[SystemErrorEvent] = []

        try:
            records = win32evtlog.ReadEventLog(hlog, flags, 0)
            while records and len(events) < max_records:
                for ev in records:
                    event_id = ev.EventID & 0xFFFF
                    event_type = getattr(ev, "EventType", 0)
                    source_name = getattr(ev, "SourceName", "") or ""

                    # Strictly filter for genuine reliability events
                    if not is_supported_reliability_event(source_name, event_id, event_type):
                        continue

                    msg = " ".join(str(s) for s in ev.StringInserts or [])
                    parsed = parse_application_error_message(msg, provider=source_name)

                    # Determine severity
                    if event_type == EVENTLOG_ERROR_TYPE:
                        severity = "Crash" if source_name == "Application Error" else ("Hang" if source_name == "Application Hang" else "Error")
                    elif event_type == EVENTLOG_WARNING_TYPE:
                        severity = "Warning"
                    else:
                        severity = "Report"

                    t_utc = ev.TimeGenerated.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
                    events.append(
                        SystemErrorEvent(
                            event_ts_utc=format_utc_iso(t_utc),
                            app_name=parsed["app_name"],
                            error_code=parsed["error_code"] or "-",
                            faulting_module=parsed["faulting_module"] or "-",
                            description=parsed["description"] or msg[:240],
                            source="Windows_Application_Error",
                            severity=severity,
                            provider=source_name,
                            event_id=event_id,
                        )
                    )
                    if len(events) >= max_records:
                        break
                records = win32evtlog.ReadEventLog(hlog, flags, 0)
        finally:
            win32evtlog.CloseEventLog(hlog)

        return events, f"Operational (retrieved {len(events)} recent error events)"

    except ImportError:
        pass
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.debug("win32evtlog query failed: %s", exc)

    # Fallback to PowerShell Get-WinEvent with safety timeout
    try:
        ps_cmd = (
            f"Get-WinEvent -FilterHashtable @{{LogName='Application'; ProviderName=@('Application Error', 'Application Hang'); Level=2}} "
            f"-MaxEvents {max_records} -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | "
            f"Select-Object -Property TimeCreated, ProviderName, Id, Message | ConvertTo-Json -Compress"
        )
        res = subprocess.run(
            ["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-Command", ps_cmd],
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=5,
        )
        if res.returncode == 0 and res.stdout.strip():
            raw_json = json.loads(res.stdout.strip())
            items = raw_json if isinstance(raw_json, list) else [raw_json]
            events: List[SystemErrorEvent] = []
            for item in items:
                msg = item.get("Message", "")
                prov = item.get("ProviderName", "Application Error")
                parsed = parse_application_error_message(msg, provider=prov)
                eid = item.get("Id", 1000)
                events.append(
                    SystemErrorEvent(
                        event_ts_utc=now_ts,
                        app_name=parsed["app_name"],
                        error_code=parsed["error_code"] or "-",
                        faulting_module=parsed["faulting_module"] or "-",
                        description=parsed["description"],
                        source="Windows_Application_Error",
                        severity="Crash" if prov == "Application Error" else "Hang",
                        provider=prov,
                        event_id=eid,
                    )
                )
            return events, f"Operational (PowerShell retrieved {len(events)} error events)"
        else:
            return [], "No recent Application Error events recorded in Windows Event Log."

    except Exception as exc:
        return [], f"Application error source unavailable: {exc}"



================================================================================
FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\collectors\windows_process_events.py
================================================================================

"""Windows process-start event listener using WMI ProcessTrace with graceful fallback and health reporting."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import logging
import sys
import threading
import time
from typing import Callable, Optional

from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import BaseEventSource, Clock, SystemClock, RawEvent
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.foreground_events import get_process_info_from_pid

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class WindowsProcessStartListener(BaseEventSource):
    """Listens for process-start events using WMI Win32_ProcessStartTrace with fallback to WMI event queries."""

    def __init__(self, callback: Callable[[RawEvent], None], clock: Optional[Clock] = None):
        super().__init__(callback, name="Process Trace")
        self.clock = clock or SystemClock()
        self._thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None
        self._stop_event = threading.Event()
        self.health_state = "stopped"
        self.status_message = "Stopped"

    def start(self) -> None:
        if self._running or sys.platform != "win32":
            if sys.platform != "win32":
                self.health_state = "failed"
                self.status_message = "Unsupported on non-Windows OS"
            return

        self._running = True
        self._stop_event.clear()
        self._start_time_utc = self.clock.now_utc().strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%fZ")
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run_wmi_listener, daemon=True, name="WMIProcessThread")
        self._thread.start()

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self._running = False
        self._stop_event.set()
        if self._thread and self._thread.is_alive():
            self._thread.join(timeout=2.0)
        self.health_state = "stopped"
        self.status_message = "Stopped"

    def _run_wmi_listener(self) -> None:
        try:
            import pythoncom
            import wmi
        except ImportError:
            self.health_state = "unavailable"
            self.status_message = "Failed: wmi or pythoncom module not installed"
            logger.warning("%s; process-start listener disabled", self.status_message)
            self._running = False
            return

        pythoncom.CoInitialize()
        watcher = None
        wmi_client = None
        is_trace = False

        try:
            try:
                wmi_client = wmi.WMI()
            except Exception as exc:
                self.health_state = "unavailable"
                self.status_message = f"Failed: WMI connection failed ({exc})"
                logger.warning("WMI client initialization failed: %s", exc)
                self._running = False
                return

            # Attempt 1: High-performance kernel process start trace
            try:
                watcher = wmi_client.Win32_ProcessStartTrace.watch_for("creation")
                is_trace = True
                self.health_state = "active"
                self.status_message = "Active (Kernel Process Trace)"
                logger.info("Process-start listener initialized: %s", self.status_message)
            except (wmi.x_access_denied, Exception) as exc:
                logger.info(
                    "Win32_ProcessStartTrace subscription unavailable (%s); attempting standard WMI process watcher...",
                    exc,
                )

            # Attempt 2: Standard user-accessible WMI process creation event query (polling-based fallback)
            if watcher is None:
                try:
                    watcher = wmi_client.watch_for(
                        notification_type="Creation",
                        wmi_class="Win32_Process",
                        delay_secs=1,
                    )
                    is_trace = False
                    self.health_state = "degraded"
                    self.status_message = "Degraded (polling-based; sub-second processes may be missed)"
                    logger.info("Process-start listener initialized: %s", self.status_message)
                except Exception as exc:
                    self.health_state = "unavailable"
                    self.status_message = f"Failed: WMI process subscription restricted ({exc})"
                    logger.warning("WMI process creation watcher could not be started: %s", exc)
                    self._running = False
                    return

            # Listening loop
            while self._running and not self._stop_event.is_set():
                try:
                    proc_event = watcher(timeout_ms=1000)
                    if proc_event:
                        if is_trace:
                            pid = int(getattr(proc_event, "ProcessID", getattr(proc_event, "ProcessId", 0)))
                            proc_name = str(getattr(proc_event, "ProcessName", ""))
                        else:
                            pid = int(getattr(proc_event, "ProcessId", getattr(proc_event, "ProcessID", getattr(proc_event, "Handle", 0))))
                            proc_name = str(getattr(proc_event, "Caption", getattr(proc_event, "Name", "")))

                        if pid > 0:
                            _, exe_path = get_process_info_from_pid(pid)
                            now = self.clock.now_utc()
                            self._last_event_ts_utc = now.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%fZ")
                            self._event_count += 1
                            raw_event = RawEvent(
                                event_type="process_start",
                                pid=pid,
                                process_name=proc_name,
                                exe_path=exe_path,
                                timestamp_utc=now,
                                event_source="wmi",
                            )
                            try:
                                self.callback(raw_event)
                            except Exception as cb_exc:
                                logger.exception("Error in process-start callback: %s", cb_exc)
                except wmi.x_wmi_timed_out:
                    continue
                except Exception as loop_exc:
                    if self._running and not self._stop_event.is_set():
                        logger.warning("WMI watch iteration error: %s", loop_exc)
                        self._stop_event.wait(1.0)
        finally:
            # Clean COM release to prevent "Win32 exception occurred releasing IUnknown"
            watcher = None
            wmi_client = None
            import gc
            gc.collect()
            try:
                pythoncom.CoUninitialize()
            except Exception:
                pass
            if not self._running and self.health_state not in ("failed", "unavailable"):
                self.health_state = "stopped"
                self.status_message = "Stopped"

================================================================================
FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\storage\csv_store.py

"""CSV storage engine with thread-safe append, schema validation, and integrity checks."""

import csv
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import io
import os
from pathlib import Path
import threading
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
import pandas as pd

from desktop_intelligence.models import (
    SCHEMAS,
    ActivityEvent,
    ActivitySession,
    ResourceSample,
    InventoryItem,
    RecommendationRecord,
    AutomationLogRecord,
    parse_utc_iso,
    ISO_UTC_REGEX,
)
from desktop_intelligence.paths import ResolvedPaths


class StorageError(Exception):
    """Base exception for storage errors."""
    pass


class SchemaMismatchError(StorageError):
    """Raised when an existing CSV file's headers do not match the expected schema."""
    pass


class CorruptRowError(StorageError):
    """Raised when a CSV row has invalid column count or malformed fields."""
    pass


class CsvStore:
    """Thread-safe CSV store managing all application data records."""

    def __init__(self, paths: ResolvedPaths):
        self.paths = paths
        self._locks: Dict[str, threading.RLock] = {}
        self._global_lock = threading.RLock()

    def _get_lock(self, file_path: Path) -> threading.RLock:
        key = str(file_path.resolve())
        with self._global_lock:
            if key not in self._locks:
                self._locks[key] = threading.RLock()
            return self._locks[key]

    def initialize_file(self, schema_name: str, file_path: Path) -> None:
        """Ensure the CSV file exists and has the correct header.

        Raises SchemaMismatchError if the file exists and headers do not match.
        """
        expected_headers = SCHEMAS[schema_name]
        lock = self._get_lock(file_path)

        with lock:
            file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

            if not file_path.exists() or file_path.stat().st_size == 0:
                with open(file_path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
                    writer = csv.writer(f, quoting=csv.QUOTE_MINIMAL, lineterminator="\n")
                    writer.writerow(expected_headers)
                return

            # File exists and is non-empty; verify headers
            with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
                reader = csv.reader(f)
                try:
                    actual_headers = next(reader)
                except StopIteration:
                    actual_headers = []

            if actual_headers != expected_headers:
                raise SchemaMismatchError(
                    f"Schema mismatch in {file_path.name} for schema '{schema_name}'.\n"
                    f"Expected: {expected_headers}\n"
                    f"Actual:   {actual_headers}"
                )

    def append_row(self, schema_name: str, file_path: Path, row: List[str]) -> None:
        """Thread-safe append of a single row with schema and timestamp validation."""
        self.append_rows(schema_name, file_path, [row])

    def append_rows(self, schema_name: str, file_path: Path, rows: List[List[str]]) -> None:
        """Thread-safe append of multiple rows in one operation."""
        if not rows:
            return

        expected_headers = SCHEMAS[schema_name]
        expected_len = len(expected_headers)

        for row in rows:
            if len(row) != expected_len:
                raise CorruptRowError(
                    f"Row length {len(row)} does not match schema '{schema_name}' length {expected_len}: {row!r}"
                )

        lock = self._get_lock(file_path)
        with lock:
            self.initialize_file(schema_name, file_path)

            try:
                with open(file_path, "a", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
                    writer = csv.writer(f, quoting=csv.QUOTE_MINIMAL, lineterminator="\n")
                    for row in rows:
                        writer.writerow(row)
            except OSError as exc:
                raise StorageError(f"Failed to append to {file_path}: {exc}") from exc

    def replace_all(self, schema_name: str, file_path: Path, rows: List[List[str]]) -> None:
        """Atomically replace entire contents of CSV file using a temporary file and os.replace."""
        expected_headers = SCHEMAS[schema_name]
        expected_len = len(expected_headers)
        for r in rows:
            if len(r) != expected_len:
                raise CorruptRowError(f"Row length {len(r)} does not match schema '{schema_name}' length {expected_len}: {r!r}")

        lock = self._get_lock(file_path)
        with lock:
            file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            tmp_path = file_path.with_name(f"{file_path.name}.{os.getpid()}_{threading.get_ident()}.tmp")
            try:
                with open(tmp_path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
                    writer = csv.writer(f, quoting=csv.QUOTE_MINIMAL, lineterminator="\n")
                    writer.writerow(expected_headers)
                    for row in rows:
                        writer.writerow(row)
                os.replace(tmp_path, file_path)
            except OSError as exc:
                raise StorageError(f"Failed to atomically write {file_path}: {exc}") from exc
            finally:
                if tmp_path.exists():
                    try:
                        tmp_path.unlink()
                    except Exception:
                        pass

    def read_rows(self, schema_name: str, file_path: Path) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
        """Read all rows from a CSV file into a list of dictionaries.

        Returns empty list if file doesn't exist or is empty.
        Raises SchemaMismatchError on header mismatch.
        """
        if not file_path.exists() or file_path.stat().st_size == 0:
            return []

        expected_headers = SCHEMAS[schema_name]
        lock = self._get_lock(file_path)

        with lock:
            with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
                reader = csv.reader(f)
                try:
                    headers = next(reader)
                except StopIteration:
                    return []

                if headers != expected_headers:
                    raise SchemaMismatchError(
                        f"Schema mismatch in {file_path.name}: expected {expected_headers}, got {headers}"
                    )

                results = []
                for row_idx, row in enumerate(reader, start=2):
                    if not row or (len(row) == 1 and not row[0].strip()):
                        continue  # skip accidental empty lines
                    if len(row) != len(headers):
                        # Skip or handle corrupt row
                        continue
                    results.append(dict(zip(headers, row)))

                return results

    def read_dataframe(self, schema_name: str, file_path: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Read CSV as a Pandas DataFrame with expected columns and normalized types."""
        expected_headers = SCHEMAS[schema_name]
        if not file_path.exists() or file_path.stat().st_size == 0:
            return pd.DataFrame(columns=expected_headers)

        lock = self._get_lock(file_path)
        with lock:
            try:
                df = pd.read_csv(file_path, encoding="utf-8", dtype=str, keep_default_na=False)
            except Exception as exc:
                raise StorageError(f"Failed to read DataFrame from {file_path}: {exc}") from exc

            if list(df.columns) != expected_headers:
                raise SchemaMismatchError(
                    f"Columns mismatch in {file_path.name}: expected {expected_headers}, got {list(df.columns)}"
                )
            return df

    # Typed convenience helpers
    def append_activity_event(self, event: ActivityEvent) -> None:
        self._validate_timestamp(event.event_ts_utc)
        self.append_row("activity_events", self.paths.activity_events_csv, event.to_row())

    def append_activity_session(self, session: ActivitySession) -> None:
        self._validate_timestamp(session.start_ts_utc)
        self._validate_timestamp(session.end_ts_utc)
        self.append_row("activity_sessions", self.paths.activity_sessions_csv, session.to_row())

    def append_resource_sample(self, sample: ResourceSample) -> None:
        self._validate_timestamp(sample.sample_ts_utc)
        self.append_row("resource_samples", self.paths.resource_samples_csv, sample.to_row())

    def append_inventory_item(self, item: InventoryItem) -> None:
        self._validate_timestamp(item.observed_ts_utc)
        self.append_row("application_inventory", self.paths.application_inventory_csv, item.to_row())

    def append_recommendation(self, rec: RecommendationRecord) -> None:
        self._validate_timestamp(rec.created_ts_utc)
        self.append_row("recommendations", self.paths.recommendations_csv, rec.to_row())

    def append_automation_log(self, log: AutomationLogRecord) -> None:
        self._validate_timestamp(log.ts_utc)
        self.append_row("automation_log", self.paths.automation_log_csv, log.to_row())

    def prune_retention(self, events_days: int, sessions_days: int, samples_days: int) -> Dict[str, int]:
        """Prune records older than configured retention days based on their UTC timestamp."""
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        pruned_counts = {"events": 0, "sessions": 0, "samples": 0}

        # 1. Activity Events
        if events_days > 0 and self.paths.activity_events_csv.exists():
            cutoff = now - timedelta(days=events_days)
            pruned_counts["events"] = self._prune_file(
                "activity_events", self.paths.activity_events_csv, "event_ts_utc", cutoff
            )

        # 2. Activity Sessions
        if sessions_days > 0 and self.paths.activity_sessions_csv.exists():
            cutoff = now - timedelta(days=sessions_days)
            pruned_counts["sessions"] = self._prune_file(
                "activity_sessions", self.paths.activity_sessions_csv, "end_ts_utc", cutoff
            )

        # 3. Resource Samples
        if samples_days > 0 and self.paths.resource_samples_csv.exists():
            cutoff = now - timedelta(days=samples_days)
            pruned_counts["samples"] = self._prune_file(
                "resource_samples", self.paths.resource_samples_csv, "sample_ts_utc", cutoff
            )

        return pruned_counts

    def _prune_file(self, schema_name: str, file_path: Path, ts_col: str, cutoff: datetime) -> int:
        lock = self._get_lock(file_path)
        with lock:
            rows = self.read_rows(schema_name, file_path)
            if not rows:
                return 0

            kept_rows = []
            removed_count = 0

            for r in rows:
                try:
                    row_dt = parse_utc_iso(r[ts_col])
                    if row_dt >= cutoff:
                        kept_rows.append([r[col] for col in SCHEMAS[schema_name]])
                    else:
                        removed_count += 1
                except Exception:
                    # In case of malformed timestamp, retain for safety
                    kept_rows.append([r[col] for col in SCHEMAS[schema_name]])

            if removed_count > 0:
                self.replace_all(schema_name, file_path, kept_rows)

            return removed_count

    def _validate_timestamp(self, ts_str: str) -> None:
        """Validate timestamp string is valid timezone-aware UTC ISO 8601."""
        if not ts_str or not isinstance(ts_str, str):
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid timestamp: {ts_str!r}")
        parse_utc_iso(ts_str)

"""Main Application Window for Desktop Intelligence."""

import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional
from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, QThread, QTimer, Signal, Qt
from PySide6.QtGui import QAction, QIcon
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QApplication,
    QHBoxLayout,
    QLabel,
    QListWidget,
    QListWidgetItem,
    QMainWindow,
    QMenu,
    QMessageBox,
    QStackedWidget,
    QStyle,
    QSystemTrayIcon,
    QVBoxLayout,
    QWidget,
)
import pandas as pd

from desktop_intelligence.analytics.clean import clean_sessions_df, clean_samples_df
from desktop_intelligence.analytics.recommendations import generate_recommendations
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.application_inventory import ApplicationInventoryScanner
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.system_errors import query_recent_application_errors
from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig, save_config
from desktop_intelligence.models import (
    AutomationLogRecord,
    InventoryItem,
    RecommendationRecord,
    format_utc_iso,
    now_utc,
)
from desktop_intelligence.paths import ResolvedPaths
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CsvStore
from desktop_intelligence.ui.pages.overview import OverviewPage
from desktop_intelligence.ui.pages.usage import UsagePage
from desktop_intelligence.ui.pages.health import HealthPage
from desktop_intelligence.ui.pages.applications import ApplicationsPage
from desktop_intelligence.ui.pages.automation import AutomationPage
from desktop_intelligence.ui.pages.settings import SettingsPage
from desktop_intelligence.ui.theme import generate_stylesheet, get_effective_theme


class DataLoaderWorker(QThread):
    """Background worker loading CSV data and calculating analytics off the UI thread."""
    data_loaded = Signal(object, object, object, object, object, str)

    def __init__(self, store: CsvStore, config: AppConfig, parent: Optional[QObject] = None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.store = store
        self.config = config

    def run(self) -> None:
        try:
            # 1. Read Sessions
            raw_sessions = self.store.read_dataframe("activity_sessions", self.store.paths.activity_sessions_csv)
            sessions_df = clean_sessions_df(raw_sessions)

            # 2. Read Samples
            raw_samples = self.store.read_dataframe("resource_samples", self.store.paths.resource_samples_csv)
            samples_df = clean_samples_df(raw_samples)

            # 3. Read Inventory
            raw_inv = self.store.read_rows("application_inventory", self.store.paths.application_inventory_csv)
            inventory_items = [InventoryItem.from_row(r) for r in raw_inv]

            # 4. Read Recommendations
            raw_recs = self.store.read_rows("recommendations", self.store.paths.recommendations_csv)
            recs = [RecommendationRecord.from_row(r) for r in raw_recs]

            # 5. Read Automation Logs
            raw_logs = self.store.read_rows("automation_log", self.store.paths.automation_log_csv)
            logs = [AutomationLogRecord.from_row(r) for r in raw_logs]

            # Pre-warm Windows reliability events cache in background worker thread
            try:
                query_recent_application_errors(max_records=30)
            except Exception:
                pass

            # Calculate actual data freshness from newest recorded activity
            freshness = "No data yet"
            max_dt = None
            if not sessions_df.empty:
                col = "start_ts_utc" if "start_ts_utc" in sessions_df.columns else ("start_time_utc" if "start_time_utc" in sessions_df.columns else None)
                if col:
                    try:
                        ts_series = pd.to_datetime(sessions_df[col], utc=True, errors="coerce").dropna()
                        if not ts_series.empty:
                            max_dt = ts_series.max().to_pydatetime()
                    except Exception:
                        pass

            if not samples_df.empty and "sample_ts_utc" in samples_df.columns:
                try:
                    ts_series2 = pd.to_datetime(samples_df["sample_ts_utc"], utc=True, errors="coerce").dropna()
                    if not ts_series2.empty:
                        dt2 = ts_series2.max().to_pydatetime()
                        if max_dt is None or dt2 > max_dt:
                            max_dt = dt2
                except Exception:
                    pass

            if max_dt:
                age_secs = (now_utc() - max_dt).total_seconds()
                if age_secs < 120:
                    freshness = "Up to date"
                elif age_secs < 3600:
                    freshness = f"{int(age_secs // 60)} min ago"
                elif age_secs < 86400:
                    freshness = f"{int(age_secs // 3600)} hrs ago"
                else:
                    freshness = "Stale (> 1d)"

            self.data_loaded.emit(sessions_df, samples_df, inventory_items, recs, logs, freshness)
        except Exception as exc:
            import logging
            logging.getLogger(__name__).exception("DataLoaderWorker error: %s", exc)
            self.data_loaded.emit(pd.DataFrame(), pd.DataFrame(), [], [], [], "No data yet")


class MainWindow(QMainWindow):
    """Desktop Intelligence Main Application Dashboard."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: AppConfig,
        paths: ResolvedPaths,
        store: CsvStore,
        health_provider: Optional[object] = None,
        parent: Optional[QWidget] = None,
    ):

        self.config = config
        self.paths = paths
        self.store = store
        self.health_provider = health_provider

        self._last_sessions_df: pd.DataFrame = pd.DataFrame()
        self._last_recs: list = []
        self._last_freshness: str = "Up to date"

        self.setWindowTitle("Desktop Intelligence")
        self.setMinimumSize(980, 660)
        self.resize(1120, 740)

        central_widget = QWidget()
        central_widget.setObjectName("centralWidget")
        self.setCentralWidget(central_widget)
        main_layout = QHBoxLayout(central_widget)
        main_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        main_layout.setSpacing(0)

        # Left Sidebar Navigation Container

        sidebar_widget.setObjectName("sidebar_container")

        sidebar_layout.setSpacing(0)

        footer_title.setStyleSheet("font-size: 10px; font-weight: 600; color: #64748B; letter-spacing: 0.5px;")

        self.status_hook = QLabel("● Foreground Hook: Active")
        self.status_hook.setStyleSheet("color: #34D399; font-size: 11px;")
        self.status_hook.setToolTip("Win32 SetWinEventHook foreground focus tracking: Active")

        self.status_process = QLabel("● Process Trace: Active")
        self.status_process.setStyleSheet("color: #34D399; font-size: 11px;")
        self.status_process.setToolTip("WMI / Win32_Process creation event listener: Operational")

        self.status_diag = QLabel("● Diagnostics: Ready")
        self.status_diag.setStyleSheet("color: #22D3EE; font-size: 11px;")
        self.status_diag.setToolTip("Windows Reliability & Error diagnostics: Operational")

        self.status_dot = self.status_hook

        self.page_apps = ApplicationsPage()

        self.page_settings = SettingsPage(
            config=self.config, paths=self.paths, on_save_config=self._handle_save_config
        )

        self.stack.addWidget(self.page_overview)
        self.stack.addWidget(self.page_usage)
        self.stack.addWidget(self.page_health)
        self.stack.addWidget(self.page_apps)
        self.stack.addWidget(self.page_automation)
        self.stack.addWidget(self.page_settings)

        # Single-flight refresh tracking
        self._active_worker: Optional[DataLoaderWorker] = None
        self._refresh_pending: bool = False
        self._is_quitting: bool = False

        # Setup System Tray
        self._setup_tray_icon()

        # Apply design system stylesheet and theme
        self.apply_theme(self.config.ui.theme, self.config.ui.accent_color)

        # Periodic refresh timer (every 10 seconds)
        self.refresh_timer = QTimer(self)
        self.refresh_timer.timeout.connect(self.trigger_refresh)
        self.refresh_timer.start(10000)

        # Initial data load
        self.trigger_refresh()

    def _setup_tray_icon(self) -> None:
        self.tray_icon = None
        if not getattr(self.config.ui, "show_tray_icon", True):
            return
        if not QSystemTrayIcon.isSystemTrayAvailable():
            return

        style = QApplication.style()
        icon = style.standardIcon(QStyle.StandardPixmap.SP_ComputerIcon)
        self.tray_icon = QSystemTrayIcon(icon, self)
        self.tray_icon.setToolTip("Desktop Intelligence")

        tray_menu = QMenu(self)
        show_action = QAction("Open Dashboard", self)
        show_action.triggered.connect(self._restore_from_tray)
        tray_menu.addAction(show_action)

        refresh_action = QAction("Refresh Data", self)
        refresh_action.triggered.connect(self.trigger_refresh)
        tray_menu.addAction(refresh_action)

        tray_menu.addSeparator()

        quit_action = QAction("Exit", self)
        quit_action.triggered.connect(self._quit_application)
        tray_menu.addAction(quit_action)

        self.tray_icon.setContextMenu(tray_menu)
        self.tray_icon.activated.connect(self._on_tray_activated)
        self.tray_icon.show()

    def _restore_from_tray(self) -> None:
        self.showNormal()
        self.activateWindow()
        self.raise_()

    def _on_tray_activated(self, reason) -> None:
        if reason in (QSystemTrayIcon.ActivationReason.Trigger, QSystemTrayIcon.ActivationReason.DoubleClick):
            if self.isVisible():
                if self.isMinimized():
                    self.showNormal()
                self.activateWindow()
                self.raise_()
            else:
                self._restore_from_tray()

    def _quit_application(self) -> None:
        self._is_quitting = True
        if self.tray_icon:
            self.tray_icon.hide()
        QApplication.quit()

    def closeEvent(self, event) -> None:
        if (
            getattr(self, "tray_icon", None)
            and self.tray_icon.isVisible()
            and not getattr(self, "_is_quitting", False)
        ):
            event.ignore()
            self.hide()
            self.tray_icon.showMessage(
                "Desktop Intelligence",
                "Application is still running in the background. Access it from the system tray.",
                QSystemTrayIcon.MessageIcon.Information,
                2000,
            )
        else:
            if hasattr(self, "refresh_timer"):
                self.refresh_timer.stop()
            event.accept()

    def _on_page_changed(self, index: int) -> None:
        self.stack.setCurrentIndex(index)

    def trigger_refresh(self) -> None:
        """Trigger background data load without blocking Qt thread with single-flight worker."""
        if getattr(self, "_active_worker", None) is not None and self._active_worker.isRunning():
            self._refresh_pending = True
            return

        self._refresh_pending = False
        self._active_worker = DataLoaderWorker(self.store, self.config, self)
        self.worker = self._active_worker  # Preserve for backwards-compatibility with tests inspecting self.worker
        self._active_worker.data_loaded.connect(self._on_data_loaded)
        self._active_worker.finished.connect(self._on_worker_finished)
        self._active_worker.finished.connect(self._active_worker.deleteLater)
        self._active_worker.start()

    def _on_worker_finished(self) -> None:
        self._active_worker = None
        if getattr(self, "_refresh_pending", False):
            self.trigger_refresh()

    def update_collector_health(self) -> str:
        """Update sidebar status indicators from real collector health and return collection status."""
        collection_status = "Operational (Active)"
        if self.health_provider:
            try:
                health_dict = self.health_provider() if callable(self.health_provider) else getattr(self.health_provider, "get_health_status", lambda: {})()
            except Exception:
                health_dict = {}

            if "foreground" in health_dict:
                h = health_dict["foreground"]
                st = getattr(h, "state", "stopped")
                msg = getattr(h, "message", "")
                if st == "active":
                    self.status_hook.setText("● Foreground Hook: Active")
                    self.status_hook.setStyleSheet("color: #34D399; font-size: 11px;")
                elif st == "degraded":
                    self.status_hook.setText("● Foreground Hook: Degraded")
                    self.status_hook.setStyleSheet("color: #FBBF24; font-size: 11px;")
                elif st == "failed":
                    self.status_hook.setText("● Foreground Hook: Failed")
                    self.status_hook.setStyleSheet("color: #FB7185; font-size: 11px;")
                elif st == "disabled":
                    self.status_hook.setText("● Foreground Hook: Disabled")
                    self.status_hook.setStyleSheet("color: #64748B; font-size: 11px;")
                else:
                    self.status_hook.setText(f"● Foreground Hook: {st.capitalize()}")
                    self.status_hook.setStyleSheet("color: #64748B; font-size: 11px;")
                self.status_hook.setToolTip(f"Foreground Hook: {msg}")

            if "process" in health_dict:
                h = health_dict["process"]
                st = getattr(h, "state", "stopped")
                msg = getattr(h, "message", "")
                if st == "active":
                    self.status_process.setText("● Process Trace: Active")
                    self.status_process.setStyleSheet("color: #34D399; font-size: 11px;")
                elif st == "degraded":
                    self.status_process.setText("● Process Trace: Degraded")
                    self.status_process.setStyleSheet("color: #FBBF24; font-size: 11px;")
                elif st in ("failed", "unavailable"):
                    self.status_process.setText(f"● Process Trace: {st.capitalize()}")
                    self.status_process.setStyleSheet("color: #FB7185; font-size: 11px;")
                elif st == "disabled":
                    self.status_process.setText("● Process Trace: Disabled")
                    self.status_process.setStyleSheet("color: #64748B; font-size: 11px;")
                else:
                    self.status_process.setText(f"● Process Trace: {st.capitalize()}")
                    self.status_process.setStyleSheet("color: #64748B; font-size: 11px;")
                self.status_process.setToolTip(f"Process Trace: {msg}")

            states = [getattr(h, "state", "stopped") for h in health_dict.values()]
            if "failed" in states or "unavailable" in states:
                collection_status = "Failed" if "failed" in states else "Unavailable"
            elif "degraded" in states:
                collection_status = "Degraded (Polling)"
            elif all(s == "active" for s in states if s != "disabled"):
                collection_status = "Operational (Active)"
            elif any(s == "stopped" for s in states):
                collection_status = "Stopped"

        return collection_status

    def _on_data_loaded(
        self,
        sessions_df: pd.DataFrame,
        samples_df: pd.DataFrame,
        inventory_items: list,
        recommendations: list,
        logs: list,
        freshness: str,
    ) -> None:
        """Dispatch loaded data to respective pages."""
        self._last_sessions_df = sessions_df
        self._last_recs = recommendations
        self._last_freshness = freshness

        collection_status = self.update_collector_health()

        # Check for system error events on Windows
        errors, diag = query_recent_application_errors(max_records=30)

        # Update Overview
        self.page_overview.update_data(
            sessions_df,
            recommendations,
            freshness_text=freshness,
            collection_status_text=collection_status,
            theme_mode=self.config.ui.theme,
            accent_color=self.config.ui.accent_color,
        )

        # Update Usage
        self.page_usage.update_data(
            sessions_df,
            theme_mode=self.config.ui.theme,
            accent_color=self.config.ui.accent_color,
        )

        # Update Health
        self.page_health.update_data(samples_df, errors, diag, self.config.resources)

        # Update Applications
        self.page_apps.update_data(inventory_items)

        # Update Automation
        self.page_automation.update_data(self.config.automation.enabled, recommendations, logs)

        # Update collector status indicators
        if hasattr(self, "status_diag"):
            if diag and (diag.startswith("Operational") or diag.startswith("No recent")):
                self.status_diag.setText("● Diagnostics: Ready")
                self.status_diag.setStyleSheet("color: #22D3EE; font-size: 11px;")
                self.status_diag.setToolTip(f"Windows Event Logs: {diag}")
            else:
                self.status_diag.setText("● Diagnostics: Standby")
                self.status_diag.setStyleSheet("color: #FBBF24; font-size: 11px;")
                self.status_diag.setToolTip(f"Windows Event Logs: {diag}")

    def apply_theme(self, theme_mode: Optional[str] = None, accent_color: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        """Apply theme mode and accent color dynamically to the window and child pages."""
        if theme_mode is not None:
            self.config.ui.theme = theme_mode
        if accent_color is not None:
            self.config.ui.accent_color = accent_color

        stylesheet = generate_stylesheet(self.config.ui.theme, self.config.ui.accent_color)
        self.setStyleSheet(stylesheet)

        # Refresh page visuals with the new theme
        if hasattr(self, "page_overview") and hasattr(self, "_last_sessions_df"):
            self.page_overview.update_data(
                self._last_sessions_df,
                self._last_recs,
                self._last_freshness,
                theme_mode=self.config.ui.theme,
                accent_color=self.config.ui.accent_color,
            )
        if hasattr(self, "page_usage") and hasattr(self, "_last_sessions_df"):
            self.page_usage.update_data(
                self._last_sessions_df,
                theme_mode=self.config.ui.theme,
                accent_color=self.config.ui.accent_color,
            )

    def _handle_recommendation_action(self, rec_id: str, new_status: str) -> None:
        """Update recommendation status in recommendations.csv."""
        raw_recs = self.store.read_rows("recommendations", self.paths.recommendations_csv)
        updated = False
        for r in raw_recs:
            if r.get("recommendation_id") == rec_id:
                r["status"] = new_status
                updated = True
                break

        if updated:
            from desktop_intelligence.models import SCHEMAS
            self.store.replace_all(
                "recommendations",
                self.paths.recommendations_csv,
                [[row_dict[c] for c in SCHEMAS["recommendations"]] for row_dict in raw_recs],
            )
            self.trigger_refresh()

    def _handle_toggle_automation(self, enabled: bool) -> None:
        self.config.automation.enabled = enabled
        self._persist_config()
        self.trigger_refresh()

    def _handle_save_config(self, new_cfg: AppConfig) -> None:
        self.config = new_cfg
        self.apply_theme(new_cfg.ui.theme, new_cfg.ui.accent_color)
        self._persist_config()
        self.trigger_refresh()

    def _persist_config(self) -> None:
        """Serialize configuration changes back to config.toml safely and atomically."""
        try:
            save_config(self.config, self.paths.config_file)
        except Exception as exc:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Save Error", f"Failed to persist config: {exc}")

    def _render_toml(self) -> str:
        lines = [
            "# Desktop Intelligence - Configuration File",

            f'data_dir = "{self.config.paths.data_dir}"',
            f'log_dir = "{self.config.paths.log_dir}"',
            "",
            "[collection]",
            f'enable_process_start_events = {str(self.config.collection.enable_process_start_events).lower()}',
            f'enable_foreground_events = {str(self.config.collection.enable_foreground_events).lower()}',
            f'enable_application_inventory = {str(self.config.collection.enable_application_inventory).lower()}',
            f'enable_system_error_events = {str(self.config.collection.enable_system_error_events).lower()}',
            f'idle_timeout_seconds = {self.config.collection.idle_timeout_seconds}',
            f'idle_check_interval_seconds = {self.config.collection.idle_check_interval_seconds}',
            f'resource_snapshot_on_process_start = {str(self.config.collection.resource_snapshot_on_process_start).lower()}',
            f'resource_snapshot_on_foreground_change = {str(self.config.collection.resource_snapshot_on_foreground_change).lower()}',
            f'collect_window_titles = {str(self.config.collection.collect_window_titles).lower()}',
            "",
            "[retention]",
            f'events_days = {self.config.retention.events_days}',
            f'sessions_days = {self.config.retention.sessions_days}',
            f'resource_samples_days = {self.config.retention.resource_samples_days}',
            "",
            "[patterns]",
            f'minimum_observation_days = {self.config.patterns.minimum_observation_days}',
            f'minimum_context_occurrences = {self.config.patterns.minimum_context_occurrences}',
            f'launch_frequency_threshold = {self.config.patterns.launch_frequency_threshold:.2f}',
            f'time_bucket_minutes = {self.config.patterns.time_bucket_minutes}',
            f'eligible_day_types = {list(self.config.patterns.eligible_day_types)!r}',
            "",
            "[resources]",
            f'high_memory_percent = {self.config.resources.high_memory_percent:.1f}',
            f'high_system_memory_percent = {self.config.resources.high_system_memory_percent:.1f}',
            f'high_cpu_percent = {self.config.resources.high_cpu_percent:.1f}',
            f'sustained_observation_count = {self.config.resources.sustained_observation_count}',
            "",
            "[automation]",
            f'enabled = {str(self.config.automation.enabled).lower()}',
            f'require_rule_approval = {str(self.config.automation.require_rule_approval).lower()}',
            f'prompt_timeout_seconds = {self.config.automation.prompt_timeout_seconds}',
            f'no_response_action = "{self.config.automation.no_response_action}"',
            f'prevent_duplicate_instances = {str(self.config.automation.prevent_duplicate_instances).lower()}',
            f'launch_missed_window_seconds = {self.config.automation.launch_missed_window_seconds}',
            "",
            "[ui]",
            f'theme = "{self.config.ui.theme}"',
            f'accent_color = "{self.config.ui.accent_color}"',
            f'start_minimized = {str(self.config.ui.start_minimized).lower()}',
            f'show_tray_icon = {str(self.config.ui.show_tray_icon).lower()}',
            "",
            "[llm]",
            f'enabled = {str(self.config.llm.enabled).lower()}',
            f'provider = "{self.config.llm.provider}"',
            f'share_raw_events = {str(self.config.llm.share_raw_events).lower()}',
        ]
        return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"

"""Theme and glassmorphism styling system for Desktop Intelligence.

Provides refined glassmorphic design tokens, layered translucent surfaces,
side-glow accent treatment, Windows system theme detection, and cohesive
Qt stylesheets for Light and Dark modes.
"""

import sys
from typing import Dict, Optional

ACCENT_PRESETS: Dict[str, str] = {
    "Indigo (Default)": "#635BFF",
    "Electric Violet": "#8B5CF6",
    "Cyan Glow": "#22D3EE",
    "Emerald": "#34D399",
    "Royal Blue": "#2563EB",
    "Amber Glow": "#FBBF24",
    "Rose": "#FB7185",
}

DEFAULT_DARK_ACCENT = "#8B5CF6"
DEFAULT_LIGHT_ACCENT = "#635BFF"


def get_contrast_text_color(hex_color: str) -> str:
    """Return '#FFFFFF' or '#0F172A' depending on luminance of hex_color for accessible WCAG AA contrast."""
    hex_clean = hex_color.lstrip("#")
    if len(hex_clean) == 6:
        try:
            r = int(hex_clean[0:2], 16) / 255.0
            g = int(hex_clean[2:4], 16) / 255.0
            b = int(hex_clean[4:6], 16) / 255.0
            lum = 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b
            return "#0F172A" if lum > 0.55 else "#FFFFFF"
        except Exception:
            pass
    return "#FFFFFF"


def is_system_dark_mode() -> bool:
    """Detect if Windows OS has dark mode enabled for applications."""
    if sys.platform != "win32":
        return False
    try:
        import winreg
        key = winreg.OpenKey(
            winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Themes\Personalize",
        )
        val, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(key, "AppsUseLightTheme")
        winreg.CloseKey(key)
        return val == 0
    except Exception:
        return False


def get_effective_theme(theme_setting: str) -> str:
    """Resolve 'system' to either 'light' or 'dark' based on OS preferences."""
    mode = str(theme_setting).lower().strip()
    if mode == "system":
        return "dark" if is_system_dark_mode() else "light"
    return "dark" if mode == "dark" else "light"


def get_theme_palette(theme_mode: str = "light", accent_color: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, str]:
    """Return dictionary of design tokens for the specified theme and accent."""
    effective = get_effective_theme(theme_mode)

    if effective == "dark":
        accent = accent_color or DEFAULT_DARK_ACCENT
        return {
            "mode": "dark",
            "app_bg": "#0A0D16",
            "depth_bg": "#0E1422",
            "surface_bg": "rgba(24, 31, 48, 0.72)",
            "surface_solid": "#141A29",
            "surface_elevated": "rgba(31, 40, 61, 0.82)",
            "surface_alt": "#1A2234",
            "glass_border": "rgba(180, 198, 240, 0.14)",
            "border": "#212A3D",
            "border_subtle": "rgba(180, 198, 240, 0.08)",
            "text_primary": "#F3F5FC",
            "text_secondary": "#A7B2C8",
            "text_muted": "#7F8BA5",
            "sidebar_bg": "#0C1220",
            "sidebar_border": "rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.22)",
            "sidebar_hover": "rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.12)",
            "sidebar_selected_bg": "qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:1, y2:0, stop:0 rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.28), stop:1 rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.08))",
            "sidebar_text": "#A7B2C8",
            "sidebar_text_selected": "#FFFFFF",
            "accent": accent,
            "accent_text": get_contrast_text_color(accent),
            "accent_glow": "rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.35)",
            "accent_hover": "#9D74FF",
            "cyan": "#22D3EE",
            "input_bg": "#0E1422",
            "input_border": "rgba(180, 198, 240, 0.18)",
            "table_header_bg": "#121826",
            "table_alt_bg": "rgba(18, 24, 38, 0.50)",
            "success": "#34D399",
            "warning": "#FBBF24",
            "error": "#FB7185",
        }
    else:
        accent = accent_color or DEFAULT_LIGHT_ACCENT
        return {
            "mode": "light",
            "app_bg": "#F3F6FC",
            "depth_bg": "#EAF0FA",
            "surface_bg": "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.78)",
            "surface_solid": "#FFFFFF",
            "surface_elevated": "#FFFFFF",
            "surface_alt": "#F1F5FB",
            "glass_border": "rgba(73, 89, 125, 0.16)",
            "border": "#E2E8F0",
            "border_subtle": "rgba(73, 89, 125, 0.08)",
            "text_primary": "#182033",
            "text_secondary": "#55627A",
            "text_muted": "#68758B",
            "sidebar_bg": "#0F172A",
            "sidebar_border": "rgba(105, 87, 232, 0.22)",
            "sidebar_hover": "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06)",
            "sidebar_selected_bg": "qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:1, y2:0, stop:0 rgba(105, 87, 232, 0.32), stop:1 rgba(8, 145, 178, 0.10))",
            "sidebar_text": "#94A3B8",
            "sidebar_text_selected": "#FFFFFF",
            "accent": accent,
            "accent_text": get_contrast_text_color(accent),
            "accent_glow": "rgba(105, 87, 232, 0.25)",
            "accent_hover": "#5B48DB",
            "cyan": "#0891B2",
            "input_bg": "#FFFFFF",
            "input_border": "#CBD5E1",
            "table_header_bg": "#F8FAFC",
            "table_alt_bg": "rgba(241, 245, 251, 0.60)",
            "success": "#059669",
            "warning": "#D97706",
            "error": "#DC2626",
        }

    """Generate a full Qt stylesheet implementing elegant glassmorphism and side glow."""
    p = get_theme_palette(theme_mode, accent_color)

    return f"""
    /* Global Window & Fonts */

def __getattr__(name):
    if name == "MainWindow":
        from desktop_intelligence.ui.main_window import MainWindow
        return MainWindow
    raise AttributeError(f"module {__name__!r} has no attribute {name!r}")

FILE: .\desktop_intelligence\ui\dialogs\consent_prompt.py

"""Interactive launch consent prompt dialog with countdown timer."""

from typing import Optional
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QDialog,
    QHBoxLayout,
    QLabel,
    QPushButton,
    QVBoxLayout,
    QWidget,
)

from desktop_intelligence.automation.rules import AutomationRule


class LaunchConsentDialog(QDialog):
    """Interactive modal consent dialog with a 10-second countdown."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        rule: AutomationRule,
        timeout_seconds: int = 10,
        parent: Optional[QWidget] = None,
    ):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.rule = rule
        self.remaining_seconds = max(1, timeout_seconds)
        self.user_choice = "timeout"

        self.setWindowTitle("Automation Launch Request")
        self.setMinimumWidth(380)
        self.setWindowFlags(self.windowFlags() | Qt.WindowStaysOnTopHint)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(20, 20, 20, 20)
        layout.setSpacing(14)

        header = QLabel("Scheduled Application Launch")
        header.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; font-size: 15px;")
        layout.addWidget(header)

        msg = QLabel(
            f"Desktop Intelligence is requesting approval to launch:\n\n"
            f"<b>{rule.app_name}</b>\n"
            f"<span style='color: #64748B; font-size: 11px;'>Scheduled: {rule.day_of_week} at {rule.time_bucket}<br>Target: {rule.exe_path}</span>"
        )
        msg.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(msg)

        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_layout.setSpacing(10)

        self.btn_skip = QPushButton(f"Skip ({self.remaining_seconds}s)")
        self.btn_skip.clicked.connect(self._on_skip)

        self.btn_launch = QPushButton("Launch Now")
        self.btn_launch.setStyleSheet("background-color: #635BFF; color: white; font-weight: 600; padding: 6px 14px; border-radius: 6px;")
        self.btn_launch.clicked.connect(self._on_launch)

        btn_layout.addStretch()
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_skip)
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_launch)
        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)

        # Countdown timer
        self.timer = QTimer(self)
        self.timer.setInterval(1000)
        self.timer.timeout.connect(self._on_tick)
        self.timer.start()

    def _on_tick(self) -> None:
        self.remaining_seconds -= 1
        if self.remaining_seconds <= 0:
            self.timer.stop()
            self.user_choice = "timeout"
            self.accept()
        else:
            self.btn_skip.setText(f"Skip ({self.remaining_seconds}s)")

    def _on_skip(self) -> None:
        self.timer.stop()
        self.user_choice = "reject"
        self.reject()

    def _on_launch(self) -> None:
        self.timer.stop()
        self.user_choice = "approve"
        self.accept()


def show_launch_consent_dialog(
    rule: AutomationRule,
    timeout_seconds: int = 10,
    parent: Optional[QWidget] = None,
) -> str:
    """Show interactive consent dialog and return 'approve', 'reject', or 'timeout'."""
    dlg = LaunchConsentDialog(rule, timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds, parent=parent)
    dlg.exec()
    return dlg.user_choice
