)
def test_config_validation_rules(patch, expected_err):
    with pytest.raises(ConfigValidationError) as exc:
        validate_config_dict(patch)
    assert expected_err in str(exc.value)


def test_utc_timestamp_handling():
    # Naive timestamp must be rejected
    naive_dt = datetime(2026, 10, 18, 14, 30, 0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError) as exc:
        format_utc_iso(naive_dt)
    assert "Naive datetime rejected" in str(exc.value)

    # Timezone aware
    aware_dt = datetime(2026, 10, 18, 14, 30, 0, 123456, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    iso_str = format_utc_iso(aware_dt)
    assert iso_str.endswith("Z")
    assert iso_str == "2026-10-18T14:30:00.123Z"

    parsed = parse_utc_iso(iso_str)
    assert parsed.tzinfo == timezone.utc
    assert parsed.year == 2026
    assert parsed.month == 10
    assert parsed.day == 18
    assert parsed.hour == 14
    assert parsed.minute == 30
    assert parsed.second == 0


def test_csv_store_initialization_and_single_header(tmp_path):
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    store = CsvStore(paths)

    now_ts = format_utc_iso(now_utc())
    evt1 = ActivityEvent(
        event_id="evt_1",
        event_ts_utc=now_ts,
        event_type="process_start",
        pid=1234,
        process_name="code.exe",
        exe_path="C:\\Program Files\\VSCode\\code.exe",
        event_source="wmi",
    )
    store.append_activity_event(evt1)

    # Verify header line + 1 data line
    content = paths.activity_events_csv.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    assert len(content) == 2
    assert content[0] == ",".join(SCHEMAS["activity_events"])

    # Second append: header must not be duplicated
    evt2 = ActivityEvent(
        event_id="evt_2",
        event_ts_utc=now_ts,
        event_type="foreground_change",
        pid=1234,
        process_name="code.exe",
        exe_path="C:\\Program Files\\VSCode\\code.exe",
        event_source="winevent",
    )
    store.append_activity_event(evt2)

    content2 = paths.activity_events_csv.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    assert len(content2) == 3
    assert content2[0] == ",".join(SCHEMAS["activity_events"])
    assert "evt_1" in content2[1]
    assert "evt_2" in content2[2]


def test_csv_special_characters_and_json_roundtrip(tmp_path):
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    store = CsvStore(paths)

    evidence_dict = {
        "launch_rate": 0.85,
        "sample_count": 12,
        "context": "weekday 09:00",
        "notes": 'Quotes "inside", commas, and\nnewline characters',
        "unicode": "測試 🚀 éàç",
    }
    evidence_json = json.dumps(evidence_dict)
    ts = format_utc_iso(now_utc())

    rec = RecommendationRecord(
        recommendation_id="rec_999",
        created_ts_utc=ts,
        category="habit_launch",
        subject='App with "Quotes" and, Commas',
        evidence_json=evidence_json,
        confidence=0.88,
        status="pending",
    )
    store.append_recommendation(rec)

    rows = store.read_rows("recommendations", paths.recommendations_csv)
    assert len(rows) == 1
    loaded = rows[0]
    assert loaded["recommendation_id"] == "rec_999"
    assert loaded["subject"] == 'App with "Quotes" and, Commas'
    assert loaded["confidence"] == "0.88"
    assert loaded["status"] == "pending"

    # Decode JSON and verify exact content round-tripped
    loaded_evidence = json.loads(loaded["evidence_json"])
    assert loaded_evidence == evidence_dict

    # Check Pandas read
    df = store.read_dataframe("recommendations", paths.recommendations_csv)
    assert len(df) == 1
    assert df.iloc[0]["recommendation_id"] == "rec_999"
    assert json.loads(df.iloc[0]["evidence_json"]) == evidence_dict


def test_schema_mismatch_error(tmp_path):
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    paths.ensure_directories()
    # Write a CSV file with bogus headers
    paths.activity_events_csv.write_text("col_a,col_b,col_c\n1,2,3\n", encoding="utf-8")

    store = CsvStore(paths)
    with pytest.raises(SchemaMismatchError) as exc_info:
        store.read_rows("activity_events", paths.activity_events_csv)
    assert "Schema mismatch" in str(exc_info.value)

    with pytest.raises(SchemaMismatchError):
        store.append_row("activity_events", paths.activity_events_csv, ["a"] * 7)


def test_corrupt_row_error(tmp_path):
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    store = CsvStore(paths)

    # Activity events has 7 columns, give 5
    with pytest.raises(CorruptRowError) as exc:
        store.append_row("activity_events", paths.activity_events_csv, ["1", "2", "3", "4", "5"])
    assert "does not match schema" in str(exc.value)


def test_concurrent_appends(tmp_path):
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    store = CsvStore(paths)

    total_threads = 8
    rows_per_thread = 25
    expected_total_rows = total_threads * rows_per_thread

    def worker(worker_id: int):
        for i in range(rows_per_thread):
            ts = format_utc_iso(now_utc())
            evt = ActivityEvent(
                event_id=f"evt_{worker_id}_{i}",
                event_ts_utc=ts,
                event_type="process_start",
                pid=1000 + worker_id,
                process_name=f"proc_{worker_id}.exe",
                exe_path=f"C:\\Path\\proc_{worker_id}.exe",
                event_source="fake",
            )
            store.append_activity_event(evt)

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=total_threads) as executor:
        futures = [executor.submit(worker, w) for w in range(total_threads)]
        for f in futures:
            f.result()

    rows = store.read_rows("activity_events", paths.activity_events_csv)
    assert len(rows) == expected_total_rows

    df = store.read_dataframe("activity_events", paths.activity_events_csv)
    assert len(df) == expected_total_rows
    assert len(set(df["event_id"])) == expected_total_rows


def test_retention_pruning(tmp_path):
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    store = CsvStore(paths)

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    old_ts = format_utc_iso(now - timedelta(days=200))
    fresh_ts = format_utc_iso(now - timedelta(days=10))

    # Append one old event and one fresh event
    store.append_activity_event(ActivityEvent(
        event_id="old_evt",
        event_ts_utc=old_ts,
        event_type="process_start",
        pid=100,
        process_name="old.exe",
        exe_path="C:\\old.exe",
        event_source="test"
    ))
    store.append_activity_event(ActivityEvent(
        event_id="fresh_evt",
        event_ts_utc=fresh_ts,
        event_type="process_start",
        pid=200,
        process_name="fresh.exe",
        exe_path="C:\\fresh.exe",
        event_source="test"
    ))

    # Prune with 180 days limit
    pruned = store.prune_retention(events_days=180, sessions_days=365, samples_days=90)
    assert pruned["events"] == 1

    remaining = store.read_rows("activity_events", paths.activity_events_csv)
    assert len(remaining) == 1
    assert remaining[0]["event_id"] == "fresh_evt"

FILE: .\tests\unit\test_stage1_wmi_resilience.py

"""Stage 1 tests: WMI process-start listener failure containment, fallback, and health reporting."""

import sys
import threading
import time
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
import pytest

from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import FakeClock, RawEvent
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.windows_process_events import WindowsProcessStartListener


def test_wmi_listener_access_denied_handled_gracefully():
    """Simulate WMI access denied on both kernel trace and standard query.

    Verify that no uncaught exception escapes, collector enters unavailable state,
    and status_message explains the failure.
    """
    received_events = []
    listener = WindowsProcessStartListener(callback=lambda e: received_events.append(e))

    class MockAccessDenied(Exception):
        pass

    mock_wmi_mod = MagicMock()
    mock_wmi_mod.x_access_denied = MockAccessDenied
    mock_wmi_mod.x_wmi_timed_out = TimeoutError

    mock_client = MagicMock()
    mock_client.Win32_ProcessStartTrace.watch_for.side_effect = MockAccessDenied("Access denied to ETW trace")
    mock_client.watch_for.side_effect = MockAccessDenied("Access denied to Win32_Process creation")
    mock_wmi_mod.WMI.return_value = mock_client

    mock_pythoncom = MagicMock()

    with patch.dict(sys.modules, {"wmi": mock_wmi_mod, "pythoncom": mock_pythoncom}):
        # Direct execution of worker
        listener._running = True
        listener._run_wmi_listener()

        # Must not raise, running must become False, health_state must be unavailable
        assert listener.is_running is False
        assert listener.health_state == "unavailable"
        assert "access denied" in listener.status_message.lower() or "restricted" in listener.status_message.lower()


def test_wmi_listener_fallback_to_standard_query_on_trace_access_denied():
    """When Win32_ProcessStartTrace fails with access denied, fall back to watch_for('Creation', 'Win32_Process')."""
    received_events = []
    listener = WindowsProcessStartListener(callback=lambda e: received_events.append(e))

    class MockAccessDenied(Exception):
        pass

    class MockTimedOut(Exception):
        pass

    mock_wmi_mod = MagicMock()
    mock_wmi_mod.x_access_denied = MockAccessDenied
    mock_wmi_mod.x_wmi_timed_out = MockTimedOut

    # Mock event object returned by fallback watcher
    mock_event = MagicMock()
    mock_event.ProcessId = 1234
    mock_event.Caption = "notepad.exe"

    mock_fallback_watcher = MagicMock()
    # Return mock event on first call, then stop listener and time out
    def watcher_side_effect(timeout_ms=1000):
        listener._running = False
        return mock_event

    mock_fallback_watcher.side_effect = watcher_side_effect

    mock_client = MagicMock()
    mock_client.Win32_ProcessStartTrace.watch_for.side_effect = MockAccessDenied("Trace denied")
    mock_client.watch_for.return_value = mock_fallback_watcher
    mock_wmi_mod.WMI.return_value = mock_client

    mock_pythoncom = MagicMock()

    with patch.dict(sys.modules, {"wmi": mock_wmi_mod, "pythoncom": mock_pythoncom}):
        listener._running = True
        listener._run_wmi_listener()

        assert len(received_events) == 1
        assert received_events[0].pid == 1234
        assert received_events[0].process_name == "notepad.exe"
        assert received_events[0].event_type == "process_start"


def test_wmi_listener_missing_module():
    """When wmi module cannot be imported, listener enters unavailable state cleanly."""
    listener = WindowsProcessStartListener(callback=lambda e: None)

    with patch.dict(sys.modules, {"wmi": None, "pythoncom": None}):
        listener._running = True
        listener._run_wmi_listener()

        assert listener.is_running is False
        assert listener.health_state == "unavailable"
        assert "not installed" in listener.status_message.lower()


def test_wmi_listener_multiple_stops_are_safe():
    """Stopping multiple times must be safe and idempotent."""
    listener = WindowsProcessStartListener(callback=lambda e: None)
    listener.stop()
    listener.stop()
    assert listener.is_running is False
    assert listener.health_state == "stopped"

"""Stage 2 tests: Event-driven application activity and foreground session capture."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import time
import pytest

from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import FakeClock, RawEvent
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.fake_event_source import FakeEventSource
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.foreground_events import (
    WindowsForegroundHook,
    get_process_info_from_pid,
)
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.activity_manager import ActivityManager
from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig
from desktop_intelligence.paths import resolve_paths
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CsvStore


@pytest.fixture
def test_env(tmp_path):
    paths = resolve_paths(
        data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"),
        log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"),
        config_file_override=tmp_path / "config.toml",
    )
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()
    config.general.timezone = "UTC"
    config.collection.idle_timeout_seconds = 300
    config.collection.idle_check_interval_seconds = 1
    base_time = datetime(2026, 10, 18, 10, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    clock = FakeClock(base_time)
    return config, store, clock, paths


def test_process_start_creates_single_event_record(test_env):
    config, store, clock, paths = test_env
    mgr = ActivityManager(config=config, store=store, clock=clock)
    mgr.start()

    t0 = clock.now_utc()
    mgr.on_event(
        RawEvent(
            event_type="process_start",
            pid=5555,
            process_name="calculator.exe",
            exe_path="C:\\Windows\\calculator.exe",
            timestamp_utc=t0,
            event_source="wmi",
        )
    )

    mgr.stop()

    # Verify activity_events has 1 row
    events = store.read_rows("activity_events", paths.activity_events_csv)
    assert len(events) == 1
    assert events[0]["event_type"] == "process_start"
    assert events[0]["process_name"] == "calculator.exe"
    assert events[0]["pid"] == "5555"

    # Verify activity_sessions has 0 rows (process_start does not start a session)
    sessions = store.read_rows("activity_sessions", paths.activity_sessions_csv)
    assert len(sessions) == 0


def test_repeated_duplicate_foreground_events_idempotence(test_env):
    config, store, clock, paths = test_env
    mgr = ActivityManager(config=config, store=store, clock=clock)
    mgr.start()

    t0 = clock.now_utc()
    # Emits foreground change for same app at T=0, T=5, T=10
    mgr.on_event(
        RawEvent(
            event_type="foreground_change",
            pid=1000,
            process_name="code.exe",
            exe_path="C:\\code.exe",
            timestamp_utc=t0,
            event_source="winevent",
        )
    )
    clock.advance(5)
    mgr.on_event(
        RawEvent(
            event_type="foreground_change",
            pid=1000,
            process_name="code.exe",
            exe_path="C:\\code.exe",
            timestamp_utc=clock.now_utc(),
            event_source="winevent",
        )
    )
    clock.advance(5)
    mgr.on_event(
        RawEvent(
            event_type="foreground_change",
            pid=1000,
            process_name="code.exe",
            exe_path="C:\\code.exe",
            timestamp_utc=clock.now_utc(),
            event_source="winevent",
        )
    )

    # Now switch to browser at T=30
    clock.advance(20)
    mgr.on_event(
        RawEvent(
            event_type="foreground_change",
            pid=2000,
            process_name="browser.exe",
            exe_path="C:\\browser.exe",
            timestamp_utc=clock.now_utc(),
            event_source="winevent",
        )
    )

    mgr.stop()

    sessions = store.read_rows("activity_sessions", paths.activity_sessions_csv)
    # Must have code.exe session of 30.0 seconds, NOT multiple 0 or 5s sessions
    code_sessions = [s for s in sessions if s["process_name"] == "code.exe"]
    assert len(code_sessions) == 1
    assert float(code_sessions[0]["duration_seconds"]) == pytest.approx(30.0, abs=0.1)


def test_sequence_A_B_A_creates_correct_bounded_sessions(test_env):
    config, store, clock, paths = test_env
    mgr = ActivityManager(config=config, store=store, clock=clock)
    mgr.start()

    # T=0: App A
    t0 = clock.now_utc()
    mgr.on_event(RawEvent("foreground_change", 100, "appA.exe", "C:\\appA.exe", t0, "winevent"))

    # T=100: App B
    clock.advance(100)
    t1 = clock.now_utc()
    mgr.on_event(RawEvent("foreground_change", 200, "appB.exe", "C:\\appB.exe", t1, "winevent"))

    # T=160: App A again
    clock.advance(60)
    t2 = clock.now_utc()
    mgr.on_event(RawEvent("foreground_change", 100, "appA.exe", "C:\\appA.exe", t2, "winevent"))

    # T=200: Shutdown
    clock.advance(40)
    mgr.stop()

    sessions = store.read_rows("activity_sessions", paths.activity_sessions_csv)
    assert len(sessions) == 3

    assert sessions[0]["process_name"] == "appA.exe"
    assert float(sessions[0]["duration_seconds"]) == pytest.approx(100.0, abs=0.1)

    assert sessions[1]["process_name"] == "appB.exe"
    assert float(sessions[1]["duration_seconds"]) == pytest.approx(60.0, abs=0.1)

    assert sessions[2]["process_name"] == "appA.exe"
    assert float(sessions[2]["duration_seconds"]) == pytest.approx(40.0, abs=0.1)

    # Check non-overlapping boundaries
    assert sessions[0]["end_ts_utc"] == sessions[1]["start_ts_utc"]
    assert sessions[1]["end_ts_utc"] == sessions[2]["start_ts_utc"]


def test_switching_to_desktop_or_lockapp_closes_session(test_env):
    config, store, clock, paths = test_env
    mgr = ActivityManager(config=config, store=store, clock=clock)
    mgr.start()

    # Focus notepad
    mgr.on_event(RawEvent("foreground_change", 300, "notepad.exe", "C:\\notepad.exe", clock.now_utc(), "winevent"))
    clock.advance(45)

    # Windows LockApp screen takes focus
    mgr.on_event(RawEvent("foreground_change", 400, "LockApp.exe", "C:\\LockApp.exe", clock.now_utc(), "winevent"))
    clock.advance(300)

    mgr.stop()

    sessions = store.read_rows("activity_sessions", paths.activity_sessions_csv)
    # Only notepad session should be recorded; LockApp is excluded
    assert len(sessions) == 1
    assert sessions[0]["process_name"] == "notepad.exe"
    assert float(sessions[0]["duration_seconds"]) == pytest.approx(45.0, abs=0.1)


def test_midnight_crossing_session_split(test_env):
    config, store, clock, paths = test_env
    # Set time to 2026-10-18 23:45:00 UTC
    clock.set_time(datetime(2026, 10, 18, 23, 45, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    mgr = ActivityManager(config=config, store=store, clock=clock)
    mgr.start()

    # Start long coding session
    mgr.on_event(RawEvent("foreground_change", 500, "editor.exe", "C:\\editor.exe", clock.now_utc(), "winevent"))

    # Advance 45 minutes across midnight to 2026-10-19 00:30:00 UTC
    clock.advance(45 * 60)
    mgr.on_event(RawEvent("foreground_change", 600, "browser.exe", "C:\\browser.exe", clock.now_utc(), "winevent"))

    mgr.stop()

    sessions = store.read_rows("activity_sessions", paths.activity_sessions_csv)
    editor_slices = [s for s in sessions if s["process_name"] == "editor.exe"]

    assert len(editor_slices) == 2, "Session crossing midnight must be split into two daily slices"

    # Slice 1: 23:45 to 00:00 (15 min = 900s) on 2026-10-18
    assert editor_slices[0]["local_date"] == "2026-10-18"
    assert float(editor_slices[0]["duration_seconds"]) == pytest.approx(900.0, abs=1.0)
    assert editor_slices[0]["time_bucket"] == "23:00"

    # Slice 2: 00:00 to 00:30 (30 min = 1800s) on 2026-10-19
    assert editor_slices[1]["local_date"] == "2026-10-19"
    assert float(editor_slices[1]["duration_seconds"]) == pytest.approx(1800.0, abs=1.0)
    assert editor_slices[1]["time_bucket"] == "00:00"


def test_inaccessible_process_and_missing_metadata():
    # Negative PID or nonexistent PID returns ("unknown", "") without raising exception
    name, exe = get_process_info_from_pid(-1)
    assert name == "unknown"
    assert exe == ""

    name2, exe2 = get_process_info_from_pid(99999999)
    assert name2 == "unknown"


def test_no_high_frequency_process_enumeration():
    cfg = AppConfig()
    # Configuration guarantees low-frequency idle check (default 60s)
    assert cfg.collection.idle_check_interval_seconds >= 10
    assert cfg.collection.idle_check_interval_seconds == 60


@pytest.mark.skipif(sys.platform != "win32", reason="Windows foreground hook requires Windows OS")
def test_windows_native_foreground_hook_lifecycle():
    events = []
    hook = WindowsForegroundHook(callback=lambda e: events.append(e))
    assert not hook.is_running
    hook.start()
    assert hook.is_running
    time.sleep(0.1)
    hook.stop()
    assert not hook.is_running

"""Stage 2 tests: Honest collector health model, status indicators, and freshness tracking."""

import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import pytest
import pandas as pd
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication

from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import BaseEventSource, CollectorHealth, RawEvent
from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig
from desktop_intelligence.paths import resolve_paths
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CsvStore
from desktop_intelligence.ui.main_window import MainWindow


class DummySource(BaseEventSource):
    def __init__(self, name: str, state: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(lambda e: None, name=name)
        self.health_state = state
        self.status_message = message

    def start(self):
        pass

    def stop(self):
        pass


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "state,expected_label,expected_color",
    [
        ("active", "Active", "#34D399"),
        ("degraded", "Degraded", "#FBBF24"),
        ("failed", "Failed", "#FB7185"),
        ("stopped", "Stopped", "#64748B"),
        ("disabled", "Disabled", "#64748B"),
    ],
)
def test_collector_health_states_mapping(qapp, tmp_path, state, expected_label, expected_color):
    """Verify that collector health states map directly to truthful sidebar labels and badge colors."""
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()

    dummy_fg = DummySource("Foreground Hook", state, f"Foreground is {state}")
    dummy_proc = DummySource("Process Trace", state, f"Process is {state}")

    def health_provider():
        return {
            "foreground": dummy_fg.health(),
            "process": dummy_proc.health(),
        }

    window = MainWindow(config=config, paths=paths, store=store, health_provider=health_provider)

    status = window.update_collector_health()

    assert expected_label in window.status_hook.text()
    assert expected_color in window.status_hook.styleSheet()
    assert expected_label in window.status_process.text()
    assert expected_color in window.status_process.styleSheet()
    window.close()


def test_process_listener_fallback_sets_degraded(monkeypatch):
    """Verify that when kernel trace raises access denied, listener falls back to degraded polling."""
    from desktop_intelligence.collectors.windows_process_events import WindowsProcessStartListener
    import types

    fake_wmi = types.ModuleType("wmi")
    fake_pythoncom = types.ModuleType("pythoncom")
    fake_pythoncom.CoInitialize = lambda: None
    fake_pythoncom.CoUninitialize = lambda: None

    class FakeAccessDenied(Exception):
        pass

    fake_wmi.x_access_denied = FakeAccessDenied
    fake_wmi.x_wmi_timed_out = TimeoutError

    class FakeWmiClient:
        def __init__(self):
            self.Win32_ProcessStartTrace = self

        def watch_for(self, *args, **kwargs):
            if "creation" in args or kwargs.get("notification_type") == "creation":
                # Simulate Access Denied on trace
                raise FakeAccessDenied("COM access denied")
            # Fallback watcher succeeds
            return lambda timeout_ms=1000: None

    fake_wmi.WMI = lambda: FakeWmiClient()

    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "wmi", fake_wmi)
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "pythoncom", fake_pythoncom)
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, "platform", "win32")

    listener = WindowsProcessStartListener(callback=lambda e: None)
    # Run the listener thread body
    listener._running = True
    listener._stop_event.set()  # Stop immediately after init
    listener._run_wmi_listener()

    assert listener.health_state == "degraded"
    assert "degraded" in listener.status_message.lower()


def test_process_listener_total_failure_sets_failed(monkeypatch):
    """Verify that when both trace and polling fail, listener state is failed."""
    from desktop_intelligence.collectors.windows_process_events import WindowsProcessStartListener
    import types

    fake_wmi = types.ModuleType("wmi")
    fake_pythoncom = types.ModuleType("pythoncom")
    fake_pythoncom.CoInitialize = lambda: None
    fake_pythoncom.CoUninitialize = lambda: None

    class FakeAccessDenied(Exception):
        pass

    fake_wmi.x_access_denied = FakeAccessDenied
    fake_wmi.x_wmi_timed_out = TimeoutError

    class BrokenWmiClient:
        def __init__(self):
            self.Win32_ProcessStartTrace = self

        def watch_for(self, *args, **kwargs):
            raise RuntimeError("WMI service unavailable")

    fake_wmi.WMI = lambda: BrokenWmiClient()

    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "wmi", fake_wmi)
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "pythoncom", fake_pythoncom)
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, "platform", "win32")

    listener = WindowsProcessStartListener(callback=lambda e: None)
    listener._running = True
    listener._stop_event.set()
    listener._run_wmi_listener()

    assert listener.health_state in ("failed", "unavailable")
    assert "failed" in listener.status_message.lower() or "restricted" in listener.status_message.lower()


def test_stale_data_freshness_text(qapp, tmp_path):
    """Verify that old session data renders honest stale / relative freshness times."""
    from desktop_intelligence.models import ActivitySession, format_utc_iso
    from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
    from desktop_intelligence.ui.main_window import DataLoaderWorker

    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()

    # Append session from 3 hours ago
    past_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=3)
    ts_str = format_utc_iso(past_time)
    store.append_activity_session(ActivitySession(
        "s_old", "code.exe", 100, ts_str, ts_str, 3600.0, 0.0, "2026-10-19", "Monday", "10:00"
    ))

    worker = DataLoaderWorker(store, config)
    captured = []
    worker.data_loaded.connect(lambda s, sm, i, r, l, f: captured.append(f))
    worker.run()

    assert len(captured) == 1
    freshness = captured[0]
    assert "hrs ago" in freshness or "min ago" in freshness or "Stale" in freshness
    assert freshness != "Up to date"


def test_no_hardcoded_active_status_in_overview_page():
    """Verify OverviewPage does not hardcode 'Collection Status: Active'."""
    page_file = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "desktop_intelligence" / "ui" / "pages" / "overview.py"
    content = page_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert 'Collection Status: Active' not in content, (
        "OverviewPage still contains hardcoded 'Collection Status: Active' string!"
    )

FILE: .\tests\unit\test_stage2_overview_lifecycle.py
================================================================================

"""Stage 2 regression tests: Overview page recommendation widget lifecycle across repeated refreshes."""

import os
import sys
import pandas as pd
import pytest

os.environ["QT_QPA_PLATFORM"] = "offscreen"
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication

from desktop_intelligence.models import RecommendationRecord, format_utc_iso, now_utc
from desktop_intelligence.ui.pages.overview import OverviewPage, RecommendationWidget


@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
def qapp():
    app = QApplication.instance()
    if app is None:
        app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    yield app


def make_rec(rec_id: str, subject: str, status: str = "pending") -> RecommendationRecord:
    return RecommendationRecord(
        recommendation_id=rec_id,
        created_ts_utc=format_utc_iso(now_utc()),
        category="habit_launch",
        subject=subject,
        evidence_json='{"app_name": "app.exe", "frequency_percent": 85.0}',
        confidence=0.85,
        status=status,
    )


def test_overview_page_repeated_refreshes_across_all_transitions(qapp):
    """Test the 8 transitions to prove no RuntimeError or deleted-object crashes occur."""
    page = OverviewPage()
    page.show()
    empty_df = pd.DataFrame()

    # Transition 1: Empty -> Empty
    page.update_data(empty_df, [])
    assert page.empty_rec_label.isVisible()

    page.update_data(empty_df, [])
    assert page.empty_rec_label.isVisible()

    # Transition 2: Empty -> Populated
    rec1 = make_rec("rec1", "Launch App 1")
    rec2 = make_rec("rec2", "Launch App 2")
    page.update_data(empty_df, [rec1, rec2])
    assert not page.empty_rec_label.isVisible()
    rec_widgets = [page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget() for i in range(page.rec_layout.count()) if isinstance(page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget(), RecommendationWidget)]
    assert len(rec_widgets) == 2

    # Transition 3: Populated -> Empty
    page.update_data(empty_df, [])
    assert page.empty_rec_label.isVisible()
    rec_widgets = [page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget() for i in range(page.rec_layout.count()) if isinstance(page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget(), RecommendationWidget)]
    assert len(rec_widgets) == 0

    # Transition 4: One recommendation -> Several recommendations
    rec3 = make_rec("rec3", "Launch App 3")
    page.update_data(empty_df, [rec3])
    assert not page.empty_rec_label.isVisible()
    rec_widgets = [page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget() for i in range(page.rec_layout.count()) if isinstance(page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget(), RecommendationWidget)]
    assert len(rec_widgets) == 1

    rec4 = make_rec("rec4", "Launch App 4")
    rec5 = make_rec("rec5", "Launch App 5")
    page.update_data(empty_df, [rec3, rec4, rec5])
    assert not page.empty_rec_label.isVisible()
    rec_widgets = [page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget() for i in range(page.rec_layout.count()) if isinstance(page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget(), RecommendationWidget)]
    assert len(rec_widgets) == 3

    # Transition 5: Several recommendations -> One recommendation
    page.update_data(empty_df, [rec5])
    assert not page.empty_rec_label.isVisible()
    rec_widgets = [page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget() for i in range(page.rec_layout.count()) if isinstance(page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget(), RecommendationWidget)]
    assert len(rec_widgets) == 1

    # Transition 6: Repeated refreshes with unchanged data
    for _ in range(5):
        page.update_data(empty_df, [rec5])
        assert not page.empty_rec_label.isVisible()
        rec_widgets = [page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget() for i in range(page.rec_layout.count()) if isinstance(page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget(), RecommendationWidget)]
        assert len(rec_widgets) == 1

    # Transition 7: Empty data arriving after populated data
    page.update_data(empty_df, [])
    assert page.empty_rec_label.isVisible()
    rec_widgets = [page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget() for i in range(page.rec_layout.count()) if isinstance(page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget(), RecommendationWidget)]
    assert len(rec_widgets) == 0

    # Transition 8: Recommendation approved/dismissed (status != 'pending')
    approved_rec = make_rec("rec5", "Launch App 5", status="approved")
    page.update_data(empty_df, [approved_rec])
    # Since approved_rec is not pending, empty label should be shown
    assert page.empty_rec_label.isVisible()
    rec_widgets = [page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget() for i in range(page.rec_layout.count()) if isinstance(page.rec_layout.itemAt(i).widget(), RecommendationWidget)]
    assert len(rec_widgets) == 0

"""Regression tests for Stage 3: Chart edge cases, singular transformation prevention, and theming."""

import warnings
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from desktop_intelligence.visualization.charts import (
    create_empty_figure,
    create_top_apps_chart,
    create_daily_trend_chart,
    create_day_of_week_chart,
    create_hourly_heatmap_chart,
    get_chart_theme_colors,
)


def test_chart_theme_colors():
    light_colors = get_chart_theme_colors("light", "#635BFF")
    assert light_colors["fig_bg"] == "#FFFFFF"
    assert light_colors["accent"] == "#635BFF"

    dark_colors = get_chart_theme_colors("dark", "#10B981")
    assert dark_colors["fig_bg"] == "#0E1422"
    assert dark_colors["accent"] == "#10B981"


def test_top_apps_chart_zero_hours_no_singular_warning():
    """Verify that all-zero hours do not produce identical xlims or singular warnings."""
    zero_df = pd.DataFrame({
        "process_name": ["code.exe", "slack.exe", "chrome.exe"],
        "active_hours": [0.0, 0.0, 0.0],
        "total_hours": [0.0, 0.0, 0.0],
    })

    with warnings.catch_warnings(record=True) as recorded_warnings:
        warnings.simplefilter("always")
        fig = create_top_apps_chart(zero_df, theme_mode="dark", accent_color="#8B83FF")

    singular_warnings = [w for w in recorded_warnings if "singular" in str(w.message).lower()]
    assert len(singular_warnings) == 0, f"Unexpected singular warning: {singular_warnings}"

    ax = fig.axes[0]
    xlim = ax.get_xlim()
    assert xlim[0] == 0.0
    assert xlim[1] >= 1.0
    assert xlim[1] > xlim[0]


def test_top_apps_chart_nan_and_empty():
    """Verify handling of empty or NaN values."""
    empty_df = pd.DataFrame()
    fig_empty = create_top_apps_chart(empty_df)
    assert len(fig_empty.axes) == 1

    none_fig = create_top_apps_chart(None)
    assert len(none_fig.axes) == 1

    nan_df = pd.DataFrame({
        "process_name": ["app1.exe", "app2.exe"],
        "active_hours": [np.nan, None],
    })
    with warnings.catch_warnings(record=True) as recorded_warnings:
        warnings.simplefilter("always")
        fig_nan = create_top_apps_chart(nan_df)
    singular_warnings = [w for w in recorded_warnings if "singular" in str(w.message).lower()]
    assert len(singular_warnings) == 0


def test_daily_trend_chart_zero_values_no_warning():
    """Verify daily trend chart with zero or empty hours."""
    daily_zero = pd.DataFrame({
        "local_date": ["2026-10-17", "2026-10-18"],
        "active_hours": [0.0, 0.0],
        "idle_hours": [0.0, 0.0],
    })
    with warnings.catch_warnings(record=True) as recorded_warnings:
        warnings.simplefilter("always")
        fig = create_daily_trend_chart(daily_zero, theme_mode="dark")
    singular_warnings = [w for w in recorded_warnings if "singular" in str(w.message).lower()]
    assert len(singular_warnings) == 0

    ax = fig.axes[0]
    ylim = ax.get_ylim()
    assert ylim[0] == 0.0
    assert ylim[1] >= 1.0


def test_day_of_week_chart_zero_and_theming():
    dow_zero = pd.DataFrame({
        "day_of_week": ["Monday", "Tuesday", "Wednesday"],
        "active_hours": [0.0, 0.0, 0.0],
    })
    with warnings.catch_warnings(record=True) as recorded_warnings:
        warnings.simplefilter("always")
        fig = create_day_of_week_chart(dow_zero, theme_mode="light", accent_color="#2563EB")
    singular_warnings = [w for w in recorded_warnings if "singular" in str(w.message).lower()]
    assert len(singular_warnings) == 0

    ax = fig.axes[0]
    assert ax.get_ylim()[1] >= 1.0


def test_hourly_heatmap_robustness():
    # All zeros matrix
    zeros_mat = np.zeros((7, 24))
    fig_zeros = create_hourly_heatmap_chart(zeros_mat)
    assert len(fig_zeros.axes) == 1  # Empty figure fallback

    # Valid matrix with some NaNs and Infs
    mat = np.ones((7, 24)) * 5.0
    mat[0, 0] = np.nan
    mat[1, 1] = np.inf
    fig_valid = create_hourly_heatmap_chart(mat, theme_mode="dark", accent_color="#8B83FF")
    assert len(fig_valid.axes) == 2  # Plot axis + Colorbar


def test_themed_chart_canvas_geometry_synchronization():
    """Verify ThemedChartCanvas binds canvas and synchronizes geometry to eliminate white rectangles."""
    import os
    import sys
    os.environ["QT_QPA_PLATFORM"] = "offscreen"
    from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
    app = QApplication.instance() or QApplication(sys.argv)

    from desktop_intelligence.visualization.charts import ThemedChartCanvas

    canvas = ThemedChartCanvas()
    canvas.resize(700, 350)

    # Initial figure has canvas bound
    assert canvas.figure.canvas is canvas

    # Update with new figure
    new_fig = create_top_apps_chart(
        pd.DataFrame({"process_name": ["app.exe"], "active_hours": [2.5]}),
        theme_mode="dark",
    )
    canvas.update_chart_figure(new_fig)

    assert canvas.figure is new_fig
    assert new_fig.canvas is canvas
    # Verify figure size in inches multiplied by dpi equals widget geometry
    px_size = new_fig.get_size_inches() * new_fig.dpi
    assert abs(px_size[0] - 700) < 2
    assert abs(px_size[1] - 350) < 2


def test_system_theme_resolution_matches_effective_mode(monkeypatch):
    """Verify that get_chart_theme_colors('system') canonicalizes to dark or light matching OS preference."""
    import desktop_intelligence.ui.theme as theme_mod

    # Case 1: Windows system reports dark mode
    monkeypatch.setattr(theme_mod, "is_system_dark_mode", lambda: True)
    dark_resolved = get_chart_theme_colors("system", "#8B5CF6")
    assert dark_resolved["fig_bg"] == "#0E1422"
    assert dark_resolved["text"] == "#F3F5FC"

    # Case 2: Windows system reports light mode
    monkeypatch.setattr(theme_mod, "is_system_dark_mode", lambda: False)
    light_resolved = get_chart_theme_colors("system", "#6957E8")
    assert light_resolved["fig_bg"] == "#FFFFFF"
    assert light_resolved["text"] == "#182033"


def test_themed_chart_canvas_multiple_resolutions():
    """Verify ThemedChartCanvas scales cleanly across standard window resolutions (1600x900, 1366x768, 1280x720)."""
    import os
    import sys
    os.environ["QT_QPA_PLATFORM"] = "offscreen"
    from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
    from PySide6.QtCore import QSize
    from PySide6.QtGui import QResizeEvent
    app = QApplication.instance() or QApplication(sys.argv)

    from desktop_intelligence.visualization.charts import ThemedChartCanvas

    canvas = ThemedChartCanvas()
    resolutions = [(800, 320), (680, 280), (600, 250)]

    for w, h in resolutions:
        canvas.resize(w, h)
        # Simulate Qt resizeEvent
        canvas.resizeEvent(QResizeEvent(QSize(w, h), QSize(100, 100)))
        fig_px = canvas.figure.get_size_inches() * canvas.figure.dpi
        assert abs(fig_px[0] - w) < 2
        assert abs(fig_px[1] - h) < 2

"""Tests for Stage 3: Collector correctness, idle calculations, and session lifecycle."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import pytest

from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import FakeClock, idle_seconds, RawEvent
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.activity_manager import ActivityManager
from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig
from desktop_intelligence.models import format_utc_iso
from desktop_intelligence.paths import resolve_paths
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CsvStore


def test_idle_seconds_wrap_around():
    """Verify idle calculation handles 32-bit tick count overflow gracefully."""
    # Normal case: 5000 ms = 5.0 seconds
    assert idle_seconds(10000, 5000) == 5.0

    # Wrap-around case: tick has wrapped to 100, last input was near 2^32 - 1
    # 0xFFFFFF00 is 4,294,967,040. Unsigned difference to 100 is 356 ms.
    tick = 100
    last_input = 0xFFFFFF00
    res = idle_seconds(tick, last_input)
    assert res == pytest.approx(0.356, rel=1e-3)
    assert res >= 0.0


def test_activity_manager_casefolds_process_names(tmp_path):
    """Verify process names are casefolded so disparate cases don't produce duplicate entries."""
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()

    start_time = datetime(2026, 10, 19, 10, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    clock = FakeClock(start_time)

    manager = ActivityManager(config=config, store=store, clock=clock)
    manager._running = True

    # Transition to uppercase Code.EXE
    manager.on_event(RawEvent(
        event_type="foreground_change",
        pid=1001,
        process_name="Code.EXE",
        exe_path="C:\\Apps\\Code.exe",
        timestamp_utc=start_time,
        event_source="winevent",
    ))
    # Process event queue synchronously
    while not manager.event_queue.empty():
        item = manager.event_queue.get_nowait()
        manager._handle_raw_event(item)

    assert manager.active_session is not None
    assert manager.active_session.process_name == "Code.EXE"

    # Switching case for same PID should not split session (case-insensitive matching)
    clock.advance(30)
    manager.on_event(RawEvent(
        event_type="foreground_change",
        pid=1001,
        process_name="code.exe",
        exe_path="C:\\Apps\\Code.exe",
        timestamp_utc=clock.now_utc(),
        event_source="winevent",
    ))
    while not manager.event_queue.empty():
        item = manager.event_queue.get_nowait()
        manager._handle_raw_event(item)

    # Advance time and switch to another app
    clock.advance(120)
    manager.on_event(RawEvent(
        event_type="foreground_change",
        pid=2002,
        process_name="CHROME.EXE",
        exe_path="C:\\Apps\\chrome.exe",
        timestamp_utc=clock.now_utc(),
        event_source="winevent",
    ))
    while not manager.event_queue.empty():
        item = manager.event_queue.get_nowait()
        manager._handle_raw_event(item)

    # Check sessions table
    sessions = store.read_dataframe("activity_sessions", paths.activity_sessions_csv)
    assert not sessions.empty
    assert "Code.EXE" in sessions["process_name"].values


def test_open_session_state_lifecycle_and_recovery(tmp_path):
    """Verify open session state is written during active tracking and recovered after sudden exit."""
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()

    start_time = datetime(2026, 10, 19, 14, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    clock = FakeClock(start_time)

    manager = ActivityManager(config=config, store=store, clock=clock)
    manager._running = True

    # 1. Start session
    manager.on_event(RawEvent(
        event_type="foreground_change",
        pid=5555,
        process_name="Notepad.exe",
        exe_path="C:\\Windows\\notepad.exe",
        timestamp_utc=start_time,
        event_source="winevent",
    ))
    while not manager.event_queue.empty():
        item = manager.event_queue.get_nowait()
        manager._handle_raw_event(item)

    open_session_file = paths.data_dir / "open_session.json"
    assert open_session_file.exists()
    state_data = json.loads(open_session_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert state_data["process_name"] == "Notepad.exe"
    assert state_data["pid"] == 5555

    # 2. Advance time and update last_active via heartbeat simulation
    clock.advance(300)  # 5 minutes active
    manager.active_session.last_active_utc = clock.now_utc()
    manager._save_open_session_state(manager.active_session)

    # 3. Simulate sudden crash: do NOT call manager.stop()
    # Now create a fresh ActivityManager on the same storage paths
    manager2 = ActivityManager(config=config, store=store, clock=clock)
    # On start(), it should recover the open session
    manager2.recover_orphaned_sessions()

    # The orphaned file should be processed and removed
    assert not open_session_file.exists()

    # The recovered session should be saved into activity_sessions.csv
    sessions_df = store.read_dataframe("activity_sessions", paths.activity_sessions_csv)
    assert not sessions_df.empty
    recovered = sessions_df[sessions_df["process_name"] == "Notepad.exe"]
    assert len(recovered) == 1
    assert float(recovered.iloc[0]["duration_seconds"]) == pytest.approx(300.0, rel=1e-2)


def test_resolve_uwp_window_unwraps_applicationframehost(monkeypatch):
    """Verify resolve_uwp_window resolves child window PID for ApplicationFrameHost."""
    from desktop_intelligence.collectors.foreground_events import resolve_uwp_window

    if sys.platform != "win32":
        pytest.skip("Windows-only test")

    import ctypes
    from ctypes import wintypes

    # Mock get_process_info_from_pid
    def fake_get_process_info(pid):
        if pid == 100:
            return ("ApplicationFrameHost.exe", "C:\\Windows\\System32\\ApplicationFrameHost.exe")
        elif pid == 200:
            return ("CalculatorApp.exe", "C:\\Program Files\\WindowsApps\\CalculatorApp.exe")
        return ("unknown", "")

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "desktop_intelligence.collectors.foreground_events.get_process_info_from_pid",
        fake_get_process_info,
    )

    # Mock EnumChildWindows to simulate finding child window with PID 200
    def fake_enum_child_windows(hwnd, enum_proc, lparam):
        # Call enum_proc with a fake child hwnd
        # Set fake GetWindowThreadProcessId to write pid 200
        original_gwtpid = ctypes.windll.user32.GetWindowThreadProcessId

        def fake_gwtpid(h, pid_ptr):
            if h == 999:  # child hwnd
                ctypes.cast(pid_ptr, ctypes.POINTER(wintypes.DWORD)).contents.value = 200
                return 1
            elif h == 500:  # frame hwnd
                ctypes.cast(pid_ptr, ctypes.POINTER(wintypes.DWORD)).contents.value = 100
                return 1
            return original_gwtpid(h, pid_ptr)

        monkeypatch.setattr(ctypes.windll.user32, "GetWindowThreadProcessId", fake_gwtpid)
        enum_proc(999, lparam)
        return True

    # Setup frame hwnd GetWindowThreadProcessId
    def fake_gwtpid_top(h, pid_ptr):
        ctypes.cast(pid_ptr, ctypes.POINTER(wintypes.DWORD)).contents.value = 100
        return 1

    monkeypatch.setattr(ctypes.windll.user32, "GetWindowThreadProcessId", fake_gwtpid_top)
    monkeypatch.setattr(ctypes.windll.user32, "EnumChildWindows", fake_enum_child_windows)

    resolved_pid, resolved_name, resolved_exe = resolve_uwp_window(500)
    assert resolved_pid == 200
    assert resolved_name == "CalculatorApp.exe"
    assert "CalculatorApp.exe" in resolved_exe


def test_single_instance_guard():
    """Verify SingleInstanceGuard allows only one owner at a time."""
    from desktop_intelligence.app import SingleInstanceGuard

    guard1 = SingleInstanceGuard(mutex_name="Local\\Test_DesktopIntelligence_UniqueMutex123")
    guard2 = SingleInstanceGuard(mutex_name="Local\\Test_DesktopIntelligence_UniqueMutex123")

    try:
        acquired1 = guard1.acquire()
        assert acquired1 is True
        assert guard1.is_primary is True

        if sys.platform == "win32":
            acquired2 = guard2.acquire()
            assert acquired2 is False
            assert guard2.is_primary is False
    finally:
        guard1.release()
        guard2.release()

"""Stage 3 tests: Resource snapshots, application inventory discovery, and system error events."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import pytest

from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import FakeClock, RawEvent
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.activity_manager import ActivityManager
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.application_inventory import (
    ApplicationInventoryScanner,
    normalize_app_key,
)
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.resource_snapshot import (
    capture_resource_sample,
    format_bytes_to_human,
)
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.system_errors import (
    parse_application_error_message,
    query_recent_application_errors,
)
from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig
from desktop_intelligence.paths import resolve_paths
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CsvStore


def test_byte_to_display_unit_formatting():
    assert format_bytes_to_human(None) == "N/A"
    assert format_bytes_to_human(500) == "500 B"
    assert format_bytes_to_human(1024) == "1.0 KB"
    assert format_bytes_to_human(15 * 1024 * 1024) == "15.0 MB"
    assert format_bytes_to_human(4 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024) == "4.00 GB"


def test_resource_snapshot_on_inaccessible_process():
    # Process does not exist (PID 99999999); must not crash and null metrics stay null
    sample = capture_resource_sample(
        pid=99999999,
        process_name="phantom.exe",
        trigger_event="process_start",
    )
    assert sample is not None
    assert sample.pid == 99999999
    assert sample.rss_bytes is None
    assert sample.process_memory_percent is None
    assert sample.cpu_time_seconds is None
    # System memory should still be captured
    assert sample.system_memory_percent is not None
    assert 0.0 <= sample.system_memory_percent <= 100.0


def test_event_triggered_resource_sample_count(tmp_path):
    paths = resolve_paths(
        data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"),
        log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"),
        config_file_override=tmp_path / "config.toml",
    )
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()
    config.collection.resource_snapshot_on_process_start = True
    config.collection.resource_snapshot_on_foreground_change = True

    base_time = datetime(2026, 10, 18, 12, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    clock = FakeClock(base_time)
    mgr = ActivityManager(config=config, store=store, clock=clock)
    mgr.start()

    # Emit 1 process_start and 2 foreground changes (different apps)
    mgr.on_event(RawEvent("process_start", os.getpid(), "python.exe", sys.executable, clock.now_utc(), "wmi"))
    clock.advance(5)
    mgr.on_event(RawEvent("foreground_change", os.getpid(), "python.exe", sys.executable, clock.now_utc(), "winevent"))
    clock.advance(10)
    # Foreground change to phantom PID
    mgr.on_event(RawEvent("foreground_change", 99999999, "other.exe", "C:\\other.exe", clock.now_utc(), "winevent"))

    mgr.stop()

    samples = store.read_rows("resource_samples", paths.resource_samples_csv)
    # Exactly 3 samples triggered (1 process_start + 2 foreground_change)
    assert len(samples) == 3
    assert samples[0]["trigger_event"] == "process_start"
    assert samples[1]["trigger_event"] == "foreground_change"
    assert samples[2]["trigger_event"] == "foreground_change"


def test_inventory_deduplication_and_provenance():
    scanner = ApplicationInventoryScanner(clock=FakeClock(datetime(2026, 10, 18, 12, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)))

    raw_items = [
        {"display_name": "Visual Studio Code", "exe_path": "C:\\VSCode\\code.exe", "source": "registry"},
        {"display_name": "Visual Studio Code", "exe_path": "C:\\VSCode\\code.exe", "source": "start_menu"},
        {"display_name": "Git", "exe_path": "C:\\Git\\cmd\\git.exe", "source": "registry"},
        {"display_name": "Git Bash", "exe_path": "C:\\Git\\git-bash.exe", "source": "start_menu"},
    ]

    launch_counts = {"code.exe": 42, "git": 0}
    last_launches = {"code.exe": "2026-10-18T10:00:00.000Z"}

    inventory = scanner.build_inventory(
        raw_items=raw_items,
        observed_launch_counts=launch_counts,
        last_launch_timestamps=last_launches,
    )

    # Visual Studio Code should be merged into 1 item with source "registry;start_menu"
    vscode_items = [item for item in inventory if "Visual Studio Code" in item.display_name]
    assert len(vscode_items) == 1
    vscode = vscode_items[0]
    assert vscode.inventory_source == "registry;start_menu"
    assert vscode.observed_launch_count == 42
    assert vscode.last_observed_launch_ts_utc == "2026-10-18T10:00:00.000Z"

    # Git items
    git_item = next(item for item in inventory if item.display_name == "Git")
    assert git_item.observed_launch_count == 0
    # Must not claim "never used"; launch count is 0 and last_observed is empty
    assert git_item.last_observed_launch_ts_utc == ""


def test_application_error_message_parsing():
    sample_error_log = (
        "Faulting application name: badapp.exe, version: 1.0.0.0, time stamp: 0x5ee8372d\r\n"
        "Faulting module name: crashdll.dll, version: 2.1.0.0, time stamp: 0x5ee8372d\r\n"
        "Exception code: 0xc0000005\r\n"
        "Fault offset: 0x0000000000012345\r\n"
    )

    parsed = parse_application_error_message(sample_error_log)
    assert parsed["app_name"] == "badapp.exe"
    assert parsed["faulting_module"] == "crashdll.dll"
    assert parsed["error_code"] == "0xc0000005"


def test_application_error_message_parsing_malformed():
    # Empty or localized message without standard tokens
    parsed = parse_application_error_message("Ein unbekannter Fehler ist aufgetreten.")
    assert parsed["app_name"] == "unknown"
    assert parsed["error_code"] == ""
    assert "Ein unbekannter Fehler" in parsed["description"]


def test_query_recent_application_errors_diagnostics():
    events, diag = query_recent_application_errors(max_records=5)
    assert isinstance(events, list)
    assert isinstance(diag, str)
    assert len(diag) > 0

"""Stage 4 tests: NumPy/Pandas analytics, pattern learning, and evidence-backed recommendations."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import json
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from desktop_intelligence.analytics.clean import (
    clean_sessions_df,
    clean_samples_df,
    clean_events_df,
)
from desktop_intelligence.analytics.usage import (
    calculate_usage_summary,
    calculate_daily_summary,
    calculate_day_of_week_distribution,
    calculate_hourly_heatmap_matrix,
    calculate_app_baselines,
)
from desktop_intelligence.analytics.resources import analyze_resource_hogs
from desktop_intelligence.analytics.patterns import detect_launch_patterns
from desktop_intelligence.analytics.recommendations import generate_recommendations
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import FakeClock
from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig, PatternsConfig, ResourcesConfig
from desktop_intelligence.models import InventoryItem

def test_empty_dataframe_handling():
    empty_df = pd.DataFrame()
    cleaned_ses = clean_sessions_df(empty_df)
    assert cleaned_ses.empty

    usage_summary = calculate_usage_summary(cleaned_ses)
    assert usage_summary.empty
    assert "process_name" in usage_summary.columns
    assert "active_hours" in usage_summary.columns

    daily_summary = calculate_daily_summary(cleaned_ses)
    assert daily_summary.empty

    baselines = calculate_app_baselines(cleaned_ses)
    assert baselines.empty

    matrix = calculate_hourly_heatmap_matrix(cleaned_ses)
    assert matrix.shape == (7, 24)
    assert np.all(matrix == 0.0)


def test_clean_sessions_sanitizes_malformed_and_duplicate_rows():
    raw_data = {
        "session_id": ["ses_1", "ses_1", "ses_2", "ses_3", "ses_4", "ses_5"],
        "process_name": ["code.exe", "code.exe", "  browser.exe  ", "nan", "unknown", "game.exe"],
        "pid": ["100", "100", "200", "300", "400", "500"],
        "start_ts_utc": [
            "2026-10-18T10:00:00.000Z",
            "2026-10-18T10:00:00.000Z",  # Duplicate session_id
            "2026-10-18T11:00:00.000Z",
            "2026-10-18T12:00:00.000Z",
            "2026-10-18T13:00:00.000Z",
            "2026-10-18T14:00:00.000Z",
        ],
        "end_ts_utc": [
            "2026-10-18T10:30:00.000Z",
            "2026-10-18T10:30:00.000Z",
            "2026-10-18T11:45:00.000Z",
            "2026-10-18T12:15:00.000Z",
            "2026-10-18T13:30:00.000Z",
            "2026-10-18T13:00:00.000Z",  # Inverted end < start (malformed)
        ],
        "duration_seconds": ["1800.0", "1800.0", "2700.0", "900.0", "1800.0", "-3600.0"],  # Negative duration
        "idle_seconds": ["0.0", "0.0", "300.0", "0.0", "0.0", "0.0"],
        "local_date": ["2026-10-18"] * 6,
        "day_of_week": ["Sunday"] * 6,
        "time_bucket": ["10:00", "10:00", "11:00", "12:00", "13:00", "14:00"],
    }
    df = pd.DataFrame(raw_data)
    cleaned = clean_sessions_df(df)

    # Expected: ses_1 kept once, browser.exe kept (trimmed), "nan" dropped, "unknown" dropped, game.exe dropped (end < start)
    assert len(cleaned) == 2
    assert set(cleaned["process_name"]) == {"code.exe", "browser.exe"}
    assert cleaned.loc[cleaned["process_name"] == "browser.exe", "active_seconds"].iloc[0] == 2400.0


def test_numpy_usage_aggregations():
    sessions = pd.DataFrame([
        {
            "session_id": "s1", "process_name": "code.exe", "duration_seconds": 3600.0, "active_seconds": 3600.0,
            "idle_seconds": 0.0, "local_date": "2026-10-18", "day_of_week": "Sunday", "time_bucket": "09:00"
        },
        {
            "session_id": "s2", "process_name": "code.exe", "duration_seconds": 1800.0, "active_seconds": 1800.0,
            "idle_seconds": 0.0, "local_date": "2026-10-18", "day_of_week": "Sunday", "time_bucket": "11:00"
        },
        {
            "session_id": "s3", "process_name": "browser.exe", "duration_seconds": 1800.0, "active_seconds": 1800.0,
            "idle_seconds": 0.0, "local_date": "2026-10-18", "day_of_week": "Sunday", "time_bucket": "10:00"
        },
    ])

    summary = calculate_usage_summary(sessions)
    assert len(summary) == 2

    # code.exe: total active 5400s = 1.5 hours, browser.exe: 1800s = 0.5 hours
    # total active = 2.0 hours. code.exe pct = 75.0%, browser.exe pct = 25.0%
    code_row = summary[summary["process_name"] == "code.exe"].iloc[0]
    assert code_row["active_hours"] == 1.5
    assert code_row["session_count"] == 2
    assert code_row["pct_active_time"] == 75.0

    browser_row = summary[summary["process_name"] == "browser.exe"].iloc[0]
    assert browser_row["active_hours"] == 0.5
    assert browser_row["session_count"] == 1
    assert browser_row["pct_active_time"] == 25.0


def test_numpy_statistical_baselines():
    # 5 observed days for editor.exe: [10, 20, 30, 40, 50] minutes
    dates = ["2026-10-01", "2026-10-02", "2026-10-03", "2026-10-04", "2026-10-05"]
    minutes = [10.0, 20.0, 30.0, 40.0, 50.0]
    rows = []
    for d, m in zip(dates, minutes):
        rows.append({
            "session_id": f"s_{d}",
            "process_name": "editor.exe",
            "active_seconds": m * 60.0,
            "duration_seconds": m * 60.0,
            "idle_seconds": 0.0,
            "local_date": d,
            "day_of_week": "Monday",
            "time_bucket": "09:00",
        })

    sessions = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    baselines = calculate_app_baselines(sessions)

    assert len(baselines) == 1
    row = baselines.iloc[0]
    assert row["process_name"] == "editor.exe"
    assert row["mean_daily_min"] == 30.0
    assert row["median_daily_min"] == 30.0
    expected_std = float(np.std(minutes))
    assert row["std_daily_min"] == pytest.approx(expected_std, abs=0.1)
    assert row["p75_daily_min"] == 40.0
    assert row["days_observed"] == 5


def test_pattern_minimum_observation_days_enforced():
    # Setup a 3-day history where app is launched 100% of the time
    cfg = PatternsConfig(
        minimum_observation_days=14,
        minimum_context_occurrences=2,
        launch_frequency_threshold=0.80,
    )

    rows = []
    for i, d in enumerate(["2026-10-01", "2026-10-02", "2026-10-03"]):
        rows.append({
            "process_name": "app.exe",
            "local_date": d,
            "day_of_week": "Thursday" if i == 0 else "Friday" if i == 1 else "Saturday",
            "time_bucket": "09:00",
        })
    sessions = pd.DataFrame(rows)

    patterns = detect_launch_patterns(sessions, cfg)
    # MUST NOT qualify: observation period is 3 days, required is 14 days!
    assert len(patterns) == 0


def test_pattern_frequency_threshold_boundary():
    # Setup 10 Mondays across 10 weeks (observation span > 14 days and context occurrences = 10)
    cfg = PatternsConfig(
        minimum_observation_days=14,
        minimum_context_occurrences=10,
        launch_frequency_threshold=0.80,
    )

    # 10 consecutive Mondays: 2026-08-03 to 2026-10-05
    start_date = datetime(2026, 8, 3)
    monday_dates = [(start_date + timedelta(weeks=w)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d") for w in range(10)]

    rows = []
    # Base monitoring presence on all 10 Mondays
    for d in monday_dates:
        rows.append({"process_name": "system_idle.exe", "local_date": d, "day_of_week": "Monday", "time_bucket": "18:00"})

    # App A: launched on 7 of 10 Mondays (freq = 0.70 < 0.80)
    for d in monday_dates[:7]:
        rows.append({"process_name": "appA.exe", "local_date": d, "day_of_week": "Monday", "time_bucket": "09:00"})

    # App B: launched on 8 of 10 Mondays (freq = 0.80 == 0.80)
    for d in monday_dates[:8]:
        rows.append({"process_name": "appB.exe", "local_date": d, "day_of_week": "Monday", "time_bucket": "09:00"})

    # App C: launched on 9 of 10 Mondays (freq = 0.90 > 0.80)
    for d in monday_dates[:9]:
        rows.append({"process_name": "appC.exe", "local_date": d, "day_of_week": "Monday", "time_bucket": "09:00"})

    sessions = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    patterns = detect_launch_patterns(sessions, cfg)

    # App A does NOT qualify; App B and App C qualify
    qualifying_apps = {p.app_name for p in patterns}
    assert "appA.exe" not in qualifying_apps
    assert "appB.exe" in qualifying_apps
    assert "appC.exe" in qualifying_apps

    b_pattern = next(p for p in patterns if p.app_name == "appB.exe")
    assert b_pattern.frequency == 0.80
    assert b_pattern.context_occurrences == 10
    assert b_pattern.launches_in_context == 8


def test_resource_hogs_analysis():
    cfg = ResourcesConfig(
        high_memory_percent=15.0,
        sustained_observation_count=3,
    )

    samples = pd.DataFrame([
        # App 1: 2 observations above 15.0% (not a hog)
        {"process_name": "normal.exe", "process_memory_percent": 16.0, "rss_bytes": 1000000},
        {"process_name": "normal.exe", "process_memory_percent": 18.0, "rss_bytes": 1200000},
        {"process_name": "normal.exe", "process_memory_percent": 5.0, "rss_bytes": 500000},

        # App 2: 3 observations above 15.0% (is a hog)
        {"process_name": "hog.exe", "process_memory_percent": 22.0, "rss_bytes": 3000000},
        {"process_name": "hog.exe", "process_memory_percent": 25.0, "rss_bytes": 3500000},
        {"process_name": "hog.exe", "process_memory_percent": 20.0, "rss_bytes": 2800000},
    ])

    findings = analyze_resource_hogs(samples, cfg)
    assert len(findings) == 2

    hog_finding = next(f for f in findings if f.process_name == "hog.exe")
    assert hog_finding.is_hog is True
    assert hog_finding.sustained_samples == 3
    assert hog_finding.max_memory_percent == 25.0

    normal_finding = next(f for f in findings if f.process_name == "normal.exe")
    assert normal_finding.is_hog is False


def test_recommendation_generator_and_deduplication(tmp_path):
    paths = resolve_paths(
        data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"),
        log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"),
        config_file_override=tmp_path / "config.toml",
    )
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()
    config.patterns.minimum_observation_days = 14
    config.patterns.minimum_context_occurrences = 2
    config.patterns.launch_frequency_threshold = 0.80

    clock = FakeClock(datetime(2026, 10, 18, 12, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc))

    # Create 14 days of sessions
    dates = [(datetime(2026, 10, 1) + timedelta(days=i)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d") for i in range(15)]
    rows = []
    for d in dates:
        rows.append({
            "process_name": "habit_app.exe",
            "local_date": d,
            "day_of_week": "Friday" if d in ["2026-10-02", "2026-10-09"] else "Monday",
            "time_bucket": "09:00",
            "active_seconds": 1800.0,
            "duration_seconds": 1800.0,
            "idle_seconds": 0.0,
            "session_id": f"s_{d}",
        })
    sessions_df = pd.DataFrame(rows)

    samples_df = pd.DataFrame([
        {"process_name": "leaker.exe", "process_memory_percent": 30.0, "rss_bytes": 5000000},
        {"process_name": "leaker.exe", "process_memory_percent": 32.0, "rss_bytes": 5200000},
        {"process_name": "leaker.exe", "process_memory_percent": 35.0, "rss_bytes": 5500000},
    ])

    inventory = [
        InventoryItem(
            observed_ts_utc="2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z",
            display_name="Unused Legacy App",
            exe_path="C:\\legacy.exe",
            inventory_source="registry",
            first_seen_ts_utc="2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z",
            last_observed_launch_ts_utc="",
            observed_launch_count=0,
        )
    ]

    recs1 = generate_recommendations(config, store, sessions_df, samples_df, inventory, clock)
    assert len(recs1) >= 2  # At least habit_launch and resource_hog

    # Second call must not duplicate pending recommendations
    recs2 = generate_recommendations(config, store, sessions_df, samples_df, inventory, clock)
    assert len(recs2) == 0

    all_stored = store.read_rows("recommendations", paths.recommendations_csv)
    assert len(all_stored) == len(recs1)

"""Stage 4 tests: Reliability event filtering, classification, and metadata accuracy."""

import pytest

from desktop_intelligence.collectors.system_errors import (
    EVENTLOG_ERROR_TYPE,
    EVENTLOG_INFORMATION_TYPE,
    EVENTLOG_WARNING_TYPE,
    SystemErrorEvent,
    is_supported_reliability_event,
    parse_application_error_message,
    query_recent_application_errors,
)


def test_informational_service_events_excluded():
    """Verify that informational service heartbeats/starts are NEVER classified as application crashes."""
    # Codex sandbox service heartbeat
    assert is_supported_reliability_event(
        source_name="CodexSandboxService.OpenAI.Codex",
        event_id=1000,
        event_type=EVENTLOG_INFORMATION_TYPE,
    ) is False

    # Generic third-party service starting
    assert is_supported_reliability_event(
        source_name="MyBackgroundService",
        event_id=1000,
        event_type=EVENTLOG_INFORMATION_TYPE,
    ) is False

    # Empty source
    assert is_supported_reliability_event("", 1000, EVENTLOG_ERROR_TYPE) is False


def test_genuine_application_crashes_and_hangs_included():
    """Verify supported crash, hang, and WER providers are classified correctly."""
    # Genuine Application Error crash
    assert is_supported_reliability_event(
        source_name="Application Error",
        event_id=1000,
        event_type=EVENTLOG_ERROR_TYPE,
    ) is True

    # Genuine Application Hang
    assert is_supported_reliability_event(
        source_name="Application Hang",
        event_id=1002,
        event_type=EVENTLOG_ERROR_TYPE,
    ) is True

    # Windows Error Reporting diagnostics
    assert is_supported_reliability_event(
        source_name="Windows Error Reporting",
        event_id=1001,
        event_type=EVENTLOG_INFORMATION_TYPE,
    ) is True


def test_hang_message_parsing():
    hang_msg = (
        "The program excel.exe version 16.0.14326.20454 stopped interacting with Windows "
        "and was closed. To see if more information about the problem is available, check the problem history."
    )
    parsed = parse_application_error_message(hang_msg)
    assert parsed["app_name"] == "excel.exe"
    assert parsed["error_code"] == ""


def test_crash_message_parsing_full_metadata():
    crash_msg = (
        "Faulting application name: code.exe, version: 1.85.0.0, time stamp: 0x654321ab\r\n"
        "Faulting module name: v8.dll, version: 1.85.0.0, time stamp: 0x654321cd\r\n"
        "Exception code: 0xc0000005\r\n"
        "Fault offset: 0x0000000000123456"
    )
    parsed = parse_application_error_message(crash_msg)
    assert parsed["app_name"] == "code.exe"
    assert parsed["faulting_module"] == "v8.dll"
    assert parsed["error_code"] == "0xc0000005"


def test_honest_unavailable_metadata():
    """Missing or localized message fields should not fabricate data."""
    ev = SystemErrorEvent(
        event_ts_utc="2026-10-18T12:00:00.000Z",
        app_name="unknown",
        error_code="-",
        faulting_module="-",
        description="Localized service event",
        source="Windows_Application_Error",
        severity="Crash",
        provider="Application Error",
        event_id=1000,
    )
    assert ev.error_code == "-"
    assert ev.faulting_module == "-"
    assert ev.provider == "Application Error"


def test_live_kernel_event_parsing():
    """Verify that LiveKernelEvent watchdog reports are classified as System / OS Reports without unknown app name."""
    msg = (
        " 0 LiveKernelEvent Not available 0 1b8 aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa 10_0_26200 0_0 768_1 "
        r"\Windows\LiveKernelReports\WATCHDOG4401\WATCHDOG4401-20261018-1451.dmp"
    )
    parsed = parse_application_error_message(msg, provider="Windows Error Reporting")
    assert parsed["app_name"] == "System / OS Report"
    assert parsed["error_code"] == "0x1B8"
    assert parsed["faulting_module"] == "WATCHDOG4401"


def test_bluescreen_event_parsing():
    """Verify that BlueScreen crash records are classified as System / OS Reports."""
    msg = " 0 BlueScreen Not available 0 7e ffffffffc0000005"
    parsed = parse_application_error_message(msg, provider="Windows Error Reporting")
    assert parsed["app_name"] == "System / OS Report"
    assert parsed["error_code"] == "0x7E"


def test_wer_generic_event_classification():
    """Verify that unresolved Windows Error Reporting records identify the provider instead of 'unknown'."""
    msg = "Generic Windows Error Reporting payload with no application signature."
    parsed = parse_application_error_message(msg, provider="Windows Error Reporting")
    assert parsed["app_name"] == "Windows Error Reporting"
    assert parsed["error_code"] == ""


def test_wer_appcrash_signature_parsing():
    """Verify that APPCRASH signatures in WER payloads extract the true faulted application."""
    msg = (
        "Fault bucket 12345678, type 5\r\n"
        "Event Name: APPCRASH\r\n"
        "Response: Not available\r\n"
        "Cab Id: 0\r\n\r\n"
        "Problem signature:\r\n"
        "P1: notepad.exe\r\n"
        "P2: 10.0.19041.1\r\n"
        "P3: 5ee10f6e\r\n"
        "P4: ntdll.dll\r\n"
        "P5: 10.0.19041.1\r\n"
        "P6: 93301a27\r\n"
        "P7: c0000005\r\n"
        "P8: 0000000000062b10\r\n"
    )
    parsed = parse_application_error_message(msg, provider="Windows Error Reporting")
    assert parsed["app_name"] == "notepad.exe"
    assert parsed["faulting_module"] == "ntdll.dll"
    assert parsed["error_code"] == "c0000005"


def test_reliability_events_caching(monkeypatch):
    """Verify that query_recent_application_errors uses TTL cache and does not repeat expensive queries."""
    from desktop_intelligence.collectors.system_errors import clear_errors_cache, query_recent_application_errors
    import desktop_intelligence.collectors.system_errors as se_module

    clear_errors_cache()
    call_count = [0]

    def fake_fetch(max_records=50, clock=None):
        call_count[0] += 1
        return ([SystemErrorEvent(
            event_ts_utc="2026-10-19T10:00:00.000Z",
            app_name="dummy.exe",
            error_code="0x1",
            faulting_module="mod.dll",
            description="Dummy crash",
        )], f"Call {call_count[0]}")

    monkeypatch.setattr(se_module, "_fetch_recent_application_errors", fake_fetch)

    # First call calls underlying fetcher
    res1, diag1 = query_recent_application_errors(cache_ttl_seconds=10.0)
    assert call_count[0] == 1
    assert diag1 == "Call 1"
    assert len(res1) == 1

    # Second call should hit the cache without calling fetcher again
    res2, diag2 = query_recent_application_errors(cache_ttl_seconds=10.0)
    assert call_count[0] == 1
    assert diag2 == "Call 1"

    # Force refresh should bypass cache
    res3, diag3 = query_recent_application_errors(force_refresh=True)
    assert call_count[0] == 2
    assert diag3 == "Call 2"

    clear_errors_cache()

FILE: .\tests\unit\test_stage5_storage_integrity.py

"""Tests for Stage 5: Storage performance, atomic replace, and timestamp integrity."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
import pytest

from desktop_intelligence.app import DesktopIntelligenceApp
from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig
from desktop_intelligence.models import (
    SCHEMAS,
    ActivityEvent,
    ActivitySession,
    ResourceSample,
    format_utc_iso,
    parse_utc_iso,
)
from desktop_intelligence.paths import resolve_paths
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CorruptRowError, CsvStore


def test_parse_utc_iso_strict_validation():
    """Verify parse_utc_iso strictly enforces ISO_UTC_REGEX."""
    # Valid UTC timestamps with Z
    dt1 = parse_utc_iso("2026-10-19T10:00:00Z")
    assert dt1.tzinfo == timezone.utc

    dt2 = parse_utc_iso("2026-10-19T10:00:00.123Z")
    assert dt2.tzinfo == timezone.utc

    # Valid UTC timestamp with +00:00
    dt3 = parse_utc_iso("2026-10-19T10:00:00+00:00")
    assert dt3.tzinfo == timezone.utc

    # Invalid: naive timestamp (no Z or offset)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        parse_utc_iso("2026-10-19T10:00:00")

    # Invalid: non-UTC offset (e.g. +05:30)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        parse_utc_iso("2026-10-19T10:00:00+05:30")

    # Invalid: malformed date string
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        parse_utc_iso("not-a-timestamp")


def test_atomic_replace_all(tmp_path):
    """Verify replace_all updates CSV atomically and validates schema length."""
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)

    now_ts = format_utc_iso(datetime(2026, 10, 19, 12, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc))

    # Append initial row
    store.append_activity_event(ActivityEvent(
        event_id="evt_1",
        event_ts_utc=now_ts,
        event_type="process_start",
        pid=100,
        process_name="code.exe",
        exe_path="C:\\Apps\\code.exe",
        event_source="wmi",
    ))
    assert len(store.read_rows("activity_events", paths.activity_events_csv)) == 1

    # Atomically replace with 2 new rows
    new_rows = [
        ["evt_2", now_ts, "foreground_change", "200", "notepad.exe", "C:\\Windows\\notepad.exe", "winevent"],
        ["evt_3", now_ts, "foreground_change", "300", "calc.exe", "C:\\Windows\\calc.exe", "winevent"],
    ]
    store.replace_all("activity_events", paths.activity_events_csv, new_rows)

    rows = store.read_rows("activity_events", paths.activity_events_csv)
    assert len(rows) == 2
    assert rows[0]["event_id"] == "evt_2"
    assert rows[1]["event_id"] == "evt_3"

    # Verify no dangling temp files remain
    tmp_files = list(paths.data_dir.glob("*.tmp"))
    assert len(tmp_files) == 0

    # Malformed row length must raise CorruptRowError and not alter file
    with pytest.raises(CorruptRowError):
        store.replace_all("activity_events", paths.activity_events_csv, [["invalid_row_too_short"]])

    # File contents should still have the 2 valid rows
    assert len(store.read_rows("activity_events", paths.activity_events_csv)) == 2


def test_app_prune_retention_wiring(tmp_path):
    """Verify DesktopIntelligenceApp.prune_retention cleans records older than retention threshold."""
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)

    # Insert old record (400 days old) and new record (today)
    old_dt = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=400)
    old_ts = format_utc_iso(old_dt)
    new_ts = format_utc_iso(datetime.now(timezone.utc))

    store.append_activity_session(ActivitySession(
        "s_old", "code.exe", 100, old_ts, old_ts, 60.0, 0.0, "2025-09-01", "Monday", "10:00"
    ))
    store.append_activity_session(ActivitySession(
        "s_new", "code.exe", 100, new_ts, new_ts, 60.0, 0.0, "2026-10-19", "Monday", "10:00"
    ))

    assert len(store.read_rows("activity_sessions", paths.activity_sessions_csv)) == 2

    # App with sessions_days = 365
    app = DesktopIntelligenceApp(config_path=paths.config_file)
    app.paths = paths
    app.store = store
    app.config.retention.sessions_days = 365

    res = app.prune_retention()
    assert res["sessions"] == 1

    remaining = store.read_rows("activity_sessions", paths.activity_sessions_csv)
    assert len(remaining) == 1
    assert remaining[0]["session_id"] == "s_new"

"""Stage 5 tests: Matplotlib visualizations and PySide6 UI navigation and rendering."""

import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

# Ensure headless Qt offscreen platform
os.environ["QT_QPA_PLATFORM"] = "offscreen"

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication

from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig, load_config
from desktop_intelligence.models import (
    InventoryItem,
    RecommendationRecord,
    AutomationLogRecord,
    format_utc_iso,
    now_utc,
)
from desktop_intelligence.paths import resolve_paths
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CsvStore
from desktop_intelligence.ui.main_window import MainWindow
from desktop_intelligence.visualization.charts import (
    create_top_apps_chart,
    create_daily_trend_chart,
    create_day_of_week_chart,
    create_hourly_heatmap_chart,
    create_empty_figure,
)


@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
def qapp():
    app = QApplication.instance()
    if app is None:
        app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    yield app


def test_charts_empty_and_filled_generation():
    empty_df = pd.DataFrame()

    # Empty generation
    fig_empty_top = create_top_apps_chart(empty_df)
    assert fig_empty_top is not None

    fig_empty_trend = create_daily_trend_chart(empty_df)
    assert fig_empty_trend is not None

    fig_empty_dow = create_day_of_week_chart(empty_df)
    assert fig_empty_dow is not None

    fig_empty_heat = create_hourly_heatmap_chart(np.zeros((7, 24)))
    assert fig_empty_heat is not None

    # Filled generation
    filled_summary = pd.DataFrame([
        {"process_name": "code.exe", "active_hours": 3.5},
        {"process_name": "browser.exe", "active_hours": 1.8},
    ])
    fig_filled_top = create_top_apps_chart(filled_summary)
    assert len(fig_filled_top.axes) == 1
    assert fig_filled_top.axes[0].get_title() == "Top 2 Applications by Usage Time"

    filled_daily = pd.DataFrame([
        {"local_date": "2026-10-17", "active_hours": 4.0, "idle_hours": 0.5},
        {"local_date": "2026-10-18", "active_hours": 5.2, "idle_hours": 1.0},
    ])
    fig_filled_trend = create_daily_trend_chart(filled_daily)
    assert len(fig_filled_trend.axes) == 1

    filled_dow = pd.DataFrame([
        {"day_of_week": "Monday", "active_hours": 2.0},
        {"day_of_week": "Tuesday", "active_hours": 4.5},
    ])
    fig_filled_dow = create_day_of_week_chart(filled_dow)
    assert len(fig_filled_dow.axes) == 1

    heat_mat = np.ones((7, 24), dtype=np.float64) * 15.0
    fig_filled_heat = create_hourly_heatmap_chart(heat_mat)
    assert len(fig_filled_heat.axes) == 2  # Plot axis + Colorbar axis
