        '[general]\napp_name = "Desktop Intelligence"\nconfig_version = 1\nlog_level = "INFO"\ntimezone = "local"\n\n'
        '[paths]\ndata_dir = ""\nlog_dir = ""\n\n'
        '[collection]\nenable_process_start_events = true\nenable_foreground_events = true\n'
        'enable_application_inventory = true\nenable_system_error_events = true\n'

        '[retention]\nevents_days = 180\nsessions_days = 365\nresource_samples_days = 90\n\n'
        '[patterns]\nminimum_observation_days = 14\nminimum_context_occurrences = 10\n'
        'launch_frequency_threshold = 0.80\ntime_bucket_minutes = 60\n'
        'eligible_day_types = ["weekday", "weekend"]\n\n'

        'high_cpu_percent = 80.0\nsustained_observation_count = 3\n\n'

        '[llm]\nenabled = false\nprovider = "none"\nshare_raw_events = false\n',
        encoding="utf-8"
    )

    assert window.stack.count() == 6

    # Test switching pages
    for page_idx in range(6):
        window.nav_list.setCurrentRow(page_idx)
        assert window.stack.currentIndex() == page_idx

    window.close()


def test_ui_data_dispatch_with_unicode_and_large_datasets(qapp, tmp_path):
    paths = resolve_paths(
        data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"),
        log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"),
        config_file_override=tmp_path / "config.toml",
    )
    paths.ensure_directories()
    paths.config_file.write_text("[general]\nconfig_version = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()

    window = MainWindow(config=config, paths=paths, store=store)

    # Dispatch filled datasets with Unicode names and spaces
    sessions = pd.DataFrame([
        {
            "session_id": "ses_u1",
            "process_name": "代码编辑器.exe",
            "active_seconds": 7200.0,
            "duration_seconds": 7200.0,
            "idle_seconds": 0.0,
            "local_date": "2026-10-18",
            "day_of_week": "Sunday",
            "time_bucket": "10:00",
        },
        {
            "session_id": "ses_u2",
            "process_name": "App With Spaces & (Special-Chars).exe",
            "active_seconds": 3600.0,
            "duration_seconds": 3600.0,
            "idle_seconds": 0.0,
            "local_date": "2026-10-18",
            "day_of_week": "Sunday",
            "time_bucket": "12:00",
        },
    ])

    samples = pd.DataFrame([
        {
            "sample_id": "s1",
            "sample_ts_utc": "2026-10-18T10:00:00.000Z",
            "trigger_event": "foreground_change",
            "pid": 100,
            "process_name": "代码编辑器.exe",
            "rss_bytes": 1024 * 1024 * 500,
            "process_memory_percent": 18.5,
            "cpu_time_seconds": 12.5,
            "system_memory_percent": 65.0,
        }
    ])

    inventory = [
        InventoryItem(
            observed_ts_utc="2026-10-18T00:00:00.000Z",
            display_name="代码编辑器",
            exe_path="C:\\Apps\\代码编辑器.exe",
            inventory_source="registry",
            first_seen_ts_utc="2026-10-18T00:00:00.000Z",
            last_observed_launch_ts_utc="2026-10-18T10:00:00.000Z",
            observed_launch_count=5,
        )
    ]

    recs = [
        RecommendationRecord(
            recommendation_id="rec_1",
            created_ts_utc="2026-10-18T10:00:00.000Z",
            category="habit_launch",
            subject="Launch 代码编辑器 on Sunday at 10:00",
            evidence_json='{"app_name": "代码编辑器", "frequency_percent": 85.0}',
            confidence=0.85,
            status="pending",
        )
    ]

    logs = [
        AutomationLogRecord(
            log_id="log_1",
            ts_utc="2026-10-18T10:00:00.000Z",
            rule_id="rec_1",
            action="launch",
            result="success",
            reason="Scheduled launch completed",
        )
    ]

    window._on_data_loaded(sessions, samples, inventory, recs, logs, "Operational")

    # Overview checks
    assert window.page_overview.card_active.value_label.text() == "3.0 hrs"
    assert window.page_overview.card_top_app.value_label.text() == "代码编辑器.exe"

    # Usage table checks
    assert window.page_usage.table.rowCount() == 2

    # Inventory table checks
    assert window.page_apps.table.rowCount() == 1

    # Automation rules checks
    assert window.page_automation.log_table.rowCount() == 1

    window.close()


def test_recommendation_action_and_config_persistence(qapp, tmp_path):
    paths = resolve_paths(
        data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"),
        log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"),
        config_file_override=tmp_path / "config.toml",
    )
    paths.ensure_directories()
    paths.config_file.write_text("[general]\nconfig_version = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()

    # Pre-populate recommendations.csv
    rec = RecommendationRecord(
        recommendation_id="rec_approve_test",
        created_ts_utc=format_utc_iso(now_utc()),
        category="habit_launch",
        subject="Launch Test App",
        evidence_json="{}",
        confidence=0.9,
        status="pending",
    )
    store.append_recommendation(rec)

    window = MainWindow(config=config, paths=paths, store=store)

    # Approve recommendation
    window._handle_recommendation_action("rec_approve_test", "approved")

    # Verify status changed in CSV
    rows = store.read_rows("recommendations", paths.recommendations_csv)
    assert len(rows) == 1
    assert rows[0]["status"] == "approved"

    # Toggle automation master switch
    window._handle_toggle_automation(True)
    assert window.config.automation.enabled is True

    # Check that config.toml on disk was updated
    saved_cfg = load_config(paths.config_file)
    assert saved_cfg.automation.enabled is True

    window.close()

"""Stage 6 tests: Scheduled launch rules, consent behavior, duplicate prevention, and audit logs."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json

import pytest

from desktop_intelligence.automation.launcher import FakeLauncher, SafeProcessLauncher

from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig
from desktop_intelligence.models import InventoryItem, RecommendationRecord

@pytest.fixture
def auto_env(tmp_path):
    paths = resolve_paths(
        data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"),
        log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"),
        config_file_override=tmp_path / "config.toml",
    )
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()
    config.general.timezone = "UTC"

    # Set clock to Monday at 09:00 UTC
    clock = FakeClock(datetime(2026, 10, 19, 9, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    launcher = FakeLauncher()
    return config, store, clock, launcher, paths


def test_automation_disabled_by_default(auto_env):
    config, store, clock, launcher, paths = auto_env
    # Default is disabled
    assert config.automation.enabled is False

    rule = AutomationRule(
        rule_id="r1",
        app_name="editor.exe",
        exe_path="C:\\Apps\\editor.exe",
        day_of_week="Monday",
        time_bucket="09:00",
        frequency_percent=85.0,
        approved=True,
        enabled=True,
    )

    scheduler = AutomationScheduler(config, store, launcher, clock)
    scheduler.set_rules([rule])

    logs = scheduler.evaluate_and_trigger()
    assert len(logs) == 0
    assert len(launcher.launched_history) == 0


def test_unapproved_or_disabled_rule_never_executes(auto_env):
    config, store, clock, launcher, paths = auto_env
    config.automation.enabled = True

    unapproved_rule = AutomationRule(
        rule_id="r_unapp",
        app_name="game.exe",
        exe_path="C:\\game.exe",
        day_of_week="Monday",
        time_bucket="09:00",
        frequency_percent=90.0,
        approved=False,  # NOT approved
        enabled=True,
    )

    disabled_rule = AutomationRule(
        rule_id="r_dis",
        app_name="tool.exe",
        exe_path="C:\\tool.exe",
        day_of_week="Monday",
        time_bucket="09:00",
        frequency_percent=88.0,
        approved=True,
        enabled=False,  # Disabled
    )

    scheduler = AutomationScheduler(config, store, launcher, clock)
    scheduler.set_rules([unapproved_rule, disabled_rule])

    logs = scheduler.evaluate_and_trigger()
    assert len(logs) == 0
    assert len(launcher.launched_history) == 0


def test_successful_scheduled_launch(auto_env):
    config, store, clock, launcher, paths = auto_env
    config.automation.enabled = True

    rule = AutomationRule(
        rule_id="r_ok",
        app_name="ide.exe",
        exe_path="C:\\Apps\\ide.exe",
        day_of_week="Monday",
        time_bucket="09:00",
        frequency_percent=85.0,
        approved=True,
        enabled=True,
    )

    scheduler = AutomationScheduler(config, store, launcher, clock)
    scheduler.set_rules([rule])

    logs = scheduler.evaluate_and_trigger()
    assert len(logs) == 1
    assert logs[0].result == "success"
    assert logs[0].rule_id == "r_ok"
    assert "C:\\Apps\\ide.exe" in launcher.launched_history

    # Verify audit record persisted in automation_log.csv
    stored_logs = store.read_rows("automation_log", paths.automation_log_csv)
    assert len(stored_logs) == 1
    assert stored_logs[0]["result"] == "success"

    # Same day evaluation must NOT re-trigger
    logs2 = scheduler.evaluate_and_trigger()
    assert len(logs2) == 0
    assert len(launcher.launched_history) == 1


def test_duplicate_instance_prevention(auto_env):
    config, store, clock, launcher, paths = auto_env
    config.automation.enabled = True
    config.automation.prevent_duplicate_instances = True

    # Mark browser as already running in fake launcher
    launcher.running_apps.append("browser.exe")

    rule = AutomationRule(
        rule_id="r_dup",
        app_name="browser.exe",
        exe_path="C:\\browser.exe",
        day_of_week="Monday",
        time_bucket="09:00",
        frequency_percent=85.0,
        approved=True,
        enabled=True,
    )

    scheduler = AutomationScheduler(config, store, launcher, clock)
    scheduler.set_rules([rule])

    logs = scheduler.evaluate_and_trigger()
    assert len(logs) == 1
    assert logs[0].result == "skipped_duplicate"
    assert "already running" in logs[0].reason
    assert len(launcher.launched_history) == 0


def test_consent_prompt_timeout_and_rejection(auto_env):
    config, store, clock, launcher, paths = auto_env
    config.automation.enabled = True
    config.automation.no_response_action = "skip"

    rule = AutomationRule(
        rule_id="r_consent",
        app_name="app.exe",
        exe_path="C:\\app.exe",
        day_of_week="Monday",
        time_bucket="09:00",
        frequency_percent=85.0,
        approved=True,
        enabled=True,
    )

    # 1. Test timeout (silence / no response)
    scheduler_timeout = AutomationScheduler(
        config, store, launcher, clock, consent_provider=lambda r, t: "timeout"
    )
    scheduler_timeout.set_rules([rule])
    logs_to = scheduler_timeout.evaluate_and_trigger()
    assert len(logs_to) == 1
    assert logs_to[0].result == "timeout_skip"
    assert len(launcher.launched_history) == 0

    # Reset rule last_triggered_date for next test
    rule.last_triggered_date = ""

    # 2. Test explicit user rejection
    scheduler_reject = AutomationScheduler(
        config, store, launcher, clock, consent_provider=lambda r, t: "reject"
    )
    scheduler_reject.set_rules([rule])
    logs_rej = scheduler_reject.evaluate_and_trigger()
    assert len(logs_rej) == 1
    assert logs_rej[0].result == "rejected"
    assert len(launcher.launched_history) == 0


def test_missing_or_unresolved_executable_path(auto_env):
    config, store, clock, launcher, paths = auto_env
    config.automation.enabled = True

    rule = AutomationRule(
        rule_id="r_missing",
        app_name="ghost.exe",
        exe_path="",  # Unresolved path
        day_of_week="Monday",
        time_bucket="09:00",
        frequency_percent=85.0,
        approved=True,
        enabled=True,
    )

    scheduler = AutomationScheduler(config, store, launcher, clock)
    scheduler.set_rules([rule])

    logs = scheduler.evaluate_and_trigger()
    assert len(logs) == 1
    assert logs[0].result == "error"
    assert "Executable path unresolved" in logs[0].reason


def test_safe_launcher_rejects_empty_and_nonexistent_paths():
    real_launcher = SafeProcessLauncher()
    success, msg = real_launcher.launch("")
    assert success is False
    assert "empty" in msg

    success2, msg2 = real_launcher.launch("C:\\NonExistent\\FakeApp12345.exe")
    assert success2 is False
    assert "not found" in msg2


def test_sync_rules_from_inventory():
    recs = [
        RecommendationRecord(
            recommendation_id="rec_vscode",
            created_ts_utc="2026-10-18T10:00:00.000Z",
            category="habit_launch",
            subject="Launch code.exe on Monday at 09:00",
            evidence_json='{"app_name": "code.exe", "day_of_week": "Monday", "time_bucket": "09:00", "frequency_percent": 85.0}',
            confidence=0.85,
            status="approved",
        )
    ]
    inventory = [
        InventoryItem(
            observed_ts_utc="2026-10-18T00:00:00.000Z",
            display_name="Visual Studio Code",
            exe_path="C:\\Program Files\\VSCode\\Code.exe",
            inventory_source="registry",
            first_seen_ts_utc="2026-10-18T00:00:00.000Z",
            last_observed_launch_ts_utc="2026-10-18T10:00:00.000Z",
            observed_launch_count=20,
        )
    ]

    rules = sync_rules_from_recommendations(recs, inventory)
    assert len(rules) == 1
    rule = rules[0]
    assert rule.approved is True
    assert rule.day_of_week == "Monday"
    assert rule.time_bucket == "09:00"
    assert rule.exe_path == "C:\\Program Files\\VSCode\\Code.exe"

"""Tests for Stage 6: Inventory accuracy, multi-hour heatmap slicing, recommendation cooldown, and chart limits."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import warnings
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from desktop_intelligence.analytics.recommendations import generate_recommendations
from desktop_intelligence.analytics.usage import calculate_hourly_heatmap_matrix
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.application_inventory import (
    ApplicationInventoryScanner,
    resolve_shortcut_target,
)
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import FakeClock
from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig
from desktop_intelligence.models import (
    InventoryItem,
    RecommendationRecord,
    format_utc_iso,
)
from desktop_intelligence.paths import resolve_paths
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CsvStore
from desktop_intelligence.visualization.charts import (
    create_top_apps_chart,
    create_daily_trend_chart,
    create_day_of_week_chart,
)


def test_registry_scan_missing_system_component_not_skipped(monkeypatch):
    """Verify registry scanner does not skip applications lacking the SystemComponent value."""
    if sys.platform != "win32":
        pytest.skip("Windows-only test")

    import winreg

    class FakeKey:
        def __enter__(self):
            return self
        def __exit__(self, *args):
            pass

    def fake_open_key(hive, subkey):
        return FakeKey()

    def fake_query_info_key(key):
        return (1, 0, 0)

    def fake_enum_key(key, index):
        return "TestApp_123"

    def fake_query_value_ex(key, name):
        if name == "DisplayName":
            return ("Test Great Application", 1)
        elif name == "SystemComponent":
            raise FileNotFoundError("Value not found")
        elif name == "DisplayIcon":
            return ("C:\\TestApp\\app.exe", 1)
        elif name == "InstallLocation":
            return ("C:\\TestApp", 1)
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Unknown value: {name}")

    monkeypatch.setattr(winreg, "OpenKey", fake_open_key)
    monkeypatch.setattr(winreg, "QueryInfoKey", fake_query_info_key)
    monkeypatch.setattr(winreg, "EnumKey", fake_enum_key)
    monkeypatch.setattr(winreg, "QueryValueEx", fake_query_value_ex)

    scanner = ApplicationInventoryScanner()
    results = scanner.scan_windows_registry()
    assert len(results) > 0
    names = [r["display_name"] for r in results]
    assert "Test Great Application" in names


def test_multi_hour_session_distribution_in_heatmap():
    """Verify calculate_hourly_heatmap_matrix slices sessions across multiple hours."""
    # Monday session from 10:00 to 12:00 (2 hours = 120 minutes = 7200 seconds)
    df = pd.DataFrame([{
        "session_id": "ses_multihour",
        "process_name": "code.exe",
        "day_of_week": "Monday",
        "time_bucket": "10:00",
        "start_dt": pd.Timestamp("2026-10-19 10:00:00+00:00"),
        "end_dt": pd.Timestamp("2026-10-19 12:00:00+00:00"),
        "active_seconds": 7200.0,
        "duration_seconds": 7200.0,
        "idle_seconds": 0.0,
    }])

    matrix = calculate_hourly_heatmap_matrix(df)
    # Monday is row index 0
    # Hour 10 should have 60 active minutes
    assert matrix[0, 10] == pytest.approx(60.0, rel=1e-1)
    # Hour 11 should have 60 active minutes
    assert matrix[0, 11] == pytest.approx(60.0, rel=1e-1)
    # Hour 12 should be 0
    assert matrix[0, 12] == 0.0


def test_recommendation_cooldown_and_unused_grouping(tmp_path):
    """Verify recommendation cooldown prevents regenerating declined recs, and >3 unused apps are grouped."""
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()

    now = datetime(2026, 10, 19, 12, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    clock = FakeClock(now)

    # 1. Add a declined recommendation created 2 days ago
    two_days_ago = now - timedelta(days=2)
    store.append_recommendation(RecommendationRecord(
        recommendation_id="rec_declined",
        created_ts_utc=format_utc_iso(two_days_ago),
        category="habit_launch",
        subject="Launch code.exe on Monday at 09:00",
        evidence_json="{}",
        confidence=0.9,
        status="declined",
    ))

    # Mock sessions creating that exact habit pattern
    sessions_data = []
    for d in range(15):
        day_date = (now - timedelta(days=d)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        sessions_data.append({
            "session_id": f"s_{d}",
            "process_name": "code.exe",
            "day_of_week": "Monday",
            "time_bucket": "09:00",
            "local_date": day_date,
            "duration_seconds": 1800.0,
            "active_seconds": 1800.0,
            "idle_seconds": 0.0,
            "start_dt": now,
            "end_dt": now,
        })
    sessions_df = pd.DataFrame(sessions_data)
    samples_df = pd.DataFrame()

    # 4 unused apps to test consolidation
    inventory = [
        InventoryItem(format_utc_iso(now), f"App {i}", f"C:\\app{i}.exe", "registry", format_utc_iso(now), "", 0)
        for i in range(1, 5)
    ]

    recs = generate_recommendations(
        config=config,
        store=store,
        sessions_df=sessions_df,
        samples_df=samples_df,
        inventory_items=inventory,
        clock=clock,
    )

    # Declined recommendation must not be regenerated due to 7-day cooldown
    subjects = [r.subject for r in recs]
    assert "Launch code.exe on Monday at 09:00" not in subjects

    # Unused apps (4 items) must be consolidated into a single recommendation
    unused_recs = [r for r in recs if r.category == "unused_application"]
    assert len(unused_recs) == 1
    assert "Review 4 unused applications" in unused_recs[0].subject


def test_matplotlib_zero_usage_no_singular_transformation_warning():
    """Verify charts with 0 usage do not raise Matplotlib UserWarning about identical low/high limits."""
    # Zero usage summary
    zero_summary = pd.DataFrame([
        {"process_name": "app1.exe", "active_hours": 0.0},
        {"process_name": "app2.exe", "active_hours": 0.0},
    ])

    with warnings.catch_warnings(record=True) as recorded_warnings:
        warnings.simplefilter("always")
        fig1 = create_top_apps_chart(zero_summary)
        fig2 = create_daily_trend_chart(pd.DataFrame([
            {"local_date": "2026-10-18", "active_hours": 0.0, "idle_hours": 0.0},
            {"local_date": "2026-10-19", "active_hours": 0.0, "idle_hours": 0.0},
        ]))
        fig3 = create_day_of_week_chart(pd.DataFrame([
            {"day_of_week": "Monday", "active_hours": 0.0},
            {"day_of_week": "Tuesday", "active_hours": 0.0},
        ]))

        # Filter for the specific transformation singular warning
        singular_warnings = [
            w for w in recorded_warnings
            if "Attempting to set identical low and high" in str(w.message)
        ]
        assert len(singular_warnings) == 0, f"Encountered singular transformation warnings: {singular_warnings}"

import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path
import pytest

os.environ["QT_QPA_PLATFORM"] = "offscreen"

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication

from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig, load_config
from desktop_intelligence.paths import resolve_paths
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CsvStore
from desktop_intelligence.ui.main_window import MainWindow
from desktop_intelligence.ui.theme import (
    ACCENT_PRESETS,
    generate_stylesheet,
    get_effective_theme,
    get_theme_palette,
)


@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
def qapp():
    app = QApplication.instance()
    if app is None:
        app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    yield app


def test_effective_theme_resolution():
    assert get_effective_theme("light") == "light"
    assert get_effective_theme("dark") == "dark"
    assert get_effective_theme("LIGHT") == "light"
    assert get_effective_theme("DARK") == "dark"
    assert get_effective_theme("system") in {"light", "dark"}
    # Graceful fallback for unknown strings
    assert get_effective_theme("nonexistent_theme") == "light"


def test_theme_palette_structure():
    light_p = get_theme_palette("light", "#6366F1")
    dark_p = get_theme_palette("dark", "#2563EB")

    required_keys = {
        "mode", "app_bg", "surface_bg", "border", "text_primary",
        "text_secondary", "sidebar_bg", "accent", "input_bg",
    }
    assert required_keys.issubset(set(light_p.keys()))
    assert required_keys.issubset(set(dark_p.keys()))

    assert light_p["mode"] == "light"
    assert dark_p["mode"] == "dark"
    assert light_p["accent"] == "#6366F1"
    assert dark_p["accent"] == "#2563EB"


def test_generate_stylesheet_tokens():
    light_css = generate_stylesheet("light", "#6366F1")
    assert "QMainWindow" in light_css
    assert "#F3F6FC" in light_css
    assert "#6366F1" in light_css

    dark_css = generate_stylesheet("dark", "#10B981")
    assert "QMainWindow" in dark_css
    assert "#0A0D16" in dark_css
    assert "#10B981" in dark_css


def test_main_window_theme_switching_and_persistence(qapp, tmp_path):
    paths = resolve_paths(
        data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"),
        log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"),
        config_file_override=tmp_path / "config.toml",
    )
    paths.ensure_directories()
    paths.config_file.write_text("[general]\nconfig_version = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()

    window = MainWindow(config=config, paths=paths, store=store)
    window.show()

    # Initial state should be light with default accent
    assert window.config.ui.theme == "light"

    # Switch to Dark mode and Teal accent
    window.apply_theme("dark", "#0D9488")
    assert window.config.ui.theme == "dark"
    assert window.config.ui.accent_color == "#0D9488"
    assert "#0A0D16" in window.styleSheet()
    assert "#0D9488" in window.styleSheet()

    # Persist config and inspect on disk
    window._persist_config()
    loaded = load_config(paths.config_file)
    assert loaded.ui.theme == "dark"
    assert loaded.ui.accent_color == "#0D9488"

    window.close()

"""Tests for Stage 7: Product loop, automation safety, consent dialog, and allowlist."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import pytest

from desktop_intelligence.app import DesktopIntelligenceApp
from desktop_intelligence.automation.launcher import SafeProcessLauncher
from desktop_intelligence.automation.rules import AutomationRule
from desktop_intelligence.automation.scheduler import AutomationScheduler
from desktop_intelligence.collectors.base import FakeClock
from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig
from desktop_intelligence.models import (
    AutomationLogRecord,
    format_utc_iso,
    new_id,
)
from desktop_intelligence.paths import resolve_paths
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CsvStore


def test_scheduler_last_triggered_date_hydrated_from_logs(tmp_path):
    """Verify scheduler initializes rule.last_triggered_date from historical logs on startup."""
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()
    config.automation.enabled = True

    now = datetime(2026, 10, 19, 10, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    clock = FakeClock(now)

    # 1. Historical log from today for rule "rec_habit_1"
    store.append_automation_log(AutomationLogRecord(
        log_id="log_earlier_today",
        ts_utc=format_utc_iso(now - timedelta(minutes=5)),
        rule_id="rec_habit_1",
        action="launch",
        result="success",
        reason="Launched successfully",
    ))

    # 2. Setup scheduler and load rules
    scheduler = AutomationScheduler(config=config, store=store, clock=clock)
    rule = AutomationRule(
        rule_id="rec_habit_1",
        app_name="code.exe",
        exe_path=sys.executable,
        day_of_week="Monday",
        time_bucket="10:00",
        frequency_percent=90.0,
        approved=True,
        enabled=True,
    )
    scheduler.set_rules([rule])

    # Rule must have hydrated its last_triggered_date to today's date
    today_str = now.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    assert rule.last_triggered_date == today_str

    # Evaluating scheduler now must not trigger duplicate launch
    logs = scheduler.evaluate_and_trigger()
    assert len(logs) == 0


def test_safe_process_launcher_allowlist_enforcement(tmp_path):
    """Verify SafeProcessLauncher strictly rejects targets not in allowlist."""
    dummy_exe = tmp_path / "unapproved_tool.exe"
    dummy_exe.write_text("binary content", encoding="utf-8")

    approved_exe = tmp_path / "approved_app.exe"
    approved_exe.write_text("binary content", encoding="utf-8")

    allowlist = {str(approved_exe).lower(), "approved_app.exe"}
    launcher = SafeProcessLauncher(allowlist_provider=lambda: allowlist)

    # 1. Unapproved tool must be rejected
    ok, msg = launcher.launch(str(dummy_exe))
    assert ok is False
    assert "not in the approved application allowlist" in msg

    # 2. Approved tool passes allowlist check (will fail on execution if not real PE, but passes allowlist)
    # Target in allowlist
    ok2, msg2 = launcher.launch(str(approved_exe))
    assert "not in the approved application allowlist" not in msg2


def test_app_run_analytics_cycle(tmp_path):
    """Verify DesktopIntelligenceApp.run_analytics_cycle coordinates cleaning, recs, rules, and retention."""
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    paths.ensure_directories()
    app = DesktopIntelligenceApp(config_path=paths.config_file)
    app.paths = paths
    app.store = CsvStore(paths)

    recs = app.run_analytics_cycle()
    assert isinstance(recs, list)


def test_launch_consent_dialog(qapp):
    """Verify LaunchConsentDialog options, countdown, and user choice recording."""
    from desktop_intelligence.ui.dialogs.consent_prompt import LaunchConsentDialog

    rule = AutomationRule(
        rule_id="r1",
        app_name="Notepad",
        exe_path="C:\\Windows\\notepad.exe",
        day_of_week="Monday",
        time_bucket="10:00",
        frequency_percent=85.0,
        approved=True,
    )

    dlg = LaunchConsentDialog(rule, timeout_seconds=10)
    assert dlg.remaining_seconds == 10
    assert dlg.user_choice == "timeout"

    # Simulate Launch Now click
    dlg._on_launch()
    assert dlg.user_choice == "approve"

    # Simulate Skip click
    dlg2 = LaunchConsentDialog(rule, timeout_seconds=5)
    dlg2._on_skip()
    assert dlg2.user_choice == "reject"

    # Simulate timer tick down to 0
    dlg3 = LaunchConsentDialog(rule, timeout_seconds=1)
    dlg3._on_tick()
    assert dlg3.user_choice == "timeout"

"""Stage 8 tests: UI correctness, single-flight DataLoaderWorker, OverviewPage signature caching,
High-DPI canvas synchronization, system tray integration, and theme contrast.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import unittest.mock as mock
import pytest
import pandas as pd

os.environ["QT_QPA_PLATFORM"] = "offscreen"

from PySide6.QtCore import QSize
from PySide6.QtGui import QCloseEvent, QResizeEvent
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication, QSystemTrayIcon

from desktop_intelligence.config import AppConfig
from desktop_intelligence.models import (
    ActivitySession,
    RecommendationRecord,
    format_utc_iso,
)
from desktop_intelligence.paths import resolve_paths
from desktop_intelligence.storage.csv_store import CsvStore
from desktop_intelligence.ui.main_window import MainWindow
from desktop_intelligence.ui.pages.overview import OverviewPage
from desktop_intelligence.ui.pages.settings import SettingsPage
from desktop_intelligence.ui.theme import (
    ACCENT_PRESETS,
    get_contrast_text_color,
    get_theme_palette,
)
from desktop_intelligence.visualization.charts import ThemedChartCanvas, create_empty_figure


@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
def qapp():
    app = QApplication.instance()
    if app is None:
        app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    yield app


def test_themed_chart_canvas_geometry_and_dpi_synchronization(qapp):
    """Verify ThemedChartCanvas handles dimension syncing and High-DPI without distortion."""
    fig = create_empty_figure()
    canvas = ThemedChartCanvas(fig)

    # Initial size synchronization
    canvas.resize(800, 400)
    canvas._sync_dimensions()

    assert canvas.figure is not None
    # Figure size in inches should reflect 800x400 at figure.dpi
    expected_w_inches = 800.0 / canvas.figure.dpi
    expected_h_inches = 400.0 / canvas.figure.dpi
    assert canvas.figure.get_figwidth() == pytest.approx(expected_w_inches, rel=1e-2)
    assert canvas.figure.get_figheight() == pytest.approx(expected_h_inches, rel=1e-2)

    # Resize event with real QResizeEvent
    resize_event = QResizeEvent(QSize(600, 300), QSize(800, 400))
    canvas.resizeEvent(resize_event)

    assert canvas.figure.get_figwidth() == pytest.approx(600.0 / canvas.figure.dpi, rel=1e-2)
    assert canvas.figure.get_figheight() == pytest.approx(300.0 / canvas.figure.dpi, rel=1e-2)


def test_overview_page_signature_caching_skips_redundant_renders(qapp):
    """Verify OverviewPage does not re-render charts or rebuild widgets when data is identical."""
    page = OverviewPage()

    sessions_df = pd.DataFrame([
        {
            "session_id": "s_1",
            "process_name": "code.exe",
            "pid": "100",
            "start_ts_utc": "2026-10-19T10:00:00.000Z",
            "end_ts_utc": "2026-10-19T11:00:00.000Z",
            "duration_seconds": 3600.0,
            "idle_seconds": 0.0,
            "active_seconds": 3600.0,
            "local_date": "2026-10-19",
            "day_of_week": "Monday",
            "time_bucket": "10:00",
        }
    ])

    recs = [
        RecommendationRecord(
            recommendation_id="rec_1",
            created_ts_utc="2026-10-19T10:00:00.000Z",
            category="habit_launch",
            subject="Launch code.exe",
            evidence_json='{"day_of_week": "Monday", "frequency_percent": 85}',
            confidence=0.85,
            status="pending",
        )
    ]

    with mock.patch.object(page.chart_canvas, "update_figure", wraps=page.chart_canvas.update_figure) as mock_chart_update:
        # First call: Initial render
        page.update_data(sessions_df, recs, freshness_text="Just now")
        assert mock_chart_update.call_count == 1
        assert page.card_active.value_label.text() == "1.0 hrs"
        assert page.card_top_app.value_label.text() == "code.exe"

        # Second call with identical data: must update freshness text but SKIP chart update
        page.update_data(sessions_df, recs, freshness_text="1 min ago")
        assert mock_chart_update.call_count == 1  # Unchanged!
        assert page.card_freshness.value_label.text() == "1 min ago"

        # Third call with new session: must re-render chart
        new_df = pd.DataFrame([
            {
                "session_id": "s_2",
                "process_name": "browser.exe",
                "pid": "200",
                "start_ts_utc": "2026-10-19T11:00:00.000Z",
                "end_ts_utc": "2026-10-19T12:00:00.000Z",
                "duration_seconds": 3600.0,
                "idle_seconds": 0.0,
                "active_seconds": 3600.0,
                "local_date": "2026-10-19",
                "day_of_week": "Monday",
                "time_bucket": "11:00",
            }
        ])
        page.update_data(new_df, recs, freshness_text="Just now")
        assert mock_chart_update.call_count == 2
        assert page.card_top_app.value_label.text() == "browser.exe"


def test_main_window_single_flight_worker(qapp, tmp_path):
    """Verify trigger_refresh enforces single-flight worker and coalesces pending requests."""
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()

    window = MainWindow(config=config, paths=paths, store=store)

    # Call trigger_refresh
    window.trigger_refresh()
    active_worker_1 = window._active_worker
    assert active_worker_1 is not None

    # Immediate second call while worker is running should set _refresh_pending and NOT replace worker
    if active_worker_1.isRunning():
        window.trigger_refresh()
        assert window._active_worker is active_worker_1
        assert window._refresh_pending is True

    # Wait for worker completion
    active_worker_1.wait(2000)
    window.close()


def test_main_window_system_tray_and_close_behavior(qapp, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Verify system tray initialization, context menu, and closeEvent hide-to-tray."""
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    paths.ensure_directories()
    store = CsvStore(paths)
    config = AppConfig()
    config.ui.show_tray_icon = True

    # Mock isSystemTrayAvailable to return True in headless environment
    monkeypatch.setattr(QSystemTrayIcon, "isSystemTrayAvailable", lambda: True)

    window = MainWindow(config=config, paths=paths, store=store)
    window.show()

    # System tray should be configured
    assert window.tray_icon is not None
    menu = window.tray_icon.contextMenu()
    assert menu is not None
    actions = [a.text() for a in menu.actions()]
    assert "Open Dashboard" in actions
    assert "Refresh Data" in actions
    assert "Exit" in actions

    # Closing window when tray is active should ignore event and hide window
    close_event = QCloseEvent()
    window.closeEvent(close_event)
    assert close_event.isAccepted() is False
    assert window.isVisible() is False

    # Explicit quit should accept event and close cleanly
    window._is_quitting = True
    close_event2 = QCloseEvent()
    window.closeEvent(close_event2)
    assert close_event2.isAccepted() is True


def test_accent_presets_and_contrast_wcag_compliance(qapp, tmp_path):
    """Verify accent presets align with golden defaults and guarantee readable contrast."""
    # 1. Golden default #635BFF must be in ACCENT_PRESETS
    assert any(code.upper() == "#635BFF" for code in ACCENT_PRESETS.values())

    # 2. Contrast function gives light text on dark backgrounds and dark text on bright backgrounds
    assert get_contrast_text_color("#0A0D16") == "#FFFFFF"
    assert get_contrast_text_color("#635BFF") == "#FFFFFF"
    assert get_contrast_text_color("#FBBF24") == "#0F172A"  # Amber glow has bright luminance
    assert get_contrast_text_color("#22D3EE") == "#0F172A"  # Cyan glow has bright luminance

    # 3. Settings page populates all presets
    paths = resolve_paths(data_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "data"), log_dir_override=str(tmp_path / "logs"))
    config = AppConfig()
    page = SettingsPage(config=config, paths=paths)

    combo_items = [page.combo_accent.itemData(i).upper() for i in range(page.combo_accent.count())]
    for preset_hex in ACCENT_PRESETS.values():
        assert preset_hex.upper() in combo_items
